### [1] [Designing Rewards for Rewarding Designs: Demonstrating the Impact of Rewards on the Creative Design Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26083)
*Surabhi S Nath,Vindula Jayawardana,Monica Van,Matt Klenk,Shabnam Hakimi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究将3D参数化椅子设计任务建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过对比目标一致与目标无关的奖励反馈，发现奖励能促进设计空间探索、提升目标一致性并保持设计多样性，同时目标性质影响用户对奖励有用性的感知，最终提出设计决策中有效反馈的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解奖励（反馈）在创意设计过程中对设计决策的影响机制，以揭示抽象目标如何通过约束下的决策转化为具体成果。

Method: 将3D参数化、基于目标的椅子设计任务建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），在每次迭代中向参与者提供目标一致或目标无关的奖励，追踪其决策轨迹并测量任务行为与自我报告体验。

Result: 奖励促使参与者更充分地探索设计空间；目标一致奖励相比目标无关奖励更能最大化目标达成度，同时保持设计多样性；目标性质影响参与者对奖励有用性的感知。

Conclusion: 基于实证发现，提出了为设计决策设计有效反馈的指导原则。

Abstract: The creative design process involves transforming abstract goals into concrete outcomes through a series of decisions made under constraints. While such processes are commonly shaped by feedback like rewards, their impact on design decision making remains unclear. To better understand the role of rewards in the design process, we modeled a 3D parametric, goal-based chair design task as a Markov Decision Process. We tracked participants' decisions as they iteratively developed designs for an abstract design goal, and presented either a goal-aligned or goal-agnostic reward at every step. We tested the effect of these rewards on task behaviour and self-reported experience. With rewards, participants more thoroughly explored the design space, and maximised goal-aligned over goal-agnostic rewards while preserving diversity across designs. The nature of the goal also mattered, influencing participants' perception of the reward's usefulness. Building on these insights, we propose guidelines for designing effective feedback for design decision making.

</details>


### [2] [Human-Augmented Reality Interaction in Rebar Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26112)
*Mahsa Sanei,Fernando Moreu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过30名受试者的实验，评估了基于Microsoft HoloLens 2的增强现实辅助钢筋间距检测系统。相比传统方法，AR辅助检测使躯干屈曲平均减少30.8%，颈部屈曲减少32.8%，任务完成时间减少67.7%，行走距离和手部路径长度均减少超过50%。NASA-TLX工作量得分整体降低45.6%，检测精度保持不变。系统可用性量表均分76.1，83%的参与者认为系统可接受。结果表明AR辅助检测在保持检测质量的同时降低了人体工程学风险和工作负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢筋混凝土结构中的钢筋检测需要工人长时间保持不适姿势，并将二维图纸在脑海中映射到三维装配体上，存在较高的人体工程学风险和认知负荷。

Method: 采用受试者内实验设计，30名参与者在传统方法和AR辅助方法（基于Microsoft HoloLens 2）下分别进行钢筋间距检测。使用100 Hz的运动捕捉系统记录全身运动学数据，同时采集任务完成时间、行走距离、手部路径长度等客观指标，以及NASA-TLX主观工作负荷和SUS系统可用性评分。

Result: AR辅助相比传统方法：躯干屈曲减少30.8%，颈部屈曲减少32.8%，任务完成时间减少67.7%，行走距离和手部路径长度均减少超50%，NASA-TLX总分降低45.6%（体力需求降幅最大），检测精度无显著差异，SUS均分76.1（83%评为可接受）。

Conclusion: AR辅助钢筋检测能够在保持检测精度的同时，显著降低人体工程学风险（减少不良姿势）和感知工作负荷，提高任务效率。

Abstract: Rebar inspection in reinforced concrete construction requires sustained awkward postures and complex mental mapping of two-dimensional drawings onto three-dimensional assemblies. This study evaluated an Augmented Reality (AR)-assisted rebar inspection system deployed on Microsoft HoloLens 2 through a within-subjects experiment with 30 participants. Full-body kinematics were recorded using a motion capture system at 100 Hz while participants performed traditional and AR-assisted spacing inspection. AR reduced mean trunk flexion by 30.8%, mean neck flexion by 32.8%, and task completion time by 67.7%. Walking distance and hand-path length each decreased by over 50%. NASA Task Load Index scores decreased by 45.6% overall, with the largest reduction in physical demand. Inspection accuracy was maintained across conditions. The System Usability Scale yielded a mean score of 76.1 with 83% of participants rating the system acceptable. These results provide convergent objective and subjective evidence that AR-assisted inspection reduces ergonomic risk and perceived workload maintaining inspection quality.

</details>


### [3] [Culturally Aware GenAI Risks for Youth: Perspectives from Youth, Parents, and Teachers in a Non-Western Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26494)
*Aljawharah Alzahrani,Tory Park,Tanusree Sharma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过非西方视角（沙特阿拉伯），采用混合方法（分析1998条社交媒体帖子+31人访谈），探讨了7-17岁儿童、家长和教师在使用生成式AI时的隐私与安全风险。研究发现：青少年向GenAI透露个人信息的行为与集体主义文化中的谦虚、荣誉等规范冲突；社会经济因素（如共用账号）加剧了风险。研究为符合文化规范的家长控制工具提供了设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦西方语境下青少年使用GenAI的安全问题，忽略了文化、宗教、社会维度对非西方地区（如沙特阿拉伯）青少年数字体验的深刻影响。

Method: 混合方法：分析了736条Reddit和1262条X（Twitter）帖子，并对31名沙特阿拉伯参与者（8名青少年、13名家长、10名教师）进行了访谈。

Result: 1) GenAI的隐私与安全具有情境依赖性和关系性，受集体结构和规范塑造；2) 青少年向GenAI透露个人信息（尤其是家庭信息）的风险与谦虚、隐私、荣誉等文化期望冲突，尤其在寻求情感支持时；3) 社会经济因素（如共用ChatGPT账号以节省成本）加剧了风险。

Conclusion: 需要设计符合文化规范和价值观的、情境敏感的家长控制工具，为包容性的非西方语境GenAI安全研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are widely used by youth and have introduced new privacy and safety challenges. While prior research has explored youth's safety in GenAI within western context, it often overlooks the cultural, religious, and social dimensions of technology use that strongly shape youths digital experiences in countries like Saudi Arabia. To address the gap, this study explores children (aged 7 to 17), parents and teachers interactions with GenAI tools and risk perceptions through non-western lens. Through a mixed methods approach, we analyzed 736 Reddit and 1,262 X(Twitter) posts and conducted interviews with 31 Saudi Arabian participants (8 youth, 13 parents, 10 teachers). Our findings highlight context dependent and relational privacy and safety of GenAI from non-western context which often formed by communal structure and prescribed norms. We found significant risks tied to youths disclosure of personal and family information, which conflict with culturally rooted expectations of modesty, privacy, and honor, particularly when youth seek emotional support from GenAI. These risks further compounded by socio economic factors such as cost-saving practices leading to the use of shared GenAI accounts (e.g.ChatGPT) within families or even among strangers. We provide design implication reflecting on parents and teachers expectation of how youth should use GenAI. This work lays groundwork for inclusive, context sensitive parental controls that adhere to cultural norms and values.

</details>


### [4] [Ceci n'est pas une explication: Evaluating Explanation Failures as Explainability Pitfalls in Language Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26145)
*Ben Knight,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,James Edgell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了L2-Bench基准来评估AI语言学习工具中的反馈质量，识别了六种关键维度，并分析了AI系统在这些维度上的失败所导致的"可解释性陷阱"，这些陷阱表面上看似有用但实际上有缺陷，可能导致学习成效、人机交互和社会情感方面的危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI语言学习工具提供的即时个性化反馈可能存在难以被学习者和教师察觉的缺陷，长期使用会强化误解并侵蚀学习效果，因此需要系统性地评估AI反馈质量和识别潜在失败模式。

Method: 提出了L2-Bench基准的一部分，包含六个关键维度（诊断准确性、恰当性意识、错误原因、优先级排序、改进指导、自我调节支持），分析AI系统在这些维度上的失败模式，并将其归类为"可解释性陷阱"。

Result: 识别出AI系统在语言学习反馈中存在的可解释性陷阱，这些陷阱表面上看似有帮助但本质上有缺陷，会导致三类危害：学习成效危害、人机交互危害和社会情感危害。

Conclusion: 语言学习的特定语境放大了上述风险，需要更多关注设计评估框架时的开放问题，以促进AI开发者设计更安全、可信和有效的AI解释。

Abstract: AI-powered language learning tools increasingly provide instant, personalised feedback to millions of learners worldwide. However, this feedback can fail in ways that are difficult for learners--and even teachers--to detect, potentially reinforcing misconceptions and eroding learning outcomes over extended use. We present a portion of L2-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating AI systems in language education that includes (but is not limited to) six critical dimensions of effective feedback: diagnostic accuracy, awareness of appropriacy, causes of error, prioritisation, guidance for improvement, and supporting self-regulation. We analyse how AI systems can fail with respect to these dimensions. These failures, which we argue are conducive to "explainability pitfalls," are AI-generated explanations that appear helpful on the surface but are fundamentally flawed, increasing the risk of attainment, human-AI interaction, and socioaffective harms. We discuss how the specific context of language learning amplifies these risks and outline open questions we believe merit more attention when designing evaluation frameworks specifically. Our analysis aims to expand the community's understanding of both the typology of explainability pitfalls and the contextual dynamics in which they may occur in order to encourage AI developers to better design safe, trustworthy, and effective AI explanations.

</details>


### [5] [Persona-Based Process Design for Assistive Human-Robot Workplaces for Persons with Disabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26527)
*Nils Mandischer,Daria Eckert and,Lars Mikelsons*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角色（Persona）的通用设计方法，用于简化残障人士与机器人协作工作流程的设计。通过将典型残障抽象为角色，结合设计思维制定动作策略，并基于行为树实现自适应行为，在纸箱折叠任务中针对七种角色验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协作系统多为个性化设计，难以推广至更广泛的残障用户群体；而通用设计范式虽理想，但缺乏专业知识支持，导致流程设计困难。

Method: 1) 将典型残障抽象为角色（Personas）；2) 将工作流程拆分为顺序动作；3) 针对每个动作和角色，利用设计思维制定策略；4) 按机器人辅助程度排序策略，并实现为行为树，使系统可在线自适应不同角色。

Result: 在协作纸箱折叠任务中，针对七种残障角色验证，该方法生成了更全面的流程策略，并实现了适应性的通用设计行为。

Conclusion: 基于角色的流程设计方法能够生成更全面的策略，并在通用设计框架下实现自适应行为，有望推动残障人士在机器人工作场所中的广泛融入。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction is emerging as an important paradigm for integrating persons with disabilities into the workplace. While these systems can enable individuals to work, their design is mostly personalized, hindering widespread use beyond the individual user. The universal design paradigm is a central pillar of inclusive design, describing usability of systems by all. To incorporate universal design into process design for human-robot workplaces expert knowledge is required that is often not available. To simplify process design of human-robot workplaces, we propose a persona-based design approach. First, typical impairments prevalent in the workforce or particularly relevant for the processes are abstracted into personas with disabilities. The work process is subdivided into sequential actions. For each action and persona, strategies are developed to reach the action goal by a design thinking approach. The resulting actions are ordered by level of robot assistance, i.e. robot involvement, and implemented in a behavior tree. Therefore, the macro-behavior of the workplace may adapt to individual personas online. We demonstrate the method in a collaborative box folding process with a total of seven personas with disabilities. The persona-based process design shows promising results by generating more comprehensive process strategies while enabling adaptive behavior in the sense of universal design.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Screenshots: Evaluating VLMs' Understanding of UI Animations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26148)
*Chen Liang,Xirui Jiang,Naihao Deng,Eytan Adar,Anhong Guo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究创建了AniMINT数据集（300个密集标注的UI动画视频），评估了视觉语言模型在理解UI动画方面的能力。结果显示，VLM能可靠检测基础运动，但在高级动画解释方面与人类表现存在显著差距。通过运动、上下文和感知线索（MCPC）分析揭示了关键瓶颈和改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM在UI理解研究中主要关注静态截图，忽略了作为UI核心交流方式的动画模态。鉴于动画在UI中承担重要的功能作用（而非仅仅美学），理解UI动画对于全面解释界面至关重要，但目前尚不清楚VLM处理动态UI动画的能力。

Method: 创建AniMINT数据集（300个密集标注的UI动画视频），系统评估最先进VLM在感知动画效果、识别动画目的和解释动画含义三个层面的UI动画理解能力，并采用MCPC（运动、上下文和感知线索）框架分析影响VLM性能的关键因素。

Result: VLM能够可靠检测原始运动，但在高级动画解释（识别目的和含义）方面表现不稳定，与人类表现存在显著差距。通过MCPC分析揭示了影响性能的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在UI动画的高级语义理解上远未达到人类水平，MCPC分析指出了未来改进的关键方向。

Abstract: AI agents operating on user interfaces must understand how interfaces communicate state and feedback to act reliably. As a core communicative modality, animations are increasingly used in modern interfaces, serving critical functional purposes beyond mere aesthetics. Thus, understanding UI animation is essential for comprehensive interface interpretation. However, recent studies of Vision Language Models (VLMs) for UI understanding have focused primarily on static screenshots, leaving it unclear how well these models handle dynamic UI animations. To address this gap, we created AniMINT, a novel dataset of 300 densely annotated UI animation videos. We systematically evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on UI animation understanding, including their abilities to perceive the animation effects, identify animation purposes, and interpret animation meaning. Our results show that VLMs can reliably detect primitive motion. However, their high-level animation interpretation remains inconsistent, with substantial gaps relative to human performance. Finally, we use Motion, Context, and Perceptual Cues (MCPC) to probe factors affecting VLM performance, revealing key bottlenecks and directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [7] [Towards Low-Cost Low-Power Activity-Aware Soil Moisture Sensing Platform for Large-scale Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26303)
*Jack Thoene,Omar Kamil,Thekra Alkadee,Nivedita Arora*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无电池传感器节点和移动基站的端到端平台，利用农民日常作业实现土壤湿度持续监测，成本低于35美元，通信距离达1公里。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤湿度对作物产量预测和精准灌溉至关重要，但现有IoT基础设施成本高昂，阻碍了现代农业大规模部署实时土壤湿度传感器。

Method: 设计埋地无电池传感器节点（自供电原电池土壤湿度探头、高阻抗模拟前端），利用太阳能供电（单次充电可持续21天）；移动基站附着于农用车辆，通过有限状态机握手协议实现数据交换；整合传感器、链路质量及位置数据至可视化仪表板。

Result: 在2 dBm发射功率下实现超过1公里的可靠通信；室内稳定运行超过70天；多次间歇连接期间实现连续数据恢复。

Conclusion: 该平台低成本（<35美元）、高精度、易扩展，在不干扰农事操作的前提下，实现了偏远地块的持续性、规律性数据采集。

Abstract: Deep understanding of a field's soil moisture content is the leading indicator for predicting crop yields and making data driven decisions for irrigation and application of topical chemicals for drought resilience. Despite this importance, the cost of adopting and maintaining IoT infrastructure prevents modern farms from employing widespread real time soil moisture sensors. We present an end-to-end platform of buried battery-free sensor nodes and a mobile basestation that leverages the farmer's daily routine for data retrieval. Each node features a self-powered galvanic soil-moisture probe, employing a high impedance analog front end to enable durability. Operating entirely on harvested solar energy for up to 21 days on a single capacitor charge, each node collects soil moisture, temperature, and environment condition data. Using a predictable finite-state machine, handshake-based data exchanges occur with a basestation affixed to standard farming vehicles designed to listen for the nodes while moving through the farm. Our platform organizes all sensor, link-quality, and location data into an easy-to-interpret dashboard to seamlessly integrate with the farmer's everyday routine. Costing less than $35, the platform is a financially accessible, accurate, and easily scalable platform that enables persistent, regular data collection from the most rural plots without adding to or impeding farming operations. Experimental evaluation demonstrates reliable communication over 1 km at 2 dBm transmit power, stable sensor readings over 70 days of indoor operation, and continuous data recovery during multiple periods of intermittent connection.

</details>


### [8] [Towards a Frugal Photosynthesis Sensing Toolkit for Data-Driven Plant Science Education and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26305)
*Qitong Li,Raj Nileshbhai Dave,Rhema Amanda Phiri,Leo Zhang,Xiaoyu Zheng,Ariana Blake,Livia Ford,Sarah Jones,Susan R. Strickler,Nivedita Arora*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PhytoBits是一个基于低成本传感器和开源硬件的 frugal 气体交换检测工具包，可用于区分和教学C3与CAM光合作用策略，支持多天原位监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光合作用研究工具成本高、难以获取，且大多只能检测光合作用的存在与否，无法捕捉其时间动态变化，难以满足环境变化背景下对光合策略教学与研究的需求。

Method: PhytoBits结合叶片密闭室、现成CO₂传感器和低成本的微控制器，构建了一个原位气体交换传感工具包，实现对植物气体交换的多天连续监测。

Result: 通过与研究级气体交换系统对比验证，PhytoBits能够可靠区分C3和CAM光合途径，并能解析兼性CAM和发育性CAM动态。

Conclusion: PhytoBits提供了一种低成本、可获取的光合策略教学与研究工具，但当前仅为早期硬件验证阶段，未来需进行用户部署研究、开源代码发布和自动化路径分类。

Abstract: Rapid environmental change and advances in data-driven analysis highlight the need not only to use computational tools, but also to foster understanding of the natural world and inspire creativity. Photosynthesis, the process that fuels nearly all life on Earth, provides a compelling context for such learning, particularly in understanding how plants alter their photosynthetic strategies in response to environmental changes. However, existing tools for studying photosynthesis are often inaccessible or limited to demonstrating its presence, rather than capturing its temporal dynamics. We present PhytoBits, a frugal in situ gas-exchange sensing toolkit for distinguishing and teaching photosynthetic strategies. PhytoBits combines leaf enclosure with accessible materials, an off-the-shelf CO\textsubscript{2} sensor, and a low-cost microcontroller, to support multi-day monitoring of plant gas-exchange in educational and research contexts. We validated PhytoBits against research-grade gas-exchange systems, confirming that it identifies C\textsubscript{3} and CAM (Crassulacean Acid Metabolism) photosynthetic pathways. In addition to obligate CAM, PhytoBits also resolves facultative CAM and developmental CAM dynamics in plants. This work presents an early-stage hardware validation; user deployment studies, open-source code dissemination, and automated pathway classification are planned as future work.

</details>


### [9] [UIGaze: How Closely Can VLMs Approximate Human Visual Attention on User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26352)
*Min Song,Yoonseong Lee,Yeonhu Seo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探究视觉语言模型（VLMs）在用户界面（UI）上预测人类注视点的能力。利用UEyes数据集（1,980张UI截图，62名参与者的眼动数据），通过零样本坐标预测管线评估了9个SOTA VLM，发现VLMs与人类注视模式存在中等程度对齐，且对齐程度随UI类型和观看时长显著变化，表明VLMs更擅长捕捉探索性注视模式而非初始注视点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在理解视觉内容方面表现出色，但其预测用户在UI界面上的注视点（即人类视觉注意力）的能力尚未被探索。

Method: 使用UEyes数据集（1,980张UI截图，4种类别，62名参与者眼动数据），采用零样本坐标预测管线：VLM生成注视点坐标，经高斯模糊转换为显著性图，与真实眼动数据计算CC、SIM、KL散度进行对比。实验规模：1,980张图片 × 9个模型 × 3次运行 × 3种时长。

Result: VLMs与人类注视模式呈现中等程度对齐；对齐程度在不同UI类型间存在显著差异；较长的观看时长下对齐效果更好，表明VLMs捕捉的是探索性注视模式而非初始注视点。

Conclusion: VLMs能够以中等程度近似人类在UI上的视觉注意力，但其预测更符合探索性注视行为，为UI视觉注意力建模提供了新的基线和方法。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding visual content, yet their ability to predict where humans look on user interfaces remains unexplored. We present UIGaze, a study investigating how closely VLMs can approximate human visual attention on user interfaces using real eye-tracking data. Using the UEyes dataset - comprising 1,980 UI screenshots across four categories (webpage, desktop, mobile, poster) with eye-tracking data from 62 participants - we evaluate nine state-of-the-art VLMs through a zero-shot coordinate prediction pipeline. Each model generates gaze point coordinates that are converted into saliency maps via Gaussian blurring and compared against ground truth using CC, SIM, and KL divergence. Our experiments (1,980 images x 9 models x 3 runs x 3 durations) reveal that VLMs achieve moderate alignment with human gaze patterns, with the degree of alignment varying significantly across UI types and improving with longer viewing durations - suggesting VLMs capture exploratory gaze patterns rather than initial fixations. All code, predictions, and evaluation results are publicly available.

</details>


### [10] [MultEval: Supporting Collaborative Alignment for LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26679)
*Charles Chiang,Simret Gebreegziabher,Annalisa Szymanski,Yukun Yang,Hyo Jin Do,Zahra Ashktorab,Werner Geyer,Toby Li,Diego Gomez-Zara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM-as-a-judge评估标准制定中的协作问题，指出现有方法由单一个体定义标准存在局限性。通过形成性研究揭示了多方利益相关者在制定评估标准时面临的共识建立、价值对齐和标准可解释性等挑战。为此开发了MultEval系统，支持多方协作制定标准，并通过案例研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-judge评估标准通常由单一个体定义，嵌入了该人的假设和偏好，但实际中评估标准的制定是一个涉及多方利益相关者、充满协作与争议的过程，现有工具缺乏对此过程的支持。

Method: 通过形成性研究（formative study）考察利益相关者如何协作创建、协商和完善评估标准；基于研究发现开发MultEval系统，该系统利用共识建立理论支持多方协作，实现标准迭代修订和评估透明化；并通过领域专家团队的案例研究验证系统效果。

Result: 揭示了人类监督中的三大挑战：建立共享理解的困难、不同专业背景和优先级利益相关者之间的价值对齐、将细微的人类判断转化为LLM可解释和可执行的标准。MultEval系统通过共识建立机制、附带示例的标准修订和评估编码透明化，有效支持了多方协作制定评估标准的过程。

Conclusion: 多方协作和共识形成过程对评估标准的演化至关重要，MultEval系统为此提供了有效的工具支持。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge approaches have emerged as a scalable solution for evaluating model behaviors, yet they rely on evaluation criteria often created by a single individual, embedding that person's assumptions, priorities, and interpretive lens. In practice, defining such criteria is a collaborative and contested process involving multiple stakeholders with different values, interpretations, and priorities; an aspect largely unsupported by existing tools. To examine this problem in depth, we present a formative study examining how stakeholders collaboratively create, negotiate, and refine evaluation criteria for LLM-as-a-judge systems. Our findings reveal challenges in human oversight, including difficulties in establishing shared understanding, aligning values across stakeholders with different expertise and priorities, and translating nuanced human judgments into criteria that are interpretable and actionable for LLM judges. Based on these insights, we developed MultEval, a system that supports collaborative criteria by enabling multiple evaluators to surface and diagnose disagreements using consensus-building theory, iteratively revise criteria with attached examples and proposal history, and maintain transparency over how judgments are encoded into an automated evaluator. We further report a case study in which a team of domain experts used MultEval to collaboratively author criteria, illustrating how coordination and collaborative consensus-making shape criteria evolution.

</details>


### [11] [Transferability of Token Usage Rights: A Design Space Analysis of Generative AI Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26683)
*Jaeyong Lee,Heeju Kang,Ahra Cho,Baek Eunkyung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI服务中Token使用权的可转移性设计，通过分析四大LLM服务的计费政策，识别出五种可转移性类型，将Token从经济-技术基元重新定义为以用户为中心的系统设计核心要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Token使用权具有固定性（受限于平台、账户、时间），但这种固定性源于服务商的计费政策而非技术必要性，限制了用户的选择权和自主性。研究者希望探索如何通过设计Token使用权的可转移性来改善用户体验。

Method: 基于MacLean等人(1991)的设计空间分析框架，分析ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、Grok四大LLM服务的计费政策和服务条款，识别出五个设计轴（目标、方向、单位、控制、可逆性）和五种具体可转移性类型。

Result: 识别出Token使用权的五种可转移性类型：结转(carry-over)、共同管理(co-management)、转移(transfer)、转换(conversion)和交易(trade)，并建立了五维设计轴分析框架。

Conclusion: Token应被重新定义为以用户为中心的系统设计核心要素，其使用权的可转移性设计能够扩展用户的选择权和自主性，而非仅作为经济-技术领域的基础单位。

Abstract: With the rapid spread of generative AI services, the token has gained value not only as a technical unit of language processing but also as an economic currency for accessing AI services. Major AI model providers have adopted token-based billing as their default service model, requiring users to purchase platform-bound, fixed token usage rights. However, the fixedness of these usage rights is grounded in the billing-policy decisions of service providers rather than in any technical necessity. This study defines the Transferability of token usage rights as a design property that allows users to flexibly reallocate purchased data resources free from the constraints of time, account, and service. Drawing on the Design Space Analysis framework of MacLean et al. (1991), we identify five design axes (Target, Direction, Unit, Control, Reversibility) and five concrete Transferability types (carry-over, co-management, transfer, conversion, and trade) by analyzing the billing policies and terms of service of four major LLM services (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Grok). Our analysis reframes the token from a purely economic-technical primitive into a core element of user-centered system design that expands user choice and autonomy.

</details>

### [12] [Pseudo-Complex Quantifier Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26400)
*Nicolas Faroß,Thomas Sturm*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对复数域的消去量词框架，使用带有序环语言并补充了虚数单位、实部、虚部和共轭的符号。技术上通过规约到实数消去量词，然后对结果进行启发式重解释。在开源系统Logic1中实现了原型并给出了计算示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复数域的量词消去处理较为复杂，缺乏在带序环语言下直接处理复数运算（虚数单位、实部、虚部、共轭）的高效框架。

Method: 将复数域的量词消去问题规约到实数域的量词消去，再利用启发式方法将实数量词消去结果重解释回复数框架中。

Result: 在基于Python的开源系统Logic1中实现了原型，并展示了计算示例。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效处理复数域带有序环语言及复数相关符号的量词消去问题。

Abstract: We describe the design of a quantifier elimination framework for the complex numbers in the language of ordered rings supplemented with symbols for the imaginary unit, real parts, imaginary parts, and conjugates. Technically, we use a reduction to real quantifier elimination followed by a heuristic reinterpretation of the results within our complex framework. We present computational examples using a prototypical implementation of our approach in our Python-based open-source system Logic1.

</details>

### [13] [Mining Negative Sequential Patterns to Improve Viral Genomic Feature Representation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25968)
*Wenxi Zhu,Wensheng Gan,Zhenlian Qi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出GeneNSPCla框架，基于负序列模式（NSPs）从RNA病毒基因组核苷酸序列中提取判别性缺失特征，结合改进的GONPM+算法挖掘更长的生物学意义的负模式，在8个分类器上平均准确率较原负模式算法提升10.03%，较正模式算法提升24.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组成或频率的病毒基因组分类模型可解释性有限，在复杂或不平衡数据集上准确率下降，亟需一种能同时捕获病毒序列中存在与缺失信号的新方法。

Method: 提出GeneNSPCla框架：1）设计GONPM+算法用于基因组负序列模式挖掘，发现更长且具生物学意义的负模式；2）将NSPs转化为数值特征向量；3）集成多种监督分类器进行分类。

Result: GONPM+在8个分类器上的平均准确率相比原负模式算法提升10.03%，相比正模式算法提升24.75%。

Conclusion: 纳入缺失性序列信息能有效提升病毒基因组分类性能，为病毒基因组分析和分类提供了新的互补视角。

Abstract: Viruses represent the most abundant biological entities on Earth and play a pivotal role in microbial ecosystems, yet, as prominent human pathogens, they are closely linked to human morbidity and mortality. Accurate identification of viral sequences from viral genome sequences is therefore essential, but existing genome-based classification models that largely relying on composition- or frequency-based subsequence features often suffer from limited interpretability and reduced accuracy, particularly on complex or imbalanced datasets. To address these limitations, we propose GeneNSPCla (Genomic Negative Sequential Pattern-based Classification), a novel viral classification framework based on Negative Sequential Patterns (NSPs) that extracts discriminative absence-based features from nucleotide sequences of RNA viral genomes. By transforming these NSPs into numerical feature vectors and integrating them into multiple supervised classifiers, GeneNSPCla effectively captures both presence and absence signals in viral sequences. Furthermore, we propose a negative pattern mining algorithm adapted for processing genomic data: GONPM+, which can discover longer and more biologically meaningful negative sequential patterns. The experimental results demonstrate that the average accuracy of GONPM+ in 8 classifiers has improved by 10.03% compared to the original negative pattern mining algorithm and by 24.75% compared to the positive pattern mining algorithm. These findings highlight the effectiveness of incorporating absence-based sequential information, providing a new and complementary perspective for viral genome analysis and classification.

</details>


### [14] [CacheRAG: A Semantic Caching System for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26176)
*Yushi Sun,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CacheRAG通过缓存增强架构将LLM驱动的KGQA系统从无状态规划器转变为持续学习器，引入模式无关用户界面、多样性优化缓存检索和有界启发式扩展三大创新设计，在多个基准测试上显著优于现有方法（如CRAG数据集上准确率+13.2%，真实性+17.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的KGQA系统作为无状态规划器运行，孤立地生成检索计划而不利用历史查询模式，类似于没有计划缓存的数据库系统，导致模式幻觉和检索覆盖范围有限。

Method: 提出CacheRAG架构，包含三个核心设计：(1) 模式无关用户界面：通过中间语义表示(ISR)的两阶段语义解析框架，支持自然语言交互，后端适配器将LLM与本地模式上下文结合以编译可执行物理查询；(2) 多样性优化缓存检索：双层层次索引（领域→方面）结合最大边际相关性(MMR)最大化缓存示例的结构多样性；(3) 有界启发式扩展：确定性深度和广度子图算子，具有严格复杂度保证，提高检索召回率而不导致无界API执行。

Result: 在多个基准测试上显著超越现有最先进基线，如CRAG数据集上准确率提升13.2%，真实性提升17.5%。

Conclusion: CacheRAG通过缓存增强架构有效解决了LLM-driven KGQA系统中的模式幻觉和检索覆盖不足问题，将无状态规划器转变为持续学习器，大幅提升了系统性能。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has significantly advanced Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA). However, existing LLM-driven KGQA systems act as stateless planners, generating retrieval plans in isolation without exploiting historical query patterns: analogous to a database system that optimizes every query from scratch without a plan cache. This fundamental design flaw leads to schema hallucinations and limited retrieval coverage. We propose CacheRAG, a systematic cache-augmented architecture for LLM-based KGQA that transforms stateless planners into continual learners. Unlike traditional database plan caching (which optimizes for frequency), CacheRAG introduces three novel design principles tailored for LLM contexts: (1) Schema-agnostic user interface: A two-stage semantic parsing framework via Intermediate Semantic Representation (ISR) enables non-expert users to interact purely in natural language, while a Backend Adapter grounds the LLM with local schema context to compile executable physical queries safely. (2) Diversity-optimized cache retrieval: A two-layer hierarchical index (Domain $\rightarrow$ Aspect) coupled with Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) maximizes structural variety in cached examples, effectively mitigating reasoning homogeneity. (3) Bounded heuristic expansion: Deterministic depth and breadth subgraph operators with strict complexity guarantees significantly enhance retrieval recall without risking unbounded API execution. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that CacheRAG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines (e.g., +13.2% accuracy and +17.5% truthfulness on the CRAG dataset).

</details>


### [15] [PiLLar: Matching for Pivot Table Schema via LLM-guided Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26356)
*Yunjun Gao,Chuangyu Ouyang,Congcong Ge,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出PiLLar，首个针对数据透视表（pivot table）的模式匹配框架，采用LLM驱动的搜索范式，在最小化隐私合规数据下实现免训练跨域适配，并构建了PTbench基准，在真实数据上达到87.94%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中透视表广泛存在，其模式匹配是数据集成的前提。但透视表模式匹配是一项新颖的联合模式-值匹配任务，且实际中匿名化数据的普遍性严重影响了匹配精度和泛化能力。

Method: 提出PiLLar框架：1）将任务形式化为LLM驱动的搜索范式，仅需极少量带注释的隐私合规数据，实现跨域免训练适配；2）提供理论误差动力学分析，保证方法的渐近收敛性；3）构建PTbench基准，通过挖掘适合逆透视的表、对语义连贯属性执行逆透视、采样和匿名化生成。

Result: 在四个代表性真实域上，PiLLar在正确预测的匹配上达到87.94%的平均准确率，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PiLLar有效解决了匿名化数据下透视表模式匹配的准确性与泛化挑战，为数据湖中的数据集成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pivot tables are ubiquitous in data lakes of modern data ecosystems, making accurate schema matching over pivot tables a key prerequisite for data integration. In this paper, we focus on matching for pivot table schema, which is a novel joint schema-value matching task. It aims to align schemas between pivot tables and standard relational tables, where a correct match must be semantically consistent at the schema level and compatible at the value level. However, due to the inherent data sensitivity of this task, the prevalence of anonymized data in practice poses significant challenges to its matching accuracy and generalization capability. To tackle these challenges, we propose PiLLar, the first matching for pivot table schema framework. We first formulate PiLLar as an LLM-driven search paradigm that operates with minimal annotated privacy-compliant data, thereby achieving training-free adaptation across diverse domains. Next, we provide a theoretical analysis on the error dynamics of the paradigm to ensure the asymptotic convergence of the proposed method. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark PTbench, derived from four representative real-world domains and constructed by mining unpivot-suitable tables, performing unpivot on semantically coherent attributes, and applying sampling and anonymization. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of PiLLar, which achieves an average accuracy of 87.94% on the correctly predicted matches.

</details>

### [16] [I Would If I Could: Reasoning about Dynamics of Actions in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26053)
*Rustam Galimullin,Hermine Grosinger,Munyque Mittelmann*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了带有动态动作的ATL（ATL-D）及其认知扩展ATEL-D，用于建模MAS中动作的授予与撤销及其对智能体知识的影响，并提供了表达性、与规范系统的关系及计算复杂性等理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准策略逻辑（如ATL）虽能建模智能体基于状态或历史的策略行为，但缺乏对智能体可用动作的动态变更（授予/撤销）及其对智能体知识影响的处理。为弥补这一空白，本文提出ATL-D和ATEL-D。

Method: 在ATL基础上引入动态动作机制，定义动作授予与撤销的时态逻辑运算符，形成ATL-D；进一步结合认知逻辑扩展为ATEL-D。通过形式语义定义、表达性比较（与标准ATL的互模拟关系）、规范系统关联分析以及计算问题复杂度分析来验证逻辑框架。

Result: (1) ATL-D能表达标准ATL无法刻画的动态动作变更场景；(2) 建立了与规范系统（如授权与义务）的形式化关联；(3) 给出了模型检测等关键计算问题的复杂度界（如PSPACE-complete等）。

Conclusion: ATL-D/ATEL-D为MAS中动作动态变化及其认知影响提供了统一的形式化框架，丰富了策略逻辑的表达能力，并为后续在动态环境下的智能体推理与验证奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Autonomous agents acting in realistic Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) should be able to adapt during their execution. Standard strategic logics, such as Alternating-time Temporal Logic (ATL), model agents' state- or history-dependent behaviour. However, the dynamic treatment of agents' available actions and their knowledge of required actions is still rarely addressed. In this paper, we introduce ATL with Dynamic Actions (ATL-D), which models the process of granting and revoking actions, and its extension ATEL-D, which captures how such updates affect agents' knowledge. Beyond the conceptual contribution, we provide several technical results: we analyse the expressivity of our logic in relation to ATL, study its relation to normative systems, and provide complexity results for relevant computational problems.

</details>


### [17] [Automaton-based Characterisations of First Order Logic over Infinite Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26364)
*Massimo Benerecetti,Dario Della Monica,Angelo Matteo,Fabio Mogavero,Gabriele Puppis*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶逻辑在无限树上的表达能力，引入两类犹豫树自动机，证明它们分别等价于两个分支时间时序逻辑 PolPCTL 和 CTLsf，进而揭示一阶逻辑在无限树上沿每条分支只能表达安全性或共安全性属性这一根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限树（无序）上刻画一阶逻辑的表达能力，建立其与分支时间规范形式之间的鲁棒对应关系。线性时间情形已有成熟结果，但分支时间情形存在结构性挑战，需要新的自动化特征刻画。

Method: 引入两类犹豫树自动机（hesitant tree automata），证明它们精确捕获两个分支时间时序逻辑 PolPCTL 和 CTLsf 的表达能力，这两个逻辑此前已被证明等价于无限树上的一阶逻辑。进而利用这些自动机特征导出 CTL 的一个新片段 PolCTLs，作为一阶逻辑的自然范式。

Result: 建立了犹豫树自动机与 PolPCTL、CTLsf 之间的精确表达力等价关系；获得了无限树上一阶逻辑的自动化特征和正规形式；发现一阶逻辑在无限树上沿每条分支只能表达安全（safety）或共安全（co-safety）性质。

Conclusion: 揭示了无限树上一阶逻辑表达能力的根本性局限：沿每条分支仅限于安全性或共安全性属性，这为分支时间下的一阶可定义性划定了清晰的表达边界。

Abstract: We study the expressive power of First-Order Logic (\FO) over (unordered) infinite trees, with the aim of identifying robust characterisations in terms of branching-time specification formalisms. While such correspondences are well understood in the linear-time setting, the branching-time case presents well-known structural challenges. To this end, we introduce two classes of hesitant tree automata and show that they capture precisely the expressive power of two branching-time temporal logics, namely \PolPCTL and \CTLsf, both of which have been previously shown to be equivalent to \FO over infinite trees. These results provide uniform automata-theoretic characterisations and yield a natural normal form for the latter in terms of a new fragment of \CTLs called \PolCTLs. As a consequence, we identify a fundamental limitation of \FO in this setting: along each branch, it can express only properties that are either safety or co-safety, thereby revealing a sharp expressive boundary for first-order definability over infinite trees.

</details>


### [18] [Templates in Rewriting Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26474)
*Kasper Hagens,Cynthia Kop*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模板的引理生成方法，集成到有界重写归纳（Bounded RI）中，用于高阶逻辑约束项重写系统（LCSTRSs）的程序等价性证明，能够处理以往方法无法证明的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重写归纳（RI）是项重写中用于建立程序等价性的形式化系统，但自动生成归纳假设（引理）面临挑战。现有引理生成技术主要针对纯项重写，很少考虑计算或约束带来的复杂性。因此需要一种能够处理高阶逻辑约束的新方法。

Method: 利用高阶重写归纳的最新进展，提出基于模板（templates）的方法。该方法通过将典型编程结构识别为高阶函数的实例来生成引理，并将其作为补充启发式集成到有界重写归纳（Bounded RI）中。

Result: 该方法能够证明以往无法证明的程序等价关系，扩展了有界重写归纳的证明能力。

Conclusion: 基于模板的引理生成与有界重写归纳的结合是一种有效的互补策略，能够处理更复杂的程序等价性证明问题。

Abstract: Rewriting Induction (RI) is a formal system in term rewriting to establish program equivalence. The recently defined Bounded RI for higher-order Logically Constrained Term Rewriting Systems (LCSTRSs) yields a convenient proof system for analyzing real programming code. A practical challenge in RI is the automatic generation of induction hypotheses, called lemmas. While various lemma generation techniques exist for plain term rewriting, there are much fewer that consider the intricacies brought on by calculations or constraints. Taking advantage of recent developments in higher-order RI, we here present a new approach based on templates, which operates by recognising typical programming constructs as instances of higher-order functions. While templates have been used as a stand-alone method to justify the correctness of program transformations, we here consider their integration in Bounded RI to obtain a complementary lemma generation heuristic. This allows us to prove equivalences that were previously out of reach.

</details>


### [19] [On-the-fly LTLf Synthesis under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26688)
*Nadav Alon,Supratik Chakraborty,Alexandre Duret-Lutz,Dror Fried,Lucas M. Tabajara,Moshe Y. Vardi,Shufang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测下的LTLf合成问题，提出一种基于可观测推进的即时信念状态DFA构建方法，将信念状态构建与游戏求解过程融合，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分可观测LTLf合成方法在游戏求解前预先进行子集构造生成信念状态DFA，会产生许多实际不需要的信念状态，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出基于可观测推进的即时方法：仅对可观测变量推进规约，对不可观测变量进行即时全称量化，逐步构建信念状态DFA；利用多终端二元决策图（MTBDD）紧凑表示DFA；将信念状态构建与游戏求解完全融合。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法显著优于现有方法，并突出了将即时游戏求解与信念状态构建相结合的实际收益。

Conclusion: 该方法被证明是正确的，实现了完全即时化的合成框架，通过MTBDD表示获得了高效实现。

Abstract: LTLf synthesis under partial observability requires reasoning about unobservable environment variables, which is typically handled by constructing a belief-state DFA via subset construction that universally quantifies these variables. Existing approaches perform this construction as a separate step prior to game solving, often generating belief states that are unnecessary in practice. We propose an on-the-fly approach to LTLf synthesis under partial observability based on observable progression. Our method incrementally builds the belief-state DFA by progressing the specification with respect to observable variables only, universally quantifying unobservable variables on the fly. We prove the correctness of the construction and show that it naturally enables on-the-fly game solving, leading to a fully on-the-fly synthesis framework. Our implementation leverages DFAs represented using Multi-Terminal Binary Decision Diagrams: a compact representation that has proven highly effective for LTLf synthesis under full observability. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing methods and further highlight the practical benefits of integrating on-the-fly game solving with belief-state construction.

</details>


### [20] [On the Complexity of Robust Markov Decision Processes and Bisimulation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26748)
*Marnix Suilen,Guillermo A. Pérez*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有多面体不确定性集的鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）的阈值问题复杂度。主要贡献包括：(1) 对于(s,a)-矩形RMDP，证明了鲁棒策略评估属于P，阈值问题属于NP；(2) 对于s-矩形RMDP，证明了阈值问题属于PSPACE；(3) 通过从 parity games 和双模拟度量归约，建立了下界，表明阈值问题的多项式时间算法等价于解决长期未解决的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中在近似最优解或局限于特定子类（如区间MDP或L∞-RMDP），缺乏对一般多面体不确定性集RMDP的复杂度分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: (1) 利用鲁棒线性规划证明(s,a)-矩形RMDP的策略评估在P中；(2) 利用一阶实数理论证明s-矩形RMDP阈值问题在PSPACE中；(3) 通过parity games和双模拟度量归约建立下界。

Result: (s,a)-矩形RMDP的阈值问题属于NP，s-矩形RMDP的阈值问题属于PSPACE。下界结果表明，该阈值问题与parity games的多项式时间可解性等价。

Conclusion: 本文建立了RMDP阈值问题的复杂度上下界，揭示了其与经典理论计算机科学问题的深层联系。双模拟度量的归约还带来了实际收益：可用鲁棒策略迭代替代标准不动点迭代，实验验证了其效率优势。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) extend standard Markov decision processes (MDPs) to account for uncertainty in the transition probabilities. RMDPs have an uncertainty set that defines a set of possible transition functions, each of which induces a standard MDP. The natural objective in an RMDP is to optimize the discounted cumulative reward under the worst-case transition function in the uncertainty set. We study the complexity of the associated threshold problem for RMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets in halfspace representation. Previous results focused on approximating the optimum or restricted attention to specific subclasses of RMDPs, such as interval MDPs or $L_\infty$-RMDPs. Our contributions are threefold: (1) For (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs, we prove that robust policy evaluation is in P via robust linear programming, and that the threshold problem is in NP. As a corollary, robust policy iteration is a polynomial-time algorithm for these RMDPs when the discount factor is fixed. (2) For $s$-rectangular RMDPs, we show that the threshold problem is in PSPACE via the first-order theory of the reals. (3) We establish lower bounds by reducing both parity games and bisimulation metrics between MDP states to the RMDP threshold problem. A polynomial-time algorithm for the threshold problem would resolve the long-standing open question of whether parity games can be solved in polynomial time. The reduction from bisimulation metrics also yields a practical benefit: it allows us to apply robust policy iteration as a more efficient alternative to the standard fixed-point iteration, as our empirical evaluation demonstrates.

</details>


### [21] [Runtime Verification: Monitoring, Knowledge, and Uncertainty (Lecture Notes)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26753)
*Benedikt Bollig*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了运行时验证的自动机理论、时序逻辑和认知基础，涵盖了规范形式、诊断、透明性和可监控性，并讨论了实时扩展带来的算法与语义挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时验证作为一种轻量级验证技术，弥补了模型检测需要预先探索完整系统模型的局限性，特别适用于部分可观测或黑盒系统，其中不确定性只能通过观测来消除。

Method: 采用自动机理论、时序逻辑和认知逻辑的形式化方法，构建运行时监控器；通过离线分析构建在线运行的监控器，并扩展至实时时间设置。

Result: 系统性地阐述了运行时验证的规范形式、诊断方法、透明性分析和可监控性判定，以及在实时环境下算法与语义层面的额外挑战。

Conclusion: 运行时验证通过观测执行轨迹而非完整系统模型实现轻量级验证，实时扩展虽带来额外复杂性但仍具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Runtime verification is a lightweight verification technique that complements model checking by analyzing system executions at runtime rather than exploring a complete system model in advance. It is particularly useful for partially observable or black-box systems, where uncertainty can only be resolved through observation. These lecture notes present automata-theoretic, temporal-logical, and epistemic foundations of runtime verification. They cover specification formalisms, diagnosis, opacity, and monitorability, and explain how offline analysis can be used to construct monitors that operate online on observed executions. The notes also discuss timed extensions and the additional algorithmic and semantic challenges that arise in the real-time setting.

</details>


### [22] [Full Definability in a Profunctorial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26829)
*Takeshi Tsukada,Kazuyuki Asada,Kengo Hirata*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于群胚上profunctors的（双）范畴模型中的完全可定义性，证明了所有稳定的、全的、逻辑族的profunctors都可以通过带MIX的乘法线性逻辑的proof-nets来定义，其中稳定性充当了正确性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于profunctors on groupoids的模型中建立完全可定义性，尽管profunctors比关系更复杂，但希望用类似关系模型的方法来处理。

Method: 将关系模型中的思想与profunctors中的稳定性概念相结合，利用稳定性作为正确性判据，对可定义的profunctors进行完整的刻画。

Result: 所有稳定的、全的、逻辑家族的profunctors都可以由带MIX的乘法线性逻辑的proof-nets定义。

Conclusion: 稳定性可作为正确性判据，且该结果在证明相关的模型中是独立有价值的。

Abstract: A semantic model enjoys full definability if every semantic element in the model is a denotation of some proof or program. Full definability indicates that the model captures programs and proofs in a highly detailed manner.
  This paper studies full definability in a model based on the (bi)category of profunctors on groupoids, which is a proof-relevant variant of the relational model. Despite the fact that a profunctor is far more complicated than a relation, we show that a rather straightforward application of the ideas for the relational model, together with the notion of stability in profunctors, provides a complete characterisation of definable profunctors. More precisely, all logical families of stable and total profunctors are definable by proof-nets of multiplicative linear logic with MIX. As a part of the full definability proof, we show that the stability serves as a correctness criterion, which we think is of independent interest.

</details>

### [23] [Population Dynamics in ARIEL Robotics Systems Featuring Embodied Evolution via Spatial Mating Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26822)
*Victoria Peterson,Akshat Srivastava,Raghav Prabhakar*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种空间嵌入式进化算法，在物理模拟2D环境中使用HyperNEAT控制双足机器人进行实验。研究发现：空间结构会根本性改变进化动态；近距配对与随机配对在峰值适应度上仅差4.9%；能量选择实验存在连续相变；密度依赖性死亡选择机制达到97%完成率但导致适应度下降；揭示了解耦、耦合和确定性选择三种机制的不同稳定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究空间结构如何从根本上改变进化算法的动态特性，特别是在物理模拟环境中，机器人个体需要导航寻找配偶并竞争生存的各种空间感知选择压力下的进化行为。

Method: 采用空间嵌入式进化算法，在MuJoCo物理引擎中模拟2D环境，使用ARIEL壁虎启发式四足机器人和HyperNEAT演化的神经控制器。设计了不同空间感知选择机制（近距配对、随机配对、空间父代选择、随机死亡选择、能量选择、密度依赖性死亡选择等）进行对比实验。

Result: 1) 近距配对与随机配对峰值适应度仅差4.9%，可能处于随机波动范围；2) 空间父代选择与随机死亡选择组合导致不稳定的种群动态；3) 能量选择实验发现连续相变，存在临界区域数量区分灭绝主导和爆发主导两种状态；4) 密度依赖性死亡选择达到97%完成率但导致适应度下降；5) 解耦机制产生双稳态动态，正耦合机制造成反向选择压力，仅确定性适应度选择能维持稳定性。

Conclusion: 空间结构对进化动态有根本性影响，为未来空间嵌入式进化算法的设计提供了重要的约束条件；不同选择机制的组合会产生不同的稳定性特征，需要在设计时谨慎权衡。

Abstract: We present a Spatially Embedded Evolutionary Algorithm where robot individuals exist in a physically simulated 2D environment, must navigate to encounter potential mates, and compete for survival under various spatially-aware selection pressures. Using HyperNEAT evolved neural controllers for ARIEL gecko-inspired quadrupeds in MuJoCo, we investigate how spatial structure fundamentally alters evolutionary dynamics. Our experiments show a modest 4.9% difference in peak fitness between proximity-based and random pairing possibly within stochastic variation while combining spatial parent selection with stochastic death selection produces unstable population dynamics. We discover a continuous phase transition in energy-based selection experiments, with critical zone count separating extinction-dominated and explosion-dominated regimes. Our density-dependent death selection mechanism achieves 97% completion rates but causes fitness decline, revealing a fundamental dilemma where decoupled mechanisms produce bistable dynamics, positively coupled mechanisms create counter-selection pressures, and only deterministic fitness-based selection maintains stability. These findings provide important constraints for future spatial EA design.

</details>

### [24] [FalconApp: Rapid iPhone Deployment of End-to-End Perception via Automatically Labeled Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25949)
*Yan Miao,Will Shen,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FalconApp是一款iPhone应用，通过端到端的前后端流程，将用户手持拍摄的物体短视频转化为可部署的感知模块（掩码检测+6自由度位姿估计）。核心贡献是快速移动部署管道+逼真自动标注流程：从视频重建可编辑GSplat资产，合成带真实标注的渲染图像，训练感知模块并部署回iPhone。五个物体实验表明，平均约20分钟合成数据生成与训练，约30ms设备端延迟，在4/5物体上位姿精度优于PnP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人依赖大规模标注数据，但真实数据集依赖繁重的人工标注且耗时，亟需一种无需人工标注的自动化感知模块生成方法。

Method: 端到端管道：1) 用户用iPhone拍摄物体视频；2) 通过GSplat重建可编辑3D资产；3) 与多样化逼真背景合成，自动生成带掩码和位姿真值的合成图像；4) 训练掩码检测和6-DoF位姿估计模型；5) 将模型部署回iPhone前端。核心是结合3D高斯泼溅(GSplat)的快速重建与自动标注管线。

Result: 在5个不同几何外观的刚体上，平均约20分钟完成合成数据生成和训练，iPhone端到端延迟约30ms，在4/5物体上位姿精度优于传统PnP基线方法，在仿真和真实场景中均验证有效。

Conclusion: FalconApp展示了一种无需人工标注、仅需手机短拍即可快速生成可部署感知模块的实用方案，在速度、精度和便捷性上均有优势，为移动端机器人感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reliable perception for robotics depends on large-scale labeled data, yet real-world datasets rely on heavy manual annotation and are time-consuming to produce. We present FalconApp, an iPhone app with an end-to-end frontend-backend pipeline that turns a short handheld capture of a rigid object into a perception module for mask detection and 6-DoF pose estimation. Our core contribution is a rapid mobile deployment pipeline paired with a photorealistic auto-labeling workflow: from a user-captured video of an object, FalconApp reconstructs an editable GSplat asset, composites it with diverse photorealistic backgrounds, renders synthetic images with ground-truth masks and poses, trains the perception module, and deploys it back to the iPhone frontend. Experiments across five rigid objects with diverse geometry and appearance show that FalconApp produces usable perception models with about 20 minutes of synthetic-data generation and training per object on average, around 30 ms end-to-end on-device latency on iPhone, and better overall pose accuracy than a PnP baseline on 4 / 5 objects in both simulation and real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [25] [A Scaled Three-Vehicle Platooning Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25963)
*Kaiyue Lu,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于车辆编队研究的多车缩比实验平台，包含一辆人工驾驶领航车和两辆自主跟随车，用于验证编队控制算法，特别是横向和纵向控制对编队稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆编队面临的一个关键挑战是在动态机动（如变道）中保持稳定和精确的路径跟踪，领航车产生的横向偏差和航向扰动可能会向下游传播到跟随车辆，因此需要鲁棒的纵横向控制系统来保证编队稳定性。

Method: 开发了一个缩比多车实验平台，由一辆可人工操作的领航车和两辆自主跟随车组成，支持合作控制和人在环自主性研究，可在受控可重复条件下进行领航-跟随协调实验。

Result: 该缩比平台相比全尺寸实车测试更安全、成本更低、更灵活，适用于快速原型开发、控制器验证和多智能体自主研究，同时比纯仿真评估具有更强的物理真实性。

Conclusion: 缩比实验平台是一种有效的折中方案，在安全性与物理真实性之间取得平衡，为编队控制算法的验证提供了可重复的实验环境。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning has attracted increasing attention as a promising approach to improve traffic efficiency, energy consumption, and roadway safety through coordinated multi-vehicle operation. A key challenge in platooning lies in maintaining stable and accurate path tracking during dynamic maneuvers such as lane changes, where lateral deviations and heading disturbances generated by the lead vehicle may propagate downstream to following vehicles. Robust longitudinal and lateral control systems are therefore essential not only for individual vehicle tracking performance, but also for overall platoon stability. For experimental studies, the Intelligent Mobility and Robotics Lab (IMRL) develops a scaled multi-vehicle platform for autonomous platooning research, with a particular emphasis on cooperative control and human-in-the-loop autonomy. This platform consists of one human-operable lead vehicle and two autonomous followers, enabling controlled and repeatable experiments on leader-follower coordination. Compared with full-scale field testing, this scaled platform offers a safer, lower-cost, and more flexible environment for rapid prototyping, controller validation, and multi-agent autonomy studies, while providing stronger physical realism than purely simulation-based evaluations.

</details>


### [26] [Multi-Periodogram Velocity Estimation with Irregular Reference Signals for Robot-Aided ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25974)
*Yi Geng,Pan Cao,Ting Zeng,Yongqian Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人辅助集成感知与通信（ISAC）场景，提出了一种多周期图速度估计算法。该算法利用5G/6G非规则参考信号的时间域模式，将其分解为周期峰值分量和幅度整形分量，在不引入新参考信号或修改3GPP标准的前提下，实现了低信噪比下的鲁棒速度估计。仿真结果表明，与传统周期图方法相比，该方法在10%漏检率下获得了3 dB SNR增益，并将虚警率降低了51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助ISAC系统中，移动机器人作为感知节点只能机会性地复用非规则的5G/6G参考信号，导致传统速度估计算法在该场景下性能受限。现有方法需要专门的感知参考信号或3GPP修改，缺乏标准兼容性和实际部署可行性。

Method: 首先揭示非规则时域模式诱导的速度剖面可分解为周期峰值分量和幅度整形（加权）分量；基于这一结构特性，设计多周期图速度估计算法，该算法符合现有通信标准，无需新增专用感知参考信号或修改3GPP规范。

Result: 与传统周期图处理方法相比，所提方法在低SNR下性能显著提升：在10%漏检率下实现3 dB SNR增益，虚警率降低51%。

Conclusion: 所提多周期图算法在保持标准兼容性的同时，有效提升了非规则参考信号场景下的速度估计鲁棒性，为机器人辅助ISAC系统提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses velocity estimation within robot-aided integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), where mobile robots act as sensing nodes but can only opportunistically reuse irregular 5G/6G reference signals (RSs). We show that the velocity profile induced by such irregular time-domain patterns can be decomposed into a periodic-peak component and an amplitude-shaping (weighting) component. Leveraging this structure, we propose a multi-periodogram velocity estimation algorithm that is standard-compliant and does not require new sensing-dedicated RSs or 3GPP modifications. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with conventional periodogram processing, the proposed method improves low-SNR robustness by achieving a 3 dB SNR gain at the 10% missed-detection rate and reducing false alarms by 51%.

</details>


### [27] [FlowS: One-Step Motion Prediction via Local Transport Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26065)
*Leandro Di Bella,Adrian Munteanu,Bruno Cornelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowS提出局部传输条件化（local transport conditioning）策略，通过场景条件化学习先验和步一致位移场，实现单步扩散生成式运动预测，在Waymo数据集上达到SOTA性能（Soft mAP 0.4804, mAP 0.4703）并保持75 FPS实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在运动预测中能实现高精度和多样化多模态未来预测，但需要数十上百步去噪推理，无法满足严格延迟约束。本文旨在解决精度/多样性与延迟之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 提出局部传输条件化框架FlowS，包含两个核心机制：(1) 在线场景条件学习先验，为每个智能体生成K个校准锚定轨迹，将模式发现转化为局部校正；(2) 步一致位移场，强制半群自一致性，保证单步继承多步精度。锚定在直线路径上避免了弯曲扩散路径上的高方差自举问题。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，FlowS单步推理达到Soft mAP 0.4804和mAP 0.4703（集成），推理速度75 FPS，实现SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 局部传输条件化使得单步生成式运动预测在安全关键自动驾驶中成为可能，证明了当底层传输问题是局部的时，单步积分即可达到高精度。

Abstract: Generative motion prediction must satisfy three simultaneous requirements for real-world autonomy: high accuracy, diverse multimodal futures, and strictly bounded latency. Diffusion models meet the first two but violate the third, requiring tens to hundreds of denoising steps. We identify a conditioning strategy that resolves this tension: \textit{single-step integration is accurate when the underlying transport problem is local}. A model that must both discover the correct behavioral mode and traverse a long displacement in one step accumulates large discretization errors; conditioning the base distribution to lie near plausible futures reduces the problem to short-range refinement, the regime where a single Euler step suffices. We instantiate this \emph{local transport conditioning} in FlowS, a conditional flow matching framework with two mechanisms. First, an online, scene-conditioned learned prior emits $K$ calibrated anchor trajectories per agent, each already near a plausible future, converting mode discovery into local correction. Second, a step-consistent displacement field enforces semigroup self-consistency, guaranteeing that a single step inherits multi-step accuracy. Crucially, anchoring this field at learned priors along straight-line paths yields a {stable, low-variance} training target, unlike prior self-consistency methods that suffer from {high-variance bootstrap} signals on curved diffusion paths. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, FlowS achieves state-of-the-art Soft mAP {(0.4804) and mAP (0.4703) with ensemble at 75\,FPS} with single-step inference, demonstrating that local transport conditioning makes one-step generative motion prediction practical for safety-critical autonomy. Code and pretrained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [28] [Stochastic Entanglement of Deterministic Origami Tentacles For Universal Robotic Gripping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26897)
*Alec Boron,Bokun Zheng,Ziyang Zhou,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种肌腱驱动的折纸触手夹爪，通过局部确定性变形编程与全局随机纠缠的协同作用，实现了对任意形状物体的通用抓取。每个折纸触手由Mylar薄片切割而成，通过精心设计的孔洞、折痕和锥形形状控制变形，单根肌腱拉动即可产生确定性卷曲。多个卷曲触手靠近时产生随机纠缠（编织、打结），从而抓取任意形状物体。研究建立了集成了折纸力学与Cosserat杆的仿真模型，并在重力、水下及在轨模拟场景中验证了抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受折纸启发的机器人夹爪在动态环境中抓取随机形状物体时，通常需要额外增加驱动通道和控制复杂度。本文旨在设计一种结构简单、驱动简洁但能通用抓取任意形状物体的夹爪系统。

Method: 采用Mylar薄片制成锥形折纸触手，通过特定位置的孔洞（用于穿肌腱）、折痕（控制变形方向）和锥形形状设计，实现单根肌腱拉动下的确定性卷曲、弯曲和扭转。多个触手并列时，通过随机纠缠（如编织、打结）实现对随机形状物体的抓取。建立了折纸力学与Cosserat杆耦合的仿真模型，并设计了收放部署机构进行在轨抓取模拟实验。

Result: 实验验证了该夹爪能在重力环境下、水下以及模拟在轨环境中抓取随机形状物体。卷曲触手之间的随机纠缠机制有效实现了通用抓取，无需复杂控制系统。

Conclusion: 这种折纸触手夹爪提供了一种全新的鲁棒抓取策略，通过简单的设计和驱动实现通用物体抓取，在太空、水下等复杂环境中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Origami-inspired robotic grippers have shown promising potential for object manipulation tasks due to their compact volume and mechanical flexibility. However, robust capture of objects with random shapes in dynamic working environments often comes at the cost of additional actuation channels and control complexity. Here, we introduce a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper capable of universal object gripping by exploiting a synergy between local, deterministic deformation programming and global, stochastic entanglements. Each origami tentacle is made by cutting thin Mylar sheets; It features carefully placed holes for routing an actuation tendon, origami creases for controlling the deformation, and a tapered shape. By tailoring these design features, one can prescribe the shrinking, bending, and twisting deformation, eventually creating deterministic coiling with a simple tendon pull. Then, when multiple coiling tentacles are placed in proximity, stochastic entanglement emerges, allowing the tentacles to braid, knot, and grip objects with random shapes. We derived a simulation model by integrating origami mechanics with Cosserat rods to correlate origami design, tendon deformation, and their collective gripping performance. Then, we experimentally tested how these coiling and entangling origami tentacles can grasp objects under gravity and in water. A stow-and-release deployment mechanism was also tested to simulate in-orbit grasping. Overall, the entertaining origami tentacle gripper presents a new strategy for robust object grasping with simple design and actuation.

</details>


### [29] [Lights Out: A Nighttime UAV Localization Framework Using Thermal Imagery and Semantic 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26201)
*Ryan Allen,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义重投影框架，通过将夜间热成像分割结果与白天RGB构建的语义3D地图对齐，实现无人机在GNSS拒止环境下的可靠定位，在6.5 km实际飞行轨迹上达到1.52 m的中位RMSE2D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在GNSS拒止夜间条件下，由于白天RGB地图与夜间热成像之间存在严重的模态差异而导致的可靠备份定位难题。

Method: 提出语义重投影框架：将分割后的热成像观测与白天RGB数据构建的全局语义标注3D地图对齐；在共享语义域中通过对称双向重投影目标函数进行定位，并引入混淆感知权重以提升分割不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在城市场景和半结构化环境的6.5 km夜间真实飞行轨迹上进行离线评估，相对于RTK GNSS真值，系统偏差校正后的RMSE2D为2.18 m，中位RMSE2D为1.52 m。定位性能与语义边缘证据可用性强相关，大误差事件在空间上集中于语义模糊区域而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 语义重投影为仅使用热成像实现全局参考的夜间无人机定位提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Reliable backup localization for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating in GNSS-denied nighttime conditions remains an open challenge due to the severe modality gap between daytime RGB maps and nighttime thermal imagery. This work presents a semantic reprojection framework for map-relative nighttime UAV localization by aligning segmented thermal observations with a globally referenced, semantically labeled 3D map constructed from daytime RGB data. Rather than relying on appearance-based correspondence, localization is formulated in a shared semantic domain and solved via a symmetric bidirectional reprojection objective with confusion-aware weighting to improve robustness under segmentation uncertainty. The approach is evaluated offline across 6.5 km of nighttime, real-world UAV flight trajectories in urban and semi-structured environments. Relative to RTK GNSS ground truth, the system achieves a bias-corrected RMSE2D of 2.18 m and a median RMSE2D of 1.52 m. Results show that localization performance is strongly correlated with the availability of semantic edge evidence and that large-error events are spatially localized to semantically ambiguous areas rather than uniformly distributed. These findings indicate that semantic reprojection offers a promising pathway toward globally referenced nighttime UAV localization using thermal imagery alone.

</details>


### [30] [Split over $n$ resource sharing problem: Are fewer capable agents better than many simpler ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26374)
*Karthik Soma,Mohamed S. Talamali,Genki Miyauchi,Giovanni Beltrame,Heiko Hamann,Roderich Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中有限资源应集中到少数能力强智能体还是分散到多个简单智能体的问题。通过在n个智能体间均分资源（如预算、计算资源、物理尺寸）的设定下，以多智能体覆盖问题为案例，分析了磁盘形覆盖范围随1/n缩放时的覆盖效率、速度与资源分配关系，以及故障率影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，资源分配方式（集中 vs. 分散）对系统性能有根本性影响，但缺乏形式化的理论分析来指导最优的分布度设计。

Method: 形式化定义了n个智能体均分共享资源的资源分配问题；以多智能体覆盖任务为案例，假设智能体磁盘形覆盖区域面积随1/n缩放；从理论上分析了初始覆盖率与n的关系，以及智能体速度随半径或覆盖面积不同缩放方式下的性能差异；并通过计算机模拟考察资源拆分对个体故障率的影响。

Result: 初始覆盖率随智能体数量n增加而增长；若智能体速度与其半径成比例下降，则不同规模群体性能相同；若速度与覆盖面积成比例下降，则单个智能体性能最优。此外，资源拆分会增加个体故障率。

Conclusion: 本文的模型和发现有助于识别多智能体系统在资源约束下的最优分布度水平，为系统设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems, should limited resources be concentrated into a few capable agents or distributed among many simpler ones? This work formulates the split over $n$ resource sharing problem where a group of $n$ agents equally shares a common resource (e.g., monetary budget, computational resources, physical size). We present a case study in multi-agent coverage where the area of the disk-shaped footprint of agents scales as $1/n$. A formal analysis reveals that the initial coverage rate grows with $n$. However, if the speed of agents decreases proportionally with their radii, groups of all sizes perform equally well, whereas if it decreases proportionally with their footprints, a single agent performs best. We also present computer simulations in which resource splitting increases the failure rates of individual agents. The models and findings help identify optimal distributiveness levels and inform the design of multi-agent systems under resource constraints.

</details>


### [31] [2D and 3D Grasp Planners for the GET Asymmetrical Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26212)
*Andrew Goldberg,Ethan Ransing,Anton Kourakin,Cael Magner,Edward H. Adelson,Ken Goldberg*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GET非对称夹爪设计高效的抓取规划方法，解决传统方法在该特定夹爪上性能不足的问题，同时探索2D图像级与3D网格级方法在速度与精度之间的权衡。

Method: GET-2D-1.0：基于单视角RGB-D图像，采用Ferrari-Canny抓取质量度量和新型采样策略进行快速抓取规划。GET-3D-1.0：基于3D网格模型和光线追踪技术进行抓取规划。

Result: GET-2D-1.0在提升成功率、摇晃生存率及抗阻力方面优于包围盒基线方法超过40%。GET-3D-1.0在提升成功率和摇晃生存率上略优于GET-2D-1.0，但计算时间显著更长（17秒 vs 683毫秒）。

Conclusion: GET-2D-1.0在计算效率和抓取性能之间取得了良好平衡，适合实际应用；GET-3D-1.0虽性能略有提升但计算开销过大，实用性较低。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce GET-2D-1.0, a fast grasp planner for the GET asymmetrical gripper that operates from a single-view RGB-D image, using the Ferrari-Canny metric and a novel sampling strategy, and GET-3D-1.0, a mesh-based method using a 3D gripper model and ray-tracing. We evaluate both grasp planners against baselines with physical experiments, which suggest that GET-2D-1.0 can improve over a bounding box baseline by over 40% in lift success, shake survival, and force resistance. Experiments with GET-3D-1.0 suggest slight improvement compared to GET-2D-1.0 on lift success and shake survival, but are more computationally expensive, averaging 17 seconds of planning compared to 683 ms for GET-2D-1.0.

</details>


### [32] [Reactive Motion Generation via Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26450)
*Ahmet Tekden,Dimitrios Kanoulas,Aude Billard,Yasemin Bekiroglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF)，一种基于相位变量的潜在函数学习框架，用于处理存在状态重复（如轨迹交叉）的示教学习任务，能有效泛化于点到点、周期性和6D运动任务，并在外部扰动下表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶DS方法无法处理轨迹交叉（如画"8"字）等状态重复问题；二阶DS方法虽引入速度来区分方向，但在交叉点附近易受扰动影响，且相同位置-速度对仍可能导致歧义；基于相位的方法依赖开环时间/相位变量，扰动后恢复能力有限。

Method: 将势能函数（potential function）条件化在一个相位变量（phase variable）上，该相位变量直接从状态（state）的进展中在线估计，而非使用开环的时间输入。该相位变量使系统能够处理状态重复访问，同时学习到的势能函数生成局部向量场，提供反应式且稳定的控制。

Result: PNPF在点对点、周期性及完整六维运动任务上均有效泛化；在含轨迹交叉的任务上优于现有基线方法；在真实机器人操作实验中，于外部扰动下表现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: PNPF通过将相位变量与势能函数结合，既解决了状态重复导致的歧义问题，又保留了DS方法的闭环稳定性和扰动恢复能力，是一种兼具泛化性和鲁棒性的示教学习框架。

Abstract: Dynamical systems (DS) methods for Learning-from-Demonstration (LfD) provide stable, continuous policies from few demonstrations. First-order dynamical systems (DS) are effective for many point-to-point and periodic tasks, as long as a unique velocity is defined for each state. For tasks with intersections (e.g., drawing an "8"), extensions such as second-order dynamics or phase variables are often used. However, by incorporating velocity, second-order models become sensitive to disturbances near intersections, as velocity is used to disambiguate motion direction. Moreover, this disambiguation may fail when nearly identical position-velocity pairs correspond to different onward motions. In contrast, phase-based methods rely on open-loop time or phase variables, which limit their ability to recover after perturbations. We introduce Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF), an LfD framework that conditions a potential function on a phase variable which is estimated directly from state progression, rather than on open-loop temporal inputs. This phase variable allows the system to handle state revisits, while the learned potential function generates local vector fields for reactive and stable control. PNPF generalizes effectively across point-to-point, periodic, and full 6D motion tasks, outperforms existing baselines on trajectories with intersections, and demonstrates robust performance in real-time robotic manipulation under external disturbances.

</details>


### [33] [Alter-Art: Exploring Embodied Artistic Creation through a Robot Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26473)
*Do Won Park,Samuele Bordini,Giorgio Grioli,Manuel G. Catalano,Antonio Bicchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Alter-Art范式，通过机器人化身Alter-Ego让艺术家以第一人称体验远程具身创作，在舞蹈、戏剧和绘画中验证了艺术家能快速建立临场感，且机器人的物理约束会以不同方式影响各艺术领域的创作过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人艺术框架将机器人视为自主创作者或协作工具，在人机之间留下了鸿沟。本研究旨在弥合这一差距，让艺术家通过远程具身化（inhabiting an alternative body）超越物理限制，重塑创作身份。

Method: 通过机器人化身Alter-Ego，集成沉浸式遥操作与柔顺驱动，在舞蹈、戏剧和绘画三个艺术领域实施Alter-Art范式；收集定性艺术反馈，分析具身性如何塑造创作主体性、身份认同和环境互动。

Result: 艺术家能快速在机器人身体中建立临场感；机器人的物理约束会渗透到创作过程中，且在不同艺术领域表现出差异化影响。

Conclusion: 具身性应作为核心设计原则，这对社交机器人、远程临场以及无障碍艺术表达具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: As with every emerging technology, new tools in the hands of artists reshape the nature of artwork creation. Current frameworks for robotics in arts deploy the robot as an autonomous creator or a collaborator, thus leaving a certain gap between the human artist and the machine. Now, we stand at the dawn of an era where artists can escape physical limitations and reshape their creative identity by inhabiting an alternative body. This new paradigm allows artists not only to command a robot remotely, but also to {\it be} a robot, to see and feel through it, experiencing a new embodied reality. Unlike virtual reality, where art is created in a digital dimension, in this case art creation is still firmly grounded in the material world: clay molded by mechanical hands, paint swept across a canvas or gestures performed on a physical stage alongside human actors. Through the robot avatar Alter-Ego, we explore the Alter-Art paradigm in dance, theater, and painting; it integrates immersive teleoperation and compliant actuation to enable a first-person creative experience. Analyzing qualitative artistic feedback, we investigate how embodiment shapes creative agency, identity and interaction with the environment. Our findings suggest that artists rapidly develop a sense of presence within the robotic body. The robot's physical constraints influence the creative process, manifesting differently across artistic domains. We highlight embodiment as a central design principle, contributing to social robotics and expanding the possibilities for telepresence and accessible artistic expression.

</details>


### [34] [HiPAN: Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation for Quadruped Robots in Unstructured 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26504)
*Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Seokryun Choi,Heechan Shin,Taegeun Yang,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiPAN框架，一种分层后肢自适应导航系统，通过高层策略生成导航指令、低层控制器执行运动，并引入路径引导的课程学习来扩展导航视野，在非结构化3D环境中实现了比经典反应式规划器和端到端基线更高的导航成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在非结构化3D环境中导航面临挑战：需要目标导向运动、有效探索以逃离局部极小值、以及在狭窄限高空间中调整姿态。传统顺序建图-规划方法存在感知误差累积和计算开销大等问题，限制了其在资源受限平台上的应用。

Method: HiPAN采用分层设计：高层策略生成平面速度和身体姿态等导航指令，底层后肢自适应运动控制器执行；引入路径引导的课程学习（Path-Guided Curriculum Learning），从反应式避障逐步扩展到策略性长距离导航。

Result: 在仿真中，HiPAN相比经典反应式规划器和端到端基线取得了更高的导航成功率和路径效率；真实世界实验进一步验证了其在多种非结构化3D环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: HiPAN通过在部署时直接处理机载深度图像，避免了传统建图-规划管线中的感知误差累积和高计算开销，分层设计与课程学习相结合有效解决了长距离导航中的短视问题，是一种适用于资源受限四足机器人的高效导航框架。

Abstract: Navigating quadruped robots in unstructured 3D environments poses significant challenges, requiring goal-directed motion, effective exploration to escape from local minima, and posture adaptation to traverse narrow, height-constrained spaces. Conventional approaches employ a sequential mapping-planning pipeline but suffer from accumulated perception errors and high computational overhead, restricting their applicability on resource-constrained platforms. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation (HiPAN), a framework that operates directly on onboard depth images at deployment. HiPAN adopts a hierarchical design: a high-level policy generates strategic navigation commands (planar velocity and body posture), which are executed by a low-level, posture-adaptive locomotion controller. To mitigate myopic behaviors and facilitate long-horizon navigation, we introduce Path-Guided Curriculum Learning, which progressively extends the navigation horizon from reactive obstacle avoidance to strategic navigation. In simulation, HiPAN achieves higher navigation success rates and greater path efficiency than classical reactive planners and end-to-end baselines, while real-world experiments further validate its applicability across diverse, unstructured 3D environments.

</details>


### [35] [3D Generation for Embodied AI and Robotic Simulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26509)
*Tianwei Ye,Yifan Mao,Minwen Liao,Jian Liu,Chunchao Guo,Dazhao Du,Quanxin Shou,Fangqi Zhu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了面向具身AI的3D生成技术，从数据生成器、仿真环境和Sim2Real桥梁三个角色组织文献，指出领域正从视觉真实感向交互就绪性转变，并识别了物理标注不足、几何质量与物理有效性脱节等关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和机器人系统日益依赖可扩展、多样化且物理真实的3D内容进行仿真训练和真实部署，但现有3D生成技术主要关注视觉真实感，缺乏对具身应用所需的运动学结构、物理属性和交互支持等关键要素的考虑，亟需系统梳理该领域的研究现状与挑战。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，围绕具身AI中3D生成的三个核心角色（数据生成器、仿真环境、Sim2Real桥梁）对现有工作进行分类和梳理，并分析领域发展趋势与瓶颈问题。

Result: 发现该领域正从追求视觉真实感转向强调交互就绪性；识别出四大瓶颈：物理标注数据有限、几何质量与物理有效性之间存在差距、评估标准碎片化、以及持续的仿真到现实鸿沟。

Conclusion: 3D生成要成为具身智能的可靠基础，必须解决物理标注、几何-物理一致性、标准化评估和Sim2Real迁移等关键挑战。

Abstract: Embodied AI and robotic systems increasingly depend on scalable, diverse, and physically grounded 3D content for simulation-based training and real-world deployment. While 3D generative modeling has advanced rapidly, embodied applications impose requirements far beyond visual realism: generated objects must carry kinematic structure and material properties, scenes must support interaction and task execution, and the resulting content must bridge the gap between simulation and reality. This survey presents the first survey of 3D generation for embodied AI and organizes the literature around three roles that 3D generation plays in embodied systems. In \emph{Data Generator}, 3D generation produces simulation-ready objects and assets, including articulated, physically grounded, and deformable content for downstream interaction; in \emph{Simulation Environments}, it constructs interactive and task-oriented worlds, spanning structure-aware, controllable, and agentic scene generation; and in \emph{Sim2Real Bridge}, it supports digital twin reconstruction, data augmentation, and synthetic demonstrations for downstream robot learning and real-world transfer. We also show that the field is shifting from visual realism toward interaction readiness, and we identify the main bottlenecks, including limited physical annotations, the gap between geometric quality and physical validity, fragmented evaluation, and the persistent sim-to-real divide, that must be addressed for 3D generation to become a dependable foundation for embodied intelligence. Our project page is at https://3dgen4robot.github.io.

</details>

*Yuwei Wu,Yichen Zhao,Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-Filter框架，通过星形集构造作为快速滤波器，在复杂噪声环境中高效生成凸碰撞-free空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代优化的凸集膨胀方法在障碍物复杂或传感器数据噪声大时效率下降，且对初始化和精确几何模型敏感。

Method: 提出STAR-Filter轻量级框架，利用星形集构造作为快速滤波器，通过识别障碍物点作为活动支撑约束来减少冗余计算，保持可行性和对传感器噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种环境复杂度下实现最低计算时间，降低多面体生成的保守性，在真实噪声和大规模数据上表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全飞行走廊生成和敏捷四旋翼飞行器规划中有效，尤其适用于噪声环境。

Abstract: Approximating collision-free space is fundamental to robot planning in complex environments. Convex geometric representations, such as polytopes and ellipsoids, are widely employed due to their structural properties, which can be easily integrated with convex optimization. Iterative optimization-based inflation methods can generate large volume polytopes in cluttered environments, but their efficiency degrades as the obstacle set becomes more complex or when sensor data are noisy. These methods are also sensitive to initialization and often rely on accurate geometric models. In this paper, we propose the STAR-Filter, a lightweight framework that employs starshaped set construction as a fast filter for convex region generation in collision-free space. By identifying obstacle points as active supporting constraints, the proposed method significantly reduces redundant computation while preserving feasibility and robustness to sensor noise. We provide theoretical and numerical analyses that characterize the structural properties of the starshaped set and proposed pipeline in environments of varying complexity. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves the lowest computation time and reduces conservativeness in polytope generation for real-world noisy and large-scale data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework for Safe Flight Corridor (SFC) generation and agile quadrotor planning in noisy environments.

</details>
