*Sergej Stanovcic,Daniel Sliwowski,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATLAS是一种专为长期机器人动作分割设计的标注工具，支持多模态数据（多视角视频、本体感觉信号）的时间同步可视化，兼容ROS bags和RLDS等主流数据集格式，相比ELAN工具标注时间减少至少6%，时间对齐精度提升2.8%以上，边界误差降低五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标注工具主要面向纯视觉数据，不支持机器人专有信号（如夹爪状态、力/力矩）的同步可视化，且难以适配不同数据集格式，导致机器人动作边界标注效率低、精度差。

Method: ATLAS提供时间同步的多模态数据可视化（多视角视频+本体感觉信号），支持动作边界、标签和任务结果标注；通过模块化数据集抽象层原生支持ROS bags、RLDS及REASSEMBLE等格式；采用键盘中心化交互界面提高效率。

Result: 在接触式装配任务中，ATLAS相比ELAN每动作标注时间减少至少6%；结合时间序列数据使时间对齐精度提升2.8%以上，边界误差降低五倍（相对于纯视觉标注工具）。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过多模态时间同步可视化和模块化设计，有效提升了长期机器人动作分割的标注效率与精度，且易于扩展至新数据集格式。

Abstract: Annotating long-horizon robotic demonstrations with precise temporal action boundaries is crucial for training and evaluating action segmentation and manipulation policy learning methods. Existing annotation tools, however, are often limited: they are designed primarily for vision-only data, do not natively support synchronized visualization of robot-specific time-series signals (e.g., gripper state or force/torque), or require substantial effort to adapt to different dataset formats. In this paper, we introduce ATLAS, an annotation tool tailored for long-horizon robotic action segmentation. ATLAS provides time-synchronized visualization of multi-modal robotic data, including multi-view video and proprioceptive signals, and supports annotation of action boundaries, action labels, and task outcomes. The tool natively handles widely used robotics dataset formats such as ROS bags and the Reinforcement Learning Dataset (RLDS) format, and provides direct support for specific datasets such as REASSEMBLE. ATLAS can be easily extended to new formats via a modular dataset abstraction layer. Its keyboard-centric interface minimizes annotation effort and improves efficiency. In experiments on a contact-rich assembly task, ATLAS reduced the average per-action annotation time by at least 6% compared to ELAN, while the inclusion of time-series data improved temporal alignment with expert annotations by more than 2.8% and decreased boundary error fivefold compared to vision-only annotation tools.

</details>


### [38] [Atomic-Probe Governance for Skill Updates in Compositional Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26689)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配对抽样交叉版本交换协议，用于研究机器人技能库更新对组合策略性能的影响。发现"主导技能效应"：一个ECM技能原子成功率达86.7%，而其他ECM技能均≤26.7%，主导技能是否进入组合会导致成功率波动高达+50pp。提出原子质量探针和混合选择器，在144次技能更新决策上表征Pareto前沿，原子质量探针是首个可用于技能更新治理的部署就绪原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人技能库方法（如BLADE、SymSkill、Generative Skill Chaining）在测试时将技能库视为冻结的，未分析替换一个技能后组合结果如何变化。本文旨在刻画技能更新对组合策略性能影响的这一维度。

Method: 提出配对抽样交叉版本交换协议，在robosuite操控任务上进行实验。发现主导技能效应后，测试离策略行为距离度量作为廉价预测器但失败。提出原子质量探针（零决策成本）和混合选择器（结合原子探针与选择性组合重验证），在144次技能更新决策上表征Pareto前沿。

Result: 在双轴插销孔任务中，主导技能效应显著：一个ECM原子成功率86.7%，其他≤26.7%，主导技能是否进入组合导致成功率变化高达+50pp。在简单拾取任务中边界效应未定义（所有原子策略饱和于100%）。离策略行为距离无法识别主导ECM。T6任务中原子的探针比完全重验证低23pp（64.6% vs 87.5%），零决策成本；混合选择器(m=10)将差距缩小至~12pp，成本为完全重验证的46%。144事件跨任务平均，原子探针在混合或acles条件下与完全重验证差距在3pp以内。

Conclusion: 原子质量探针是首个原则性的、可部署就绪的技能更新治理原语，适用于组合机器人策略。跨任务平均表现与完全重验证接近，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: Skill libraries in deployed robotic systems are continually updated through fine-tuning, fresh demonstrations, or domain adaptation, yet existing typed-composition methods (BLADE, SymSkill, Generative Skill Chaining) treat the library as frozen at test time and do not analyze how composition outcomes change when a skill is replaced. We introduce a paired-sampling cross-version swap protocol on robosuite manipulation tasks to characterize this dimension of compositional skill learning. On a dual-arm peg-in-hole task we discover a dominant-skill effect: one ECM achieves 86.7% atomic success rate while every other ECM is at or below 26.7%, and whether this dominant ECM enters a composition shifts the success rate by up to +50pp. We characterize the boundary on a simpler pick task where all atomic policies saturate at 100% and the effect is undefined. Across three tasks we further find that off-policy behavioral distance metrics fail to identify the dominant ECM, ruling out the natural cheap predictor. We propose an atomic-quality probe and a Hybrid Selector combining per-skill probes (zero per-decision cost) with selective composition revalidation (full cost), and characterize its Pareto frontier on 144 skill-update decisions. On T6 the atomic-only probe sits 23pp below full revalidation (64.6% vs 87.5% oracle match) at zero per-decision cost; a Hybrid Selector with m=10 closes most of that gap to ~12pp at 46% of full-revalidation cost. On the cross-task average over 144 events, atomic-only is within 3pp of full revalidation under a mixed-oracle caveat. The atomic-quality probe is, to our knowledge, the first principled, deployment-ready primitive for skill-update governance in compositional robot policies.

</details>


### [39] [Unified 4D World Action Modeling from Video Priors with Asynchronous Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26694)
*Jun Guo,Qiwei Li,Peiyan Li,Zilong Chen,Nan Sun,Yifei Su,Heyun Wang,Yuan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-WAM是一个统一的4D世界模型，在单一框架中实现了实时机器人动作执行和高保真4D世界合成（视频+3D重建），通过轻量级结构适配（将预训练扩散Transformer的最后几块复制为深度预测分支）和异步噪声采样（ANS）技术，在机器人操作基准上取得79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率，同时在视觉和几何指标上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一世界模型（如UWM）仅建模2D像素空间，无法在动作执行效率与世界建模质量之间取得平衡，缺乏4D空间感知能力。

Method: 1) 轻量级结构适配：将预训练扩散Transformer的最后几层复制为专用深度预测分支，用于重建未来空间信息；2) 异步噪声采样（ANS）：推理时利用异步去噪调度，用较少步骤快速解码动作用于实时执行，同时用完整步骤生成高保真视频；训练时从联合分布采样以对齐推理分布。

Result: 在5800+小时机器人数据上预训练，X-WAM在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率，4D重建与生成在视觉和几何指标上均超越现有方法。

Conclusion: X-WAM通过轻量级结构适配和异步噪声采样，成功将预训练视频扩散模型扩展为支持实时动作执行和高保真4D世界建模的统一框架，证明了4D世界模型在机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: We propose X-WAM, a Unified 4D World Model that unifies real-time robotic action execution and high-fidelity 4D world synthesis (video + 3D reconstruction) in a single framework, addressing the critical limitations of prior unified world models (e.g., UWM) that only model 2D pixel-space and fail to balance action efficiency and world modeling quality. To leverage the strong visual priors of pretrained video diffusion models, X-WAM imagines the future world by predicting multi-view RGB-D videos, and obtains spatial information efficiently through a lightweight structural adaptation: replicating the final few blocks of the pretrained Diffusion Transformer into a dedicated depth prediction branch for the reconstruction of future spatial information. Moreover, we propose Asynchronous Noise Sampling (ANS) to jointly optimize generation quality and action decoding efficiency. ANS applies a specialized asynchronous denoising schedule during inference, which rapidly decodes actions with fewer steps to enable efficient real-time execution, while dedicating the full sequence of steps to generate high-fidelity video. Rather than entirely decoupling the timesteps during training, ANS samples from their joint distribution to align with the inference distribution. Pretrained on over 5,800 hours of robotic data, X-WAM achieves 79.2% and 90.7% average success rate on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, while producing high-fidelity 4D reconstruction and generation surpassing existing methods in both visual and geometric metrics.

</details>


### [40] [Rule-based High-Level Coaching for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Search-and-Rescue UAV Missions Under Limited-Simulation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26833)
*Mahya Ramezani,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层决策框架，将固定规则高层顾问与在线目标条件低层强化学习控制器相结合，用于无人机搜救任务。在无预训练条件下，高层提供可解释的安全引导，低层通过模式感知优先经验回放在线学习。实验表明，该方法在碰撞减少、早期安全性和样本效率方面优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机决策方法在仿真训练受限、需要快速适应的搜救场景中表现不足。纯强化学习在无预训练条件下早期安全性差、样本效率低；纯规则方法缺乏适应性。因此需要结合两者优势的框架。

Method: 分层框架：高层为离线编译的确定性规则顾问，提供推荐/禁止动作及仲裁权重；低层为在线学习的目标条件RL控制器，采用模式感知优先经验回放，并利用规则衍生的元数据增强经验复用。

Result: 在电池感知多目标配送和移动目标配送两项任务中，该方法相比基线显著降低了碰撞终止次数，提升了早期安全性和样本效率，同时保持了对场景特定动态的在线适应能力。

Conclusion: 规则与在线学习的有效融合能够在无预训练严格条件下实现安全高效的无人机任务执行，为仿真不足的搜救场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical decision-making framework for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) missions motivated by search-and-rescue (SAR) scenarios under limited simulation training. The framework combines a fixed rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. To stress-test early adaptation, we also consider a strict no-pretraining deployment regime. The high-level advisor is defined offline from a structured task specification and compiled into deterministic rules. It provides interpretable mission- and safety-aware guidance through recommended actions, avoided actions, and regime-dependent arbitration weights. The low-level controller learns online from task-defined dense rewards and reuses experience through a mode-aware prioritized replay mechanism augmented with rule-derived metadata. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in obstacle-rich environments. Across both tasks, the proposed method improves early safety and sample efficiency primarily by reducing collision terminations, while preserving the ability to adapt online to scenario-specific dynamics.

</details>


### [41] [Walk With Me: Long-Horizon Social Navigation for Human-Centric Outdoor Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26839)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Xizhou Bu,Yingbo Tang,Hongsheng Li,Jinghui Lu,Xiu-shen Wei,Jiayi Ma,Yu Liu,Jing Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Xiaojun Liang,Long Chen,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Walk with Me 提出了一种无需预建高精地图的框架，利用GPS和公共地图API的候选兴趣点，结合高层视觉语言模型（VLM）进行语义意图接地和粗粒度路径规划，以及低层视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）执行常规导航，并通过观测感知路由机制在复杂场景（如拥挤路口）触发高层安全推理与停等行为，实现了从高级自然语言指令到安全、长时域、 socially compliant 的户外导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图依赖方法需要昂贵的预建高精地图，而基于学习的策略大多局限于室内和短时域场景，无法满足开放世界户外环境中从高层自然语言指令到安全、长时域、社交合规导航的需求。

Method: 1) 利用GPS上下文和公共地图API的轻量级候选兴趣点进行语义目的地接地和航路点提议；2) 高层VLM将抽象指令接地为具体目的地并规划粗粒度航路点序列；3) 观测感知路由机制判断当前情境由低层VLA处理还是需要高层VLM进行显式安全推理；4) 常规路段由低层VLA执行，复杂情境（如拥挤路口）触发高层推理和停等行为。

Result: 提出的Walk with Me框架实现了无需地图的户外长时域社交导航，能够从高层人类指令出发，在开放世界环境中安全、社交合规地执行导航任务。

Conclusion: 通过结合语义意图接地、无地图长时域规划、安全感知推理和低层动作生成，Walk with Me使机器人能够在户外环境中进行以人为中心的实用社交导航。

Abstract: Assisting humans in open-world outdoor environments requires robots to translate high-level natural-language intentions into safe, long-horizon, and socially compliant navigation behavior. Existing map-based methods rely on costly pre-built HD maps, while learning-based policies are mostly limited to indoor and short-horizon settings. To bridge this gap, we propose Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation from high-level human instructions. Walk with Me leverages GPS context and lightweight candidate points-of-interest from a public map API for semantic destination grounding and waypoint proposal. A High-Level Vision-Language Model grounds abstract instructions into concrete destinations and plans coarse waypoint sequences. During execution, an observation-aware routing mechanism determines whether the Low-Level Vision-Language-Action policy can handle the current situation or whether explicit safety reasoning from the High-Level VLM is needed. Routine segments are executed by the Low-Level VLA, while complex situations such as crowded crossings trigger high-level reasoning and stop-and-wait behavior when unsafe. By combining semantic intent grounding, map-free long-horizon planning, safety-aware reasoning, and low-level action generation, Walk with Me enables practical outdoor social navigation for human-centric assistance.

</details>


### [42] [STARRY: Spatial-Temporal Action-Centric World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26848)
*Yuxuan Tian,Yurun Jin,Bin Yu,Yukun Shi,Hao Wu,Chi Harold Liu,Kai Chen,Cong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STARRY，一种将时空世界模型与动作生成对齐的机器人操作策略，通过联合去噪未来时空潜变量和动作序列，并引入几何感知选择性注意力调制，在RoboTwin 2.0和真实实验中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略和世界模型增强策略未能充分建模与动作相关的时空交互结构，限制了机器人在复杂空间-时间场景下的操作能力。

Method: 1) 联合去噪未来时空潜变量与动作序列；2) 提出几何感知选择性注意力调制，将预测深度和末端执行器几何结构转换为令牌对齐的权重，用于选择性动作注意力调制。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0：Clean设置93.82%、Randomized设置93.30%平均成功率；真实世界实验：平均成功率从42.5%（基线π₀.₅）提升至70.8%。

Conclusion: 以动作为中心的时空世界建模能有效提升对时空要求高的机器人动作生成性能，验证了将时空预测与动作生成对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation critically requires reasoning about future spatial-temporal interactions, yet existing VLA policies and world-model-enhanced policies do not fully model action-relevant spatial-temporal interaction structure. We propose STARRY, a world-model-enhanced action-generation policy that aligns spatial-temporal prediction with action generation. STARRY jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences, and introduces Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to convert predicted depth and end-effector geometry into token-aligned weights for selective action-attention modulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, STARRY achieves 93.82% / 93.30% average success under Clean and Randomized settings. Real-world experiments further improve average success from 42.5% to 70.8% over $π_{0.5}$, demonstrating the effectiveness of action-centric spatial-temporal world modeling for spatial-temporally demanding robotic action generation.

</details>


### [43] [Bi-Level Optimization for Contact and Motion Planning in Rope-Assisted Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26910)
*Ruben Malacarne,Ioannis Tsikelis,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Michele Focchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于绳索辅助爬壁机器人的双层优化规划管线框架，通过交叉熵方法与非线性优化求解混合整数问题，选择可行着陆区域并优化控制输入（绳索张力、腿部力及着陆位置），并在ALPINE平台上验证了其在复杂地形中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索辅助爬壁机器人面临混合整数规划挑战，需同时选择可行着陆区域并优化控制输入，现有方法难以有效求解此类复杂耦合问题。

Method: 采用双层优化方案：外层通过交叉熵方法求解离散着陆区域选择问题，内层基于梯度的非线性优化计算动力学可行运动，同时优化绳索张力、腿部力及着陆位置。

Result: 在新型ALPINE爬壁机器人平台上，该方法在多种挑战性地形配置中成功验证了运动规划的可行性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提双层优化框架能有效解决绳索辅助爬壁机器人的混合整数运动规划问题，为复杂垂直地形中的自主攀爬提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a planning pipeline framework for locomotion in rope-assisted robots climbing vertical surfaces. The proposed framework is formulated as a bi-level optimization scheme that addresses a mixed-integer problem: selecting feasible terrain regions for landing while simultaneously optimizing the control inputs, namely rope tensions and leg forces, and landing location. The outer level of the optimization is solved using the Cross-Entropy Method, while the inner level relies on gradient-based nonlinear optimization to compute dynamically feasible motions. The approach is validated on a novel climbing robot platform, ALPINE, across a variety of challenging terrain configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [44] [Preserving Disagreement: Architectural Heterogeneity and Coherence Validation in Multi-Agent Policy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26561)
*Ariel Sela*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Council三阶段 deliberation 框架，通过120次实验验证：架构异质性（不同模型代表不同价值视角）显著降低人工共识（第一选择集中度从70.9%降至46.1%），但一致性验证（用前沿模型评估推理与价值观的对齐）存在保真度-多样性权衡——在主导选项场景中进一步降低集中度，在竞争性选项场景中反而增加集中度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体协商系统在政策模拟中存在"人工共识"问题——无论被分配何种价值视角，评估者智能体最终会收敛到同一选项，无法真实模拟多元价值下的政策辩论。

Method: 提出AI Council三阶段框架：1）架构异质性（每个价值视角分配不同的7-9B参数模型）；2）一致性验证（用前沿模型评估每个评估者推理是否基于其被赋予的价值观）；3）在两种政策场景（儿童福利、住房）下进行120次协商实验，并与同质性基线及三个失败的Delphi设计对比。

Result: 架构异质性显著降低第一选择集中度（儿童福利：70.9%→46.1%，p<0.001；住房：46.0%→22.9%，p<0.001）。一致性验证在主导选项场景下进一步降低集中度至40.8%（p=0.004），但在竞争性选项场景下反而增加至26.6%（p=0.96），呈现保真度-多样性权衡。此外，8B模型对反驳论点呈现二元而非分级响应。

Conclusion: 模型多样性在无客观正确答案的政策模拟中运作机制不同于面向准确性的多智能体辩论；保真度-多样性权衡可能是采用质量加权的多智能体系统的普遍属性；提出可信紧张度（trustworthy tension rate）作为小模型协商能力的诊断指标。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation systems using large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for policy simulation, yet they suffer from artificial consensus: evaluator agents converge on the same option regardless of their assigned value perspectives. We present the AI Council, a three-phase deliberation framework, and conduct 120 deliberations across two policy scenarios to test two interventions. First, architectural heterogeneity (assigning a different 7-9B parameter model to each value perspective) significantly reduces first-choice concentration compared to a homogeneous baseline (child welfare: 70.9% to 46.1%, p < 0.001, r = 0.58; housing: 46.0% to 22.9%, p < 0.001, r = 0.50). This contrasts with accuracy-oriented multi-agent debate, where heterogeneity does not reduce convergence, suggesting model diversity operates differently when no objectively correct answer exists. Second, coherence validation (using a frontier model to assess whether each evaluator's reasoning is grounded in its assigned values) reveals a fidelity-diversity tradeoff: on a scenario with a dominant option, it further reduces concentration (46.1% to 40.8%, p = 0.004), but on a scenario with genuinely competitive options, it increases concentration (22.9% to 26.6%, p = 0.96) by amplifying high-coherence evaluators who cluster on one option. This tradeoff may be a general property of multi-agent systems employing quality weighting. We report negative results from three failed Delphi designs, demonstrate that 8B models exhibit binary rather than graded responses to counter-arguments, and propose the trustworthy tension rate as a diagnostic measure of small-model deliberation capabilities.

</details>

### [45] [Large Language Models for Multilingual Code Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25960)
*Chao Jiang,Dugang Liu,Cheng Wen,Zhiwu Xu,Hua Zheng,Muhammad Sadiq,Jawwad Ahmed Shamsi,Shengchao Qin,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦多语言代码生成和翻译两大任务，系统回顾了代表性方法、基准测试和评估指标，揭示了当前研究对Python等高级语言的偏向性，并指出了实现可信跨语言泛化的挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统本质上是多语言的，但现有代码智能研究严重偏向Python等高级语言，对Rust、OCaml等语言支持较弱。为实现鲁棒的多语言代码智能，亟需系统梳理多语言代码生成与翻译的现状与挑战。

Method: 采用综述方法，聚焦多语言代码生成（从共享的自然语言需求生成多语言代码）和多语言代码翻译（保持语义的跨语言代码转换）两项核心任务，回顾相关代表性方法、基准测试和评估指标。

Result: 识别出现有研究在语言资源上的不均衡分布，总结了多语言代码智能领域的代表性方法、基准测试和评估体系，并归纳了实现可信跨语言泛化的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 多语言代码智能对真实世界多语言系统至关重要，但当前存在语言偏向性问题。需要进一步研究以实现可靠的跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed AI-assisted software engineering, but current research remains biased toward high-resource languages such as Python, with weaker performance in languages like Rust and OCaml. Since real-world systems are inherently polyglot, robust multilingual code intelligence is crucial. This survey focuses on two key tasks: multilingual code generation from shared natural-language requirements, and multilingual code translation that preserves semantics across languages. It reviews representative methods, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, and highlights challenges and opportunities for trustworthy cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [46] [SWE-Edit: Rethinking Code Editing for Efficient SWE-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26102)
*Yikai Zhang,Jiaxin Pei,Kenan Li,Maoquan Wang,Jin Pan,Yu Kang,Shengyu Fu,Elsie Nallipogu,Junjie Hu,Yufan Huang,Zijian Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Edit提出将代码编辑任务分解为查看器（Viewer）和编辑器（Editor）两个子智能体，通过解耦上下文窗口解决标准代码编辑接口中的上下文耦合问题，并在SWE-bench Verified上提升2.1%解决率的同时降低17.9%推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在软件工程任务中存在上下文耦合问题：标准代码编辑接口将代码查看、修改规划和编辑执行混在同一个上下文窗口中，迫使智能体在探索性查看和严格格式化的编辑生成之间交替，导致无关信息累积，降低智能体性能。

Method: SWE-Edit将代码编辑分解为两个专门的子智能体：Viewer按需提取任务相关代码，Editor根据高层计划执行修改；主智能体专注于推理，将上下文密集型操作委派给干净的上下文窗口。同时用GRPO训练Qwen3-8B自适应选择编辑模式，提高编辑效率。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，SWE-Edit提升解决率2.1%，同时降低推理成本17.9%。

Conclusion: 代码编辑的上下文解耦方法能有效提升智能体性能并降低成本；还提出了一个能可靠预测下游智能体性能的代码编辑基准，为编辑模型选择提供实用指导。

Abstract: Large language model agents have achieved remarkable progress on software engineering tasks, yet current approaches suffer from a fundamental context coupling problem: the standard code editing interface conflates code inspection, modification planning, and edit execution within a single context window, forcing agents to interleave exploratory viewing with strictly formatted edit generation. This causes irrelevant information to accumulate and degrades agent performance. To address this, we propose SWE-Edit, which decomposes code editing into two specialized subagents: a Viewer that extracts task-relevant code on demand, and an Editor that executes modifications from high-level plans--allowing the main agent to focus on reasoning while delegating context-intensive operations to clean context windows. We further investigate what makes an effective editing model: observing that the prevalent find-and-replace format is error-prone, we train Qwen3-8B with GRPO to adaptively select editing modes, yielding improved editing efficiency over single-format baselines. On SWE-bench Verified, SWE-Edit improves resolved rate by 2.1% while reducing inference cost by 17.9%. We additionally propose a code editing benchmark that reliably predicts downstream agentic performance, providing practical guidance for editing model selection. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/SWE-Edit.

</details>


### [47] [Asset Administration Shell-Based OCL Validation Framework for Model-Based System Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26384)
*Om Parkash,Jannik Bauer,Vincent Schmitt,Thomas Greiner,Rainer Drath*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过资产管理壳（AAS）管理OCL约束及其验证结果，解决MBSE模型与OCL约束分属不同工具导致的手动操作问题，并在工业场景中验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业系统日益复杂，基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）需要一致且语义验证的模型。OCL支持形式化约束定义，但MBSE模型和OCL约束通常在分离的工具中管理，导致约束应用和结果解释需要大量手动工作。

Method: 提出一种通过资产管理壳（Asset Administration Shells）管理OCL约束及其验证结果的方法，利用AAS作为企业系统中成熟的互操作性技术来桥接MBSE模型与OCL约束管理之间的工具鸿沟。

Result: 通过一个虚构的工业场景演示了该方法，所有工件在GitHub仓库中公开可用以支持可复现性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够减少MBSE模型约束验证过程中的人工操作，提升自动化与互操作水平。

Abstract: Increasing complexity of modern enterprise systems and the demand for automation and interoperability require consistent and semantically validated models in Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE). The Object Constraint Language (OCL) supports formal definition of such constraint validations. However, MBSE models and OCL constraints are typically managed in separate tools, causing manual effort during model constraint application and result interpretation. To address this gap, this paper proposes an approach to managing OCL constraints and their validation results through Asset Administration Shells (a well-established technology for interoperability in enterprise systems). The methodology is demonstrated through a fictional industrial scenario, and to support reproducibility, all artifacts are publicly available in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [48] [LLM-Guided Issue Generation from Uncovered Code Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26118)
*Diany Pressato,Honghao Tan,Mariam Elmoazen,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IssueSpecter是一个结合覆盖率分析与LLM的自动化工具，能检测未覆盖代码段中的缺陷并生成带优先级的结构化问题报告。在13个Python项目中评估，生成10,467个问题报告，其中前130个高优先级问题中84.6%有效，排名性能比规则方法提升50% P@3和41% MRR，并优于CoverUp（81.0% vs 76.2%的有效率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者被AI生成的问题报告淹没，这些报告缺乏可操作性和可复现性，侵蚀了对自动化bug检测工具的信任。

Method: 将覆盖率分析与基于LLM的缺陷识别相结合，生成包含严重性评级、复现步骤和修复建议的结构化问题报告，并通过LLM进行优先级排序。

Result: 在13个Python项目中生成10,467个问题报告。top-130中84.6%有效，仅15.4%误报。LLM排名优于规则排名50% P@3和41% MRR。相比CoverUp，bug有效率81.0% vs 76.2%。

Conclusion: IssueSpecter能有效生成可操作、可复现的高质量问题报告，帮助开发者优先关注最具影响力的bug，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Developers are increasingly overwhelmed by AI-generated issue reports that lack actionability and reproducibility, eroding trust in automated bug detection tools. In this paper, we present IssueSpecter, an automated tool that finds bugs in uncovered code segments and automatically generates prioritized, actionable issue reports. IssueSpecter combines coverage analysis with LLM-based defect identification, producing structured reports complete with severity ratings, reproduction steps, and suggested fixes. We evaluate IssueSpecter on 13 actively maintained Python projects, generating 10,467 issue reports. Manual annotation of the top-130 ranked issues by IssueSpecter confirms that 84.6% of the LLM-generated issues are valid or warrant further investigation, with only 15.4% false positives. LLM-based ranking outperforms rule-based ranking by 50% at P@3 and 41% in MRR. The identified bugs cover a wide variety of types, from logic and boundary errors to security vulnerabilities and state consistency bugs. By ranking issues by priority, IssueSpecter aims to help developers focus their attention on the most impactful bugs first. Finally, we validate IssueSpecter through case studies reproducing real bugs surfaced from its generated issue reports, demonstrating its practical value for automatic bug discovery in open-source Python projects. Compared against CoverUp, a state-of-the-art coverage-driven test generation tool, IssueSpecter achieves a higher bug validity rate (81.0% vs. 76.2%) under identical evaluation conditions, using the same model and the same number of evaluated artifacts per project, while additionally providing structured issue reports with reproduction steps and candidate fixes that are immediately actionable without requiring developers to interpret generated test intent.

</details>


### [49] [Comparing Smart Contract Paradigms: A Preliminary Study of Security and Developer Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26727)
*Matteo Vaccargiu,Andrea Pinna,Maria Ilaria Lunesu,Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对比12组功能等价的Solidity和Move智能合约（由同一团队开发）及调查11名双语言经验开发者，发现Move将安全开销显性密度降低60%（6.7% vs 16.8%），但代码量增大47%，圈复杂度不变；开发者普遍认为Move学习难度中等但安全信心更高（中位数6/7），55%倾向在安全关键场景使用Move。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞已造成数十亿美元损失，引发对编程语言范式能否降低安全开销的探究。现有研究缺乏对资源导向型语言（如Move）与命令式语言（如Solidity）在安全开销上的量化对比。

Method: 混合方法研究：定量分析12组由同一开发团队实现的功能等价Solidity-Move合约对，计算安全检查密度、代码大小、圈复杂度；定性调查11名具备双语言经验的开发者，评估学习难度、安全信心及语言偏好。

Result: Move将显性安全开销密度降低60%（6.7% vs 16.8%，p=0.002, d=-1.75），代码量增大47%（p=0.002, d=1.90），圈复杂度无显著差异。开发者安全信心中位数6/7，10/11高于中性水平，55%优先选择Move用于安全关键应用。

Conclusion: 资源导向范式将安全从运行时验证转向编译时保证，但采用需要投入学习与工具建设。该受控对比为语言选择提供了初步证据，并识别了改进开发者资源的机遇。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities have caused billions in financial losses, raising questions about whether programming language paradigms can reduce security overhead. While imperative languages like Solidity require developers to manually implement security checks, resource-oriented languages like Move encode safety guarantees in type systems. We present a preliminary mixed-methods study analyzing 12 functionally-equivalent contract pairs implemented in both Solidity and Move by the same development team, complemented by a survey of 11 developers experienced in both languages. Quantitative analysis reveals that Move reduces explicit security overhead by 60\% (security check density: 6.7% vs. 16.8%, p=0.002, Cohen's d=-1.75) at the cost of 47% larger code size (p=0.002, d=1.90), while maintaining identical cyclomatic complexity. Developer surveys show moderate learning difficulty but higher safety confidence in Move (Median=6/7, 10 of 11 above neutral), with 55% preferring Move for security-critical applications despite ecosystem maturity gaps. These preliminary findings suggest resource-oriented paradigms shift security from runtime validation to compile-time guarantees, though adoption requires investment in learning and tooling. The controlled comparison provides initial evidence for paradigm effects on smart contract development, informing language selection decisions and identifying opportunities for improved developer resources.

</details>


### [50] [ImproBR: Bug Report Improver Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26142)
*Emre Furkan Akyol,Mehmet Dedeler,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ImproBR，一个基于LLM的流水线，用于自动检测和改进漏洞报告中缺失、不完整、模糊的步骤重现(S2R)、观察行为(OB)和预期行为(EB)部分。该方法结合了微调DistilBERT、启发式分析和LLM分析器的混合检测器，利用GPT-4o mini和基于Minecraft Wiki领域知识的检索增强生成(RAG)管道。在Mojira数据集上，结构完整性从7.9%提升至96.4%，可执行的S2R比例从28.8%提升至67.6%，完全可复现的漏洞报告从1个增加到13个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞跟踪系统中用户提交的低质量报告经常遗漏关键细节（如S2R、OB、EB），导致开发者难以复现和修复漏洞，亟需自动化方法提升报告质量。

Method: 提出ImproBR流水线：1) 混合检测器（微调DistilBERT + 启发式分析 + LLM分析器）识别缺失、不完整、模糊的内容；2) GPT-4o mini配合section-specific few-shot prompts和基于Minecraft Wiki领域知识的RAG管道对问题进行改进。

Result: 在Mojira的139个真实漏洞报告上，结构完整性从7.9%提升至96.4%，可执行S2R比例从28.8%提升至67.6%，完全可复现报告从1个增至13个。

Conclusion: ImproBR有效提升了漏洞报告的完整性和可复现性，展示了LLM结合领域知识在软件质量保障中的潜力。

Abstract: Bug tracking systems play a crucial role in software maintenance, yet developers frequently struggle with low-quality user-submitted reports that omit essential details such as Steps to Reproduce (S2R), Observed Behavior (OB), and Expected Behavior (EB). We propose ImproBR, an LLM-based pipeline that automatically detects and improves bug reports by addressing missing, incomplete, and ambiguous S2R, OB, and EB sections. ImproBR employs a hybrid detector combining fine-tuned DistilBERT, heuristic analysis, and an LLM analyzer, guided by GPT-4o mini with section-specific few-shot prompts and a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in Minecraft Wiki domain knowledge. Evaluated on Mojira, ImproBR improved structural completeness from 7.9% to 96.4%, more than doubled the proportion of executable S2R from 28.8% to 67.6%, and raised fully reproducible bug reports from 1 to 13 across 139 challenging real-world reports.

</details>


### [51] [AI Observability for Large Language Model Systems: A Multi-Layer Analysis of Monitoring Approaches from Confidence Calibration to Infrastructure Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26152)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了2025-2026年五项关于AI可观测性的前沿研究，构建了五层可观测性分类体系，并指出各监控层已趋于成熟，但将模型级置信信号与基础设施级异常整合为连贯运维智能仍是该领域的关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生产环境中的部署迫切需要覆盖全栈（从模型内部到GPU内核）的可观测性系统，但现有监控方法仅针对栈的孤立层面，缺乏对这些技术之间关联、重叠或互补关系的综合分析。

Method: 对五项近期研究（2025-2026）进行结构化分析，构建五层可观测性分类体系，将贡献组织成统一对比框架，并识别四个关键未解决的差距。

Result: 各项独立监控层已快速成熟，但将模型级置信信号与基础设施级异常相连接以形成连贯运维智能的集成挑战仍是该领域定义性的开放问题。

Conclusion: 虽有五个层面的技术进展（RL置信度校准、命题探针内部状态监控、CoT可监控性评估、云运维基准测试、非侵入式推理级追踪），但实现全栈可观测性的核心障碍在于各层之间的集成而非各层本身的发展。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in production environments has created an urgent need for observability systems that span the full stack -- from model internals to GPU kernels. Yet existing monitoring approaches address isolated layers of this stack, and no comprehensive analysis has examined how these techniques relate, overlap, or complement each other. This paper presents a structured analysis of five recent research contributions (2025-2026) that collectively define the emerging landscape of AI observability: confidence calibration via reinforcement learning (MIT), internal state monitoring through propositional probes (UC Berkeley), chain-of-thought monitorability evaluation (OpenAI), autonomous cloud operations benchmarking (Microsoft Research, UC Berkeley, UIUC), and non-intrusive inference-level tracing (TRUFFLD). We organize these contributions into a five-layer observability taxonomy, synthesize their key findings into a unified comparison, and identify four critical gaps that remain unaddressed. We further contextualize these research directions against practical operational observability systems that translate infrastructure telemetry into actionable insights for site reliability teams. Our analysis reveals that while individual monitoring layers have matured rapidly, the integration challenge -- connecting model-level confidence signals with infrastructure-level anomalies into coherent operational intelligence -- remains the defining open problem for the field.

</details>


### [52] [LLM-Assisted Empirical Software Engineering: Systematic Literature Review and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26192)
*Victoria Gomes,Delaney Selb,Fabio Palomba,Rodrigo Spinola,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述分析了LLMs在实证软件工程中的应用，识别出69项LLM辅助任务，主要集中在数据分类、过滤和评估。LLM的使用以自动化导向为主，存在幻觉、不一致性和可重复性问题，报告实践不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证软件工程面临数据规模、方法复杂性和可重复性挑战，LLMs虽有潜力但应用分散，缺乏全面综述指导其负责任采用。

Method: 对2020-2025年间12个顶级软件工程 venues 的同行评审论文进行系统文献综述，最终纳入50篇主要研究，通过定性和定量综合进行分析。

Result: 识别出69项LLM辅助任务，主要应用于软件仓库挖掘和受控实验，集中在数据分类、过滤和评估阶段。LLM集成以自动化导向为主，决策支持有限；优势在于效率和可扩展性，局限包括幻觉、不一致性、提示敏感性和可重复性问题；报告实践不完整。

Conclusion: LLMs在ESE中的应用正在增长但以自动化驱动为主，存在以人为本的集成和透明度方面的差距，并提出了负责任使用的研究议程。

Abstract: Context: Empirical Software Engineering (ESE) faces increasing challenges due to data scale, methodological complexity, and reproducibility concerns. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools to support empirical workflows, yet their use remains fragmented, with no comprehensive synthesis to guide responsible adoption.
  Aims: This study analyzes how LLMs are used in ESE, examining supported tasks, phases of the empirical lifecycle, integration into workflows, reported benefits and limitations, and the extent of reproducibility-related reporting. It also identifies gaps and future research directions.
  Method: We conducted a systematic literature review of peer-reviewed papers (2020-2025) across 12 leading software engineering venues, resulting in 50 primary studies analyzed through qualitative and quantitative synthesis.
  Results: We identified 69 LLM-assisted tasks, mainly in mining software repositories and controlled experiments, focusing on classification, filtering, and evaluation. LLMs are used across multiple phases but are concentrated in data processing and analysis. Their integration is largely automation-oriented, with limited decision-support use. Benefits emphasize efficiency and scalability, while limitations include hallucinations, inconsistency, prompt sensitivity, and reproducibility issues. Reporting practices are often incomplete.
  Conclusion: LLM use in ESE is growing but remains automation-driven, with gaps in human-centered integration and transparency. We outline implications and research agenda for responsible use.

</details>


### [53] [Agentic AI in the Software Development Lifecycle: Architecture, Empirical Evidence, and the Reshaping of Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26275)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了从代码补全到大语言模型驱动的智能体软件工程系统的范式转变，提出了六层参考架构和智能体SDLC，并汇总了性能、生产力和劳动力市场影响的关键数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能力的质变（多步推理、工具使用、长程规划）使得软件工程从行/函数粒度的代码补全跃迁到仓库/功能/算法粒度的智能体系统，亟需统一框架来理解这一转变并识别关键挑战。

Method: 综合来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google DeepMind、微软研究院、普林斯顿、斯坦福等机构的成果，提出六层参考架构（架构层），对比传统SDLC与A-SDLC，汇总SWE-bench验证集性能、受控实验时间节省和劳动力市场影响的实证数据。

Result: SWE-bench验证集性能从2023年10月的1.96%提升至2026年4月的78.4%；受控实验中节省13.6%-55.8%的时间；2026年Anthropic采样中49%的职业中AI用于至少四分之一的任务。

Conclusion: 核心研究问题已从代码生成转向人类监督下的委托执行，五个开放问题（评估、治理、技术债务、技能再分配、注意力经济学）将决定智能体转型对学科是否净正向。

Abstract: The arrival of large language models (LLMs) capable of multi-step reasoning, tool use, and long-horizon planning has produced a qualitative shift in software engineering. Where earlier code-completion tools such as GitHub Copilot operated at the granularity of a line or function, modern agentic systems -- Claude Code, OpenAI Codex CLI, Google Jules, Devin, OpenHands, SWE-agent, MetaGPT, ChatDev, and DeepMind's AlphaEvolve -- operate at the granularity of a repository, a feature, or an algorithm. We synthesize work from Anthropic, OpenAI, Google DeepMind, Microsoft Research, Princeton, Stanford, and the broader academic community to characterize this transition. We propose a six-layer reference architecture for agentic software engineering systems, contrast a traditional Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC) with an emerging Agentic SDLC (A-SDLC), and consolidate empirical evidence on performance (a rise from 1.96% to 78.4% on SWE-bench Verified between October 2023 and April 2026), productivity (13.6%-55.8% time savings across controlled studies), and labor-market impact (49% of jobs sampled by Anthropic in 2026 saw AI used for at least a quarter of their tasks). We argue that the central object of inquiry has shifted from code generation to delegated execution under human supervision, and we identify five open problems -- evaluation, governance, technical debt, skill redistribution, and the economics of attention -- that will determine whether the agentic transition is net-positive for the discipline.

</details>


### [54] [Towards Intelligent Computation Offloading in Dynamic Vehicular Networks: A Scalable Multilayer Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26416)
*Falk Dettinger,Matthias Weiß,Baran Can Gül,Sruthi Mangala Suresh,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向软件定义汽车的四层计算卸载流水线，通过改进的粒子群优化算法将车载计算任务动态卸载到云/边缘资源，在满足严格往返时延约束下显著降低响应时间（CPU下平均26ms，GPU下平均550ms），有效弥合了车辆全生命周期内静态硬件与动态软件需求之间的计算鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车面临日益扩大的计算鸿沟：先进算法和频繁软件更新对算力需求持续增长，而车载硬件在车辆10年以上的使用寿命内保持静态不变，这种不匹配威胁到高级驾驶辅助系统和实时感知任务等安全关键功能的性能。

Method: 提出四层计算卸载流水线架构，将车辆功能动态分发到云和边缘资源，同时满足严格的往返时延约束；核心贡献是改进的粒子群优化算法，通过结合距离和方向惩罚项以及功能需求来优化移动车辆的边缘服务器选择。

Result: 基于Kubernetes的云基础设施和真实车辆移动模式评估表明：相比传统暴力搜索方法，本方法在降低平均响应时间的同时保持了延迟关键型任务的成功率；改进PSO算法在CPU上10台服务器和任务下平均执行时间为26ms，在GPU上15台服务器和1000个任务下为550ms。

Conclusion: 实验结果证实了该流水线在弥合下一代软件定义汽车计算鸿沟方面的有效性。

Abstract: Software Defined Vehicles face an increasing computational gap as advanced algorithms and frequent software updates demand more processing power while onboard hardware remains static throughout a vehicle's 10+ year lifespan. This mismatch threatens the performance of safety-critical functions including advanced driver-assistance systems and real-time perception tasks. We propose a novel four-layer computation offloading pipeline that dynamically distributes vehicular functions to cloud and edge resources while meeting strict Round Trip Time constraints. Our key contribution is an enhanced Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm that integrates distance- and direction-based penalties with functional requirements to optimize edge server selection for mobile vehicles. Evaluation using a Kubernetes-based cloud infrastructure with realistic vehicular mobility patterns demonstrates that our approach reduces average response time compared to conventional Brute-Force methods while maintaining the success rate for latency-critical tasks. The modified Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm achieves an average execution time of 26 ms across ten servers and tasks on Central Processing Unit, and 550ms across 15 servers with 1000 tasks on Graphics Processing Unit. These results confirm the pipeline's effectiveness in bridging the computational gap for next-generation Software Defined Vehicles (SDV).

</details>


### [55] [An Empirical Study of Speculative Decoding on Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26469)
*Yijia Li,Junkai Chen,Xing Hu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性实证研究了推测解码（SD）在软件工程（SE）任务中的加速效果，覆盖基于模型和无模型方法，涵盖代码生成、编辑和修复场景。结果表明SD对小型模型加速效果显著，基于模型方法适合代码生成，无模型方法适合仓库级修复和编辑，SE任务的可预测性允许更激进的超参数设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中广泛应用，但自回归推理的高延迟成为部署瓶颈；此前缺乏对长上下文仓库级任务和复杂智能体交互中推测解码有效性的系统研究。

Method: 系统性基准测试多种推测解码策略（包括基于模型和无模型方法），在代码生成、编辑、修复等代表性场景中进行实证研究。

Result: SD对推理加速有潜力，小型模型加速比更高；基于模型方法适合代码生成，无模型方法适合仓库级修复和编辑；SE任务的重复性提升无模型方法性能，其可预测性允许更激进的超参数。

Conclusion: 总结了指导方针，帮助提高SE场景中的推理效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become widely used for Software Engineering (SE) tasks, spanning from function-level code generation to complex repository-level workflows. However, the high latency of autoregressive inference remains a significant bottleneck, hindering their deployment in interactive environments. While Speculative Decoding (SD) offers a promising technique for lossless acceleration, prior research on long-context repository-level tasks and complex agentic interactions remains limited. To bridge this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of SD in SE tasks. We systematically benchmark a comprehensive spectrum of strategies, encompassing both model-based and model-free methods, across representative generation, editing, and repair scenarios. Our empirical results indicate that SD demonstrates clear potential for accelerating inference, particularly for smaller models that achieve higher speedups than those of their larger counterparts. We find that the effectiveness of SD methods varies across different task scenarios. Model-based approaches are well-suited for code generation, whereas model-free methods are better adapted to repository-level repair and editing scenarios. Furthermore, we observe that the repetitiveness of SE tasks improves the performance of model-free methods. In contrast to natural language tasks, the higher predictability of SE tasks allows for more aggressive hyperparameters. Our findings are summarized as guidelines to help increase inference efficiency for SE scenarios.

</details>


### [56] [RepoDoc: A Knowledge Graph-Based Framework to Automatic Documentation Generation and Incremental Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26523)
*Dong Xu,Mingwei Liu,Xiwen Wang,Jianfeng Zhong,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoDoc 提出基于仓库知识图谱（RepoKG）的自动化文档生成与增量维护系统，通过实体关系提取、模块聚类和智能体生成实现结构化、跨引用的文档，在24个仓库上相比现有方法提升API覆盖率32.5%、生成速度3倍、Token消耗减少85%，增量更新耗时降低73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化文档工具将源码视为扁平文本片段，生成孤立、缺乏语义结构的文档，且存在Token消耗大、生成慢、无法捕捉代码变更传播影响的问题。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 构建仓库知识图谱RepoKG，提取代码实体及其关系；(2) 模块聚类，将代码组织为功能内聚的层次化单元；(3) 基于智能体生成，查询图谱生成模块化、交叉引用的文档及Mermaid图表。增量维护时，通过语义影响传播机制双向遍历RepoKG定位影响范围，实现选择性定向重生成。

Result: 在24个仓库、8种编程语言上，API覆盖率提升32.5%，完整性提升10.4%，生成速度快3倍，Token减少85%。增量更新耗时降低73%，Token减少77%，更新召回率提升10.2%。

Conclusion: 基于知识图谱的结构化文档生成方法在质量和效率上显著优于现有方法，能有效支持大规模代码库的文档维护和增量更新。

Abstract: Maintaining up-to-date, comprehensive documentation for large codebases is a persistent challenge. Recent progress in automated documentation has moved from template-based rules to large language models (LLMs), yet existing tools still process source code as flat fragments, producing isolated documents that lack semantic structure. This design also leads to excessive token consumption and slow generation, while failing to capture how code changes propagate across dependencies.
  We propose RepoDoc, a system that uses a repository knowledge graph (RepoKG) as the semantic foundation for the entire documentation lifecycle. Our framework consists of three stages: (1) RepoKG construction, which extracts code entities and their relationships; (2) module clustering, which groups code into functionally cohesive, hierarchical units; and (3) skillful agent-based generation, which queries the graph to create modular, cross-referenced documentation with auto-generated Mermaid diagrams. For incremental maintenance, a semantic impact propagation mechanism navigates the RepoKG bidirectionally to pinpoint all affected parts, allowing selective, targeted regeneration.
  Evaluated on 24 repositories across 8 programming languages, RepoDoc substantially outperforms state-of-the-art alternatives. It improves API coverage by 32.5% and completeness by 10.4%, while generating documentation 3x faster with 85% fewer tokens. For incremental updates, it cuts update time by 73% and token usage by 77%, and achieves 10.2% higher update recall, more accurately reflecting code changes in the regenerated documentation.
  The source code and experimental artifacts are available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/RepoDoc.

</details>


### [57] [Identifying and Characterizing Semantic Clones of Solidity Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26526)
*Ermanno Francesco Sannini,Francesco Salzano,Simone Scalabrino,Rocco Oliveto,Remo Pareschi,Corrado Aaron Visaggio,Andrea Di Sorbo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代码和注释分析的Solidity语义克隆检测方法，在约30万合约数据集上实现了59%精度和97%召回率，并探索了LLM在无注释代码中辅助检测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术在语法克隆检测方面效果良好，但语义克隆（功能等价但结构不同）的识别仍是一个开放问题。由于克隆会传播漏洞和缺陷，有效的语义克隆检测对于保障智能合约安全至关重要。

Method: 首先收集了约30万个以太坊智能合约（82.07%为Solidity 0.8版本），采用基于代码和注释分析的方法，结合句子变换器（如BERT）进行语义等价函数对的识别。同时探索了利用LLM生成代码摘要来弥补注释缺失的场景。

Result: 在1155个函数对的统计显著样本上，整体精度达59%（同名函数精度升至84%），召回率达97%。对无注释代码，LLM生成的摘要结合BERT可实现75%精度的语义克隆检测。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Solidity克隆检测的现代基准，揭示了语义等价函数的结构差异常反映安全选择、模块化和gas优化等设计替代方案，为自动发现安全高效的代码替代方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: Smart Contracts are essential blockchain components, mainly written in Solidity. The high availability of public Solidity code leads to frequent reuse and high clone ratios. Since cloning can propagate vulnerabilities and flaws, effective detection is crucial. Although existing techniques work well in detecting syntactic clones, the identification of semantic clones is an open problem. To address this challenge, in this paper, we present and empirically assess a scalable methodology, based on analyzing code and comments, to spot semantically equivalent Solidity functions. We first collected an up-to-date dataset of about 300,000 Ethereum smart contracts, 82.07% of which are compliant with modern Solidity version 0.8. Manual validation of a statistically significant sample comprising 1,155 function pairs confirms the effectiveness of our solution, achieving an overall precision of 59% (rising to 84% for homonymous functions) and a recall of 97%. Besides, we explore the structural differences occurring on semantically equivalent Solidity functions, demonstrating that they often represent design alternatives focused on security choices, modularization, and gas optimization. Finally, we investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as documentation engines in scenarios where code comments are poor or absent. Our results show that LLM-generated summaries, combined with sentence transformers like BERT, can bridge the documentation gap, enabling the identification of semantic clones in uncommented code with 75% precision. This work establishes a modern benchmark for Solidity clone detection and provides a foundation for the automated discovery of secure and efficient code alternatives.

</details>


### [58] [Graph Construction and Matching for Imperative Programs using Neural and Structural Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26578)
*Arshad Beg,Diarmuid O'Donoghue,Rosemary Monahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将带标注的指令式程序（C/ACSL、Java/JML、Dafny）转换为类型化属性图的流水线，结合AST解析与语义嵌入（SentenceTransformer、CodeBERT），为跨语言验证工件的结构相似性与语义相似性识别奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨程序复用验证工件需要识别程序及其规约之间的结构和语义相似性，而当前缺乏统一的图表示方法作为该目标的基础步骤。

Method: 构建流水线：1) 解析AST获取结构信息；2) 利用SentenceTransformer和CodeBERT等模型生成语义嵌入；3) 将两者融合为类型化属性图，捕获结构关系与语义上下文。

Result: 在C/ACSL、Java/JML、Dafny三个不同语言和标注风格的数据集上，成功生成了一致的图表示，验证了跨语言可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为后续语义增强和近似图匹配以实现可扩展的验证工件复用提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Reusing verification artefacts requires identifying structural and semantic similarities across programs and their specifications. In this paper, we focus on graph construction as a foundational step toward this goal. We present a pipeline that converts imperative programs and their annotations into typed, attributed graphs. Our experiments cover datasets including C with ACSL, Java with JML, and Dafny for C\#. The pipeline integrates abstract syntax tree parsing with semantic embeddings derived from models such as SentenceTransformer and CodeBERT. This enables the generation of graph representations that capture both structural relationships and semantic context. Our results show that consistent graph representations can be constructed across different languages and annotation styles. This work provides a practical basis for future steps in semantic enrichment and approximate graph matching for scalable verification artefact reuse.

</details>


### [59] [Recommendations for Efficient and Responsible LLM Adoption within Industrial Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26590)
*Krishna Ronanki,Beatriz Cabrero-Daniel,Tomas Herda,Stefan Sitkovich,Jennifer Horkoff,Christian Berger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对三家组织进行多案例研究和在线调查，提出了七项关于在工业软件工程中高效、负责任采用大型语言模型的可操作建议，涵盖AI助手角色、利益相关者满意度、任务适用性、工作流影响、人类监督机制和从业者技能等方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在软件工程中应用广泛，但缺乏在工业环境中高效、负责任采用LLM的可行最佳实践指南。

Method: 采用多案例研究方法，对三家在软件工程活动中使用LLM的组织进行定性主题分析，综合出七项建议；并通过在线调查评估软件从业者对建议的相关性感知。

Result: 提出了七项聚焦于用户偏好、利益相关者满意度、任务适用性范围、工作流影响、人类监督机制和从业者技能的建议；在线调查显示参与者对建议相关性有较高认同度。

Conclusion: 未来研究方向包括将七项建议与欧盟AI法案原则进行映射，以检验其与现行监管合规框架的关系。

Abstract: Context: Large language models (LLMs) are observed to have a significant positive impact on various software engineering (SE) activities. With improved accessibility, the adoption of powerful LLMs in industry has surged recently. However, there is a lack of actionable best practices for the efficient and responsible adoption of LLMs within industrial software settings. Objectives: We developed seven actionable recommendations to address this research gap. Methods: We conducted a multi-case study with three organisations that use LLMs within their SE activities and synthesised seven recommendations through qualitative thematic analysis. We conducted a complementary online survey with software practitioners from various industries to evaluate the perceived relevance of our recommendations. Results: Our results and recommendations focus on (i) users' preference to use LLMs as AI assistants, (ii) the importance of relevant stakeholders' satisfaction in the LLM-output evaluation, (iii) scoping the applicability of LLMs within SE tasks, (iv) the effect of LLMs on SE workflows, (v) the necessity and directions for developing human oversight mechanisms, and (vi) the necessary skills for practitioners for leveraging LLMs within SE. The online survey indicates a high level of agreement from the participants regarding the perceived relevance of the recommendations. Conclusion: We outline future research directions, including mapping the seven recommendations to the principles of the EU AI Act (AIA) in order to examine how they relate to the current regulatory compliance frameworks.

</details>


### [60] [TDD Governance for Multi-Agent Code Generation via Prompt Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26615)
*Tarlan Hasanli,Shahbaz Siddeeq,Bishwash Khanal,Pyry Kotilainen,Tommi Mikkonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种AI原生TDD框架，将经典TDD原则形式化为机器可读的清单，并通过分层架构（规划、生成、修复、验证）中的提示级和工作流级治理机制强制执行阶段顺序、有界修复循环、验证闸门和原子变异控制，以提高LLM辅助开发的稳定性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件开发中常表现出不稳定性、非确定性和缺乏开发纪律，而现有基于LLM的方法仅将测试作为辅助输入而非可执行的流程约束，亟需一种能将软件工程纪律直接编码到提示编排中的结构化方法。

Method: 将经典TDD原则形式化为机器可读清单，构建分层架构（规划→生成→修复→验证），通过提示级和工作流级治理机制强制执行阶段顺序、有界修复循环、验证闸门和原子变异控制，分离模型提案与确定性引擎的权威。

Result: 系统能够有效改进LLM辅助开发的稳定性和可重复性，通过强制执行TDD流程纪律来约束模型行为。

Conclusion: 将软件工程纪律直接编码到提示编排中，是实现可靠LLM辅助开发的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) accelerate software development but often exhibit instability, non-determinism, and weak adherence to development discipline in unconstrained workflows. While test-driven development (TDD) provides a structured Red-Green-Refactor process, existing LLM-based approaches typically use tests as auxiliary inputs rather than enforceable process constraints. We present an AI-native TDD framework that operationalizes classical TDD principles as structured prompt-level and workflow-level governance mechanisms. Extracted principles are formalized in a machine-readable manifesto and distributed across planning, generation, repair, and validation stages within a layered architecture that separates model proposal from deterministic engine authority. The system enforces phase ordering, bounded repair loops, validation gates, and atomic mutation control to improve stability and reproducibility. We describe architecture and discuss encoding software engineering discipline directly into prompt orchestration, which we think offers a promising direction for reliable LLM-assisted development.

</details>


### [61] [Will It Break in Production? Metric-Driven Prediction of Residual Defects in Python Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26667)
*Giuseppe De Rosa,Pietro Liguori*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用机器学习与深度学习方法预测Python项目发布后的故障，发现基于监督学习的度量模型（RandomForest、XGBoost、CatBoost）性能最优（召回率0.85-0.9），而LLM和无监督模型效果不佳；过程度量（如代码年龄、变更频次、开发者活动）及类/文件大小最具预测力，且度量与代码嵌入在表征空间中互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的动态特性使得测试复杂，缺陷易逃逸，因此需要有效的故障预测方法来保障软件质量。

Method: 使用包含83个产品、过程、统计及Python特定度量指标的平衡数据集（4,000+标注故障），结合规范化代码表示，进行跨项目实验，比较LLM、无监督模型与监督度量模型（RandomForest、XGBoost、CatBoost）的性能，并采用主成分分析（PCA）探索度量与嵌入的关系。

Result: 监督度量模型表现最佳，召回率达0.85-0.9，假阴性减少一个数量级；过程度量（年龄、变更、开发者活动）及类/文件大小最具预测性；PCA表明度量与代码嵌入在表征空间中占据互补区域。

Conclusion: 对于Python项目后发布故障预测，监督式度量模型优于LLM和无监督方法，且过程和规模度量是关键的预测因子，度量与代码嵌入可互补使用。

Abstract: Python's dynamic nature complicates testing and increases the possibility that some defects evade detection, so an effective fault prediction becomes essential. We examine whether post-release faults can be predicted using modern ML and DL. Using a balanced dataset of over 4,000 labeled faults with 83 product, process, statistical, and Python-specific metrics plus normalized code representations, we conduct cross-project experiments. LLMs and unsupervised models fail to distinguish residual from non-residual faults, while supervised metric-based models (RandomForest, XGBoost, CatBoost) perform far better, yielding a 0.85-0.9 recall and cutting false negatives by an order of magnitude. Process metrics, especially age, churn, and developer-activity, alongside class and file size, consistently prove most predictive. Notably, the Principal Component Analysis shows that metrics and code embeddings occupy distinct regions of the representation space, suggesting that they capture complementary rather than redundant information.

</details>


### [62] [Which Types of Heterogeneity Matter for Root Cause Localization in Microservice Systems ?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26670)
*Runzhou Wang,Shenglin Zhang,Wenwei Gu,Yongxin Zhao,Chenyu Zhao,Dan Pei,Yuxuan Chen,Yangyuxin Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出NexusRCL，一种半监督框架，通过构建异构图建模服务和主机之间的非对称故障传播关系，有效利用云原生系统中的多层面异构性进行微服务根因定位，在工业基准数据集上Top-1准确率提升达49.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务根因定位方法仅关注云原生系统异构性的单一层面（如仅数据层面或仅实体层面），未能充分利用异构性的完整诊断价值，导致定位效果受限。

Method: 提出NexusRCL框架：1）将服务和主机建模为异构图中的不同类型节点；2）设计基于事件的抽象机制捕获数据层面和实体层面的异构性；3）采用主动学习最小化标注成本，实现半监督学习。

Result: 在两个工业基准数据集上，NexusRCL相比现有最优方法，Top-1准确率（A@1）提升高达49.85%，Top-5平均准确率（A@5）提升32.70%。

Conclusion: 实体层面的异构性自然导致非对称的异构故障传播，其中服务和主机间的跨层交互占主导地位；显式建模这种异构性可显著提升根因定位性能。

Abstract: Microservice root cause localization is fundamentally challenged by the inherent heterogeneity of cloud-native systems, which encompasses diverse observability data and multiple system entities. Existing approaches typically focus on only one aspect of heterogeneity and thus fail to capture its full diagnostic value. In this work, we systematically examine the multifaceted role of heterogeneity within both microservice systems and the RCL process. This analysis motivates a deeper investigation into how entity-level distinctions and their asymmetric dependencies influence fault behavior. Our empirical analysis of two microservice benchmarks reveals that entity-level heterogeneity naturally gives rise to heterogeneous fault propagation, which is highly asymmetric and dominated by cross-layer interactions between services and hosts. In light of this, we propose NexusRCL, a semi-supervised framework that internalizes these propagation patterns by formalizing services and hosts as distinct node types within a heterogeneous graph. This design, coupled with an event-based abstraction mechanism, allows NexusRCL to effectively capture both data level and entity-level heterogeneity while minimizing labeling costs through active learning. Comprehensive evaluations on two industrial benchmark datasets demonstrate NexusRCL's superior performance, achieving improvements of up to 49.85\% in Top-1 accuracy (A@1) and 32.70\% in Average Top-5 accuracy (A@5) compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [63] [What Makes Software Bugs Escape Testing? Evidence from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26672)
*Domenico Cotroneo,Giuseppe De Rosa,Cristina Improta,Benedetta Gaia Varriale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对C/C++和Java系统中超过14,000个缺陷的大规模特征分析，揭示了发布后缺陷主要集中在老旧、频繁修改和高变更率的组件中，修复过程比发布前缺陷更复杂耗时，表明残留缺陷更多源于演化与过程动态而非单纯的代码结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注发布前缺陷，但逃逸测试并在发布后出现的残留缺陷的本质仍不清楚，理解软件缺陷在生产环境中的表现与演化对于提升可靠性至关重要。

Method: 对开源项目中超过14,000个缺陷进行大规模特征分析，采用涵盖复杂性、规模、结构和开发历史等多维软件度量指标，对比C/C++和Java系统中发布前与发布后缺陷的特征差异。

Result: 发布后缺陷集中在老旧、频繁修改和高变更率的组件中，其修复过程比发布前缺陷更长、更复杂。

Conclusion: 残留缺陷更多源于软件演化与过程动态而非代码结构本身，可靠性工程应优先对成熟和复杂的代码区域进行针对性测试。

Abstract: Understanding how software defects manifest and evolve in production environments is critical for improving reliability. While previous research has largely focused on pre-release defects, the nature of residual faults, i.e., those escaping testing and surfacing post-release, remains poorly understood. This paper presents a large-scale characterization of pre- and post-release defects across C/C++ and Java systems, encompassing over 14k defects mined from open-source projects. We employ a broad suite of software metrics to capture diverse code attributes such as complexity, size, structure, and development history.
  Results show that post-release defects are concentrated in older, frequently modified, and high-churn components, typically requiring longer and more complex fixes than pre-release ones. These findings highlight that residual defects arise more from evolutionary and process dynamics than code structure alone, suggesting that reliability engineering should prioritize targeted testing in mature and complex code regions.

</details>


### [64] [Reproducible Automated Program Repair Is Hard -- Experiences With the Defects4J Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26674)
*Adam Krafczyk,Klaus Schmid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自动程序修复（APR）领域广泛使用的Defects4J缺陷数据集进行了系统分析，发现835个缺陷中有180个（21.6%）在APR严格可重复性要求下不适合评估实验，另有59个（7.1%）存在测试套件欠规格问题。作者总结了缺陷数据集在APR评估中的系统化要求、实践经验及量化分析，并实现了一个更严格检查测试套件问题的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动程序修复研究中，基准数据集对评估不同APR方法至关重要。然而，作者在自身工作中发现广泛使用的缺陷数据集存在超越传统缺陷可重复性的显著挑战，因此希望系统化总结这些问题并量化其潜在影响，以引起APR社区的关注。

Method: 以Defects4J数据集（835个缺陷，17个Java开源项目）为研究对象，在APR严格可重复性要求下执行测试套件，分析不适合评估的缺陷比例；通过删除单条语句使测试全部通过的方法检测测试套件欠规格问题；并开发更严格的评估框架以识别测试套件中的不稳定测试等隐性问题。

Result: 180个缺陷（21.6%）在严格APR设置下不适合评估实验；59个缺陷（7.1%）的测试套件明显欠规格，删除单条代码语句即可使所有测试通过，尽管人工补丁并非仅删除代码。

Conclusion: 现有缺陷数据集存在显著的系统性问题，影响APR评估的有效性；需要建立更严格的缺陷数据集要求和评估框架，以确保APR方法比较的可靠性和可重复性。

Abstract: In the research of automated program repair (APR), benchmark datasets consisting of known defects in combination with test suites that indicate the defects are of high importance. They allow for an evidence-based comparison of different APR approaches. In our own work on APR we found significant challenges when working with widely used defect datasets, which go beyond mere repeatability of defects via test cases. We summarize these identified challenges and related lessons learned to bring them to the attention of the APR community and quantify the potential impact of them.
  In particular, we investigate the widely used benchmark Defects4J, which has according to Google Scholar over 1,800 citations. It consists of 835 defects from 17 open-source Java projects; a hand-curated collection of defects, test suites that clearly indicate the defect, and human patches where any unrelated changes are removed. We find that, when executing the test suites with strict requirements for reproducibility in APR settings (beyond merely reproducing the defect via test cases), 180 (21.6 %) of the defects are not suitable for evaluation experiments. Further, we find that an additional 59 (7.1 %) defects have test suites that are obviously under-specified, as deleting a single statement from the code base makes all test cases pass, although the human-written patch does not only delete code.
  Our contributions are: a systematic collection of requirements for defect datasets for APR beyond traditional reproducibility of defects, a description of practical experiences and quantitative analysis of problems with the Defects4J dataset, as well as an implementation of an evaluation framework for APR tools for Java programs. This evaluation framework does stricter checking for indications of inadequate test suites, to avoid otherwise unnoticed problems in the test suite, such as flaky tests.

</details>


### [65] [When Model Editing Meets Service Evolution: A Knowledge-Update Perspective for Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26686)
*Guodong Fan,Cuiyun Gao,Chun Yong Chong,Lu Zhang,Jing Li,Jinglin Zhang,Shizhan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOREC框架，通过模型编辑（定位-编辑范式）融入更新的服务事实，并结合有限自动机约束解码与去重机制，解决服务推荐系统面临的事实过时和服务无效/冗余问题，在真实数据集上Recall@5平均提升25.9%，相比微调方法在演化场景下提升22.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务推荐依赖静态表示和历史数据，难以适应服务生态的动态演化；LLMs虽有上下文理解优势，但面临服务事实过时和无效/冗余服务两大挑战。

Method: 提出EVOREC框架，采用定位-编辑范式的模型编辑方法高效更新服务事实；引入基于有限自动机（FA）的约束解码机制，结合去重策略保证结构和语义有效性，消除重复服务。

Result: 在真实服务数据集上，Recall@5平均提升25.9%；在服务演化场景下，比微调方法提升22.3%。

Conclusion: EVOREC通过模型编辑与有限自动机约束解码，有效应对服务演化，为动态生态系统中的服务推荐提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of software services poses substantial challenges to the design and implementation of effective recommendation systems. Traditional service recommendation approaches often rely on static representations and historical usage data, which are insufficient for adapting to the dynamic and evolving nature of service ecosystems. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential to overcome these limitations by leveraging rich contextual understanding. However, their practical use faces two major challenges: outdated service facts and invalid or redundant services. To address these issues, we propose EVOREC, an evolution-aware framework for service recommendation that leverages model editing in a locate-then-edit paradigm to incorporate updated service facts without costly retraining efficiently. This allows the model to remain aligned with evolving service ecosystems. To address invalid service issues, we introduce a Finite Automata (FA)-based constrained decoding mechanism with deduplication, which enforces structural and semantic validity while eliminating repeated services. Experiments on real-world service datasets demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing baselines, e.g., achieving an average relative improvement of 25.9% in Recall@5. Moreover, under evolving service scenarios, our approach outperforms model fine-tuning approaches by 22.3%, demonstrating strong adaptability to service evolution and providing a practical solution for service recommendation in dynamic ecosystems

</details>


### [66] [What Is the Cost of Energy Monitoring? An Empirical Study on the Overhead of RAPL-Based Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26815)
*Jeremy Diamond,Vincenzo Stoico*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文量化了基于RAPL的高频（1 kHz）能量监测工具的开销，并通过简化工具设计和使用底层指令（如rdmsr）显著降低了时间开销（现有工具为0.25%~46.75%，作者工具接近基线），发现系统调用比rdmsr慢，rdmsr比cpuid慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAPL接口广泛用于软件能耗估计，但工具设计差异和高频轮询会引入额外的测量开销（时间和能量），现有研究缺乏对1 kHz高频监测下开销的量化分析及缓解策略的系统性研究。

Method: 设计两组控制实验：实验一评估七种工具（含作者开发的用户态应用和内核模块）vs 无工具基线，使用六个NAS Benchmark函数量化开销；实验二隔离并计时轮询MSR的关键函数（rdmsr和sys/proc_read）以估算执行延迟。

Result: 现有用户态工具在1 kHz下引入0.25%~46.75%的时间开销；作者的工具通过减少系统调用开销和内联数学开销，使时间开销接近基线；系统调用慢于rdmsr，rdmsr慢于cpuid。

Conclusion: 基于RAPL的能量测量可通过简化工具设计和使用底层指令显著改进；研究为开发高频能量分析工具提供了指导，指出了可能扭曲能量值的情况，并证明了使用特定技术可以更快地访问RAPL值。

Abstract: The Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) interface is widely used to estimate software energy consumption via CPU and DRAM counters, but tool design differences and high-frequency polling can introduce measurement overhead, namely, extra time and energy consumed by the tool itself.This paper quantifies the impact of RAPL-based tools on high-frequency (1 kHz) energy monitoring and investigates mitigation strategies. We conduct two controlled experiments: the first evaluates seven tools, including a user-space application and a kernel module developed by the authors, against a no-tool baseline, using six NAS Benchmark functions to quantify overhead. The second experiment isolates and times key functions for polling Model-Specific Registers (MSRs) (rdmsr and sys/proc_read) to estimate their execution latencies and identify potential slowdowns. The results show that existing user-space tools can introduce substantial time overhead at 1 kHz, whereas our tools significantly reduce system call overhead and inline math overhead. The time overhead of existing tools ranges from 0.25% to 46.75%. Our solutions maintain time overhead levels close to the baseline. We also find that system calls are slower than rdmsr, which in turn is slower than traditionally long-running instructions like cpuid. These findings indicate that RAPL-based energy measurement can be substantially improved by simplifying tool design and employing lower-level instructions to access RAPL values. Our findings provide guidance for practitioners on how to develop high-frequency energy profiling tools, show possible situations that can skew energy values, and demonstrate that access to RAPL values can be faster using specific techniques.

</details>


### [67] [Cognitive Atrophy and Systemic Collapse in AI-Dependent Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26855)
*Frank Ginac*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"认知债务"概念，指出LLM集成到SDLC中导致工程师用被动AI验证替代逻辑推导，侵蚀思维模型，并通过递归训练合成代码造成软件生态同质化，最终引发认知-系统崩溃。以2026年亚马逊 outages 为例，论证了"机械化趋同"导致的系统性脆弱，主张建立人机协作的教学标准以维护认知主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM深度集成到软件开发流程中隐藏的认知-系统性失败，指出当前业界过度依赖AI辅助开发所忽视的结构性风险。

Method: 引入"认知债务"理论框架，通过分析2026年亚马逊服务中断事件作为案例研究，论证"机械化趋同"导致系统脆弱的机制。

Result: 发现递归训练合成代码会均质化全球软件知识库，降低工程所需的多样性；AI验证替代逻辑推导会侵蚀工程师的因果分析心智模型。

Conclusion: 工程领导者应超越基于提示词的开发模式，实施严格的人机协作教学标准，在AI驱动的生产力与认知主权之间取得平衡，以维护长期系统韧性。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into the software development lifecycle (SDLC) masks a critical socio-technical failure: Cognitive-Systemic Collapse. This paper introduces "Epistemological Debt," the hidden carrying cost incurred when engineers substitute logical derivation with passive AI verification. This debt erodes the mental models essential for root-cause analysis, widening the gap between system complexity and human comprehension. Furthermore, recursive training on synthetic code threatens to homogenize the global software reservoir, diminishing the variance required for robust engineering. Using the 2026 Amazon outages as a case study, this research illustrates how "mechanized convergence" leads to systemic fragility. To preserve long-term resilience, engineering leaders must move beyond prompt-based development to implement rigorous human-in-the-loop pedagogical standards. This framework balances AI-driven productivity with the epistemic sovereignty necessary to manage increasingly opaque software ecosystems.

</details>


### [68] [ClassEval-Pro: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Class-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26923)
*Yeheng Chen,Chaoxiang Xie,Yuling Shi,Wenhao Zeng,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ClassEval-Pro 是一个包含300个类级编程任务的基准测试，通过自动化三阶段流程构建，覆盖11个领域。评估显示最强模型Pass@1仅45.6%，逻辑错误(56.2%)和依赖错误(38.0%)是主要瓶颈，跨方法协调是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试要么局限于孤立函数级代码合成，要么依赖人工构建的类级任务，后者成本高昂且易受数据污染。函数级与仓库级代码修改之间存在空白——即根据规范构建完整、内部结构化的类的组合式代码创建能力尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 自动化三阶段流程：(1) 复杂度增强；(2) 跨领域类组合；(3) 集成2025年1月后的真实GitHub代码。每个任务通过LLM Judge Ensemble验证，并通过覆盖率超90%的测试套件。评估5个前沿LLMs在5种生成策略下的表现。

Result: 最佳模型Pass@1仅45.6%，最强与最弱模型间差距17.7个百分点。自上而下策略对弱模型提升达9.4个百分点，而组合式生成策略低至1.3%。500个人工标注失败案例中，逻辑错误占56.2%，依赖错误占38.0%。

Conclusion: 跨方法协调是当前LLM在类级代码合成中的核心瓶颈，策略选择与模型能力存在强交互作用，类级组合式代码创建仍是重大挑战。

Abstract: LLMs have achieved strong results on both function-level code synthesis and repository-level code modification, yet a capability that falls between these two extremes -- compositional code creation, i.e., building a complete, internally structured class from a specification -- remains underserved. Current evaluations are either confined to isolated functions or rely on manually curated class-level tasks that are expensive to scale and increasingly susceptible to data contamination. We introduce ClassEval-Pro, a benchmark of 300 class-level tasks spanning 11 domains, constructed through an automated three-stage pipeline that combines complexity enhancement, cross-domain class composition, and integration of real-world GitHub code contributed after January 2025. Every task is validated by an LLM Judge Ensemble and must pass test suites with over 90% line coverage. We evaluate five frontier LLMs under five generation strategies. The best model achieves only 45.6% class-level Pass@1, with a 17.7-point gap between the strongest and weakest models, confirming the benchmark's discriminative power. Strategy choice strongly interacts with model capability: structured approaches such as bottom-up improve weaker models by up to 9.4 percentage points, while compositional generation collapses to as low as 1.3%. Error analysis over 500 manually annotated failures reveals that logic errors (56.2%) and dependency errors (38.0%) dominate, identifying cross-method coordination as the core bottleneck.

</details>
