### [69] [On the Centralization of Governance Power in Decentralized Autonomous Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25959)
*Vabuk Pahari,Balakrishnan Chandrasekaran,Johnnatan Messias,Krishna P. Gummadi,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究分析了48个活跃的以太坊DAO，发现代币注册、质押和委托这三种治理机制系统性地强化了投票权集中化，揭示了DAO治理设计与其去中心化承诺之间的内在矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DAO的设计理念强调去中心化和自治，但经验证据表明实践中治理往往高度中心化。作者希望探究这种中心化现象背后的系统性原因。

Method: 对48个部署在以太坊上、具有大量资本且公开活跃使用的DAO进行治理设计与实现的研究，识别并分析三种关键治理机制（代币注册、质押、委托）对投票权集中化的影响。

Result: 发现代币注册、质押和委托这三种原本旨在提升安全性或参与度的治理机制，反而系统性强化了投票权的集中化，且这种中心化趋势并非个别DAO的特例，而是DAO治理机制本身的系统性特征。

Conclusion: 通过阐明治理设计与投票集中化之间的关系，该研究推进了对DAO治理结构的理解，并揭示了去中心化、安全性和可用性之间存在的固有权衡。

Abstract: A decentralized autonomous organization (DAO) is a governing entity that empowers its stakeholders (i.e., users who hold one or more of its tokens) to manage blockchain-based protocols (i.e., smart contracts) collaboratively. The governance of a DAO is explicitly encoded in the DAO's governance contract, which defines how stakeholders participate in governance and how much influence (or voting power) they have in any decision. While decentralization and autonomy are the fundamental tenets of a DAO's design, empirical evidence suggests that in practice governance is often highly centralized. In this work, we study the designs and implementations of 48 public and actively used DAOs, with substantially large capital, deployed on Ethereum. We identify how three key governance mechanisms--token registration, staking, and delegation--originally introduced to improve security or participation, contribute to the concentration of voting power. Unlike prior work on centralization of voting power in specific DAOs, our findings reveal that these governance mechanisms of DAOs themselves systematically reinforce centralization. By elucidating the relationship between governance design and voting centralization, this work advances the understanding of DAO governance structures and highlights the inherent trade-offs between decentralization, security, and usability of DAOs.

</details>


### [70] [A Quantitative Confirmation of the Currier Language Distinction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25979)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过Beta-Binomial混合模型对伏尼契手稿中字符对替换比率进行定量分析，验证了Currier A/B语言分类的结构真实性，无监督方法恢复分类的ARI为0.383，有监督分类准确率达89.2%，并发现字符对分离出三种功能区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Currier提出的伏尼契手稿A/B语言区分是否反映文本的真实结构属性，为解读这一未解手稿提供定量依据。

Method: 使用Beta-Binomial混合模型对原始字符计数进行无监督聚类（无标签），以及有监督分类器训练，通过字符对替换比率分析来区分A/B类。

Result: 无监督恢复Currier分类的调整兰德指数(ARI)为0.383；有监督分类器对测试页的预测准确率达89.2%；字符对分离出三种功能区间。

Conclusion: Currier的A/B语言区分确实反映了伏尼契手稿的结构属性，字符对的三种功能区间对任何关于伏尼契书写系统的理论构成约束条件。

Abstract: We present a quantitative analysis of character-pair substitution ratios in the Voynich manuscript, testing whether Currier's A/B language distinction (1976) reflects a genuine structural property of the text. A Beta-Binomial mixture model applied to raw character counts without access to labels recovers the Currier split with ARI = 0.383. A supervised Beta-Binomial classifier trained on a subset of folios predicts the A/B identity of held-out folios at 89.2% accuracy. The character pairs separate into three functional regimes that constrain any theory of the Voynich writing system.

</details>


### [71] [Large Language Models as Explainable Cyberattack Detectors for Energy Industrial Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26079)
*Weiyi Kong,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Amr Youssef,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究使用现成的大语言模型（LLM）作为Modbus流量的互补性人机协同入侵检测层，将协议字段转化为紧凑token字符串，LLM输出正常/关键告警并生成可审计记录，在基准测试上取得与强监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ICS入侵检测主要依赖监督式检测器，缺乏可审计性；需要一种既能保持准确性又支持操作员审查的互补检测方案。

Method: 将Modbus通信实例的离散协议字段转化为紧凑token字符串，通过提示配置的LLM进行二分类（正常/关键），同时生成基于token的简洁事件记录；采用干预式诊断（充分性/必要性测试）评估审计记录质量。

Result: 在两个公开ICS Modbus数据集上，LLM流水线取得了与强监督基线相当的高预测性能，且无需特定任务权重更新；干预测试表明引用的token对模型预测具有决策相关性。

Conclusion: 现成LLM可作为ICS入侵检测中有效的互补性人机协同层，提供可审计的告警记录，但该记录应被视为审计信号而非完整的人为可解释性说明。

Abstract: In modern energy systems, industrial control systems (ICS) and power-system SCADA require intrusion detection that is not only accurate but also auditable by operators. The ICS intrusion-detection landscape is currently dominated by established supervised detectors. In this paper, we study whether an off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) can serve as a complementary, human-in-the-loop layer for Modbus traffic. We cast this as a binary network-side normal/critical decision task on two public ICS Modbus datasets, collapsing attack periods and other safety-critical behaviors into a single critical class. Each Modbus communication instance is converted into a compact token string derived from discretized protocol fields, and a prompt-configured LLM produces a normal/critical alert together with a concise, token-grounded incident record for analyst review. Under matched event information and shared evaluation splits, the resulting LLM-based triage pipeline achieves high predictive performance on both benchmarks and is broadly comparable to strong supervised baselines, while requiring no task-specific weight updates. To assess the audit record, we apply intervention-based diagnostics, including sufficiency- and necessity-style tests, which provide evidence that the cited tokens are often decision-relevant to the model's own prediction. These records are intended as audit signals rather than full human-grounded explanations.

</details>


### [72] [GenDetect: Generalizing Reactive Detection for Resilience Against Imitative DeFi Attack Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26094)
*Bowen Cai,Weiheng Bai,Youshui Lu,Haoran Xu,Yuannan Yang,Yajin Zhou,Kangjie Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenDetect系统，利用DeFi协议开源特性实现攻击语义抽象与交易逻辑匹配，能在初始攻击发生后快速生成通用检测规则，准确率98%，假阳性率1%，并发现56个此前未披露的攻击事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi攻击具有模仿性级联传播特征（超69%攻击与早期事件行为高度相似），现有反应式检测依赖手动验证和痕迹分析，速度慢且无法阻止后续模仿攻击。

Method: 将问题分解为两个挑战：①抽象多样化模糊函数签名的语义；②在噪声和规避痕迹中匹配交易逻辑。利用两个洞见：i) DeFi协议开源特性实现高保真函数签名语义分类；ii) 合约标签通过过滤无关调用和分类攻击意图来隔离核心逻辑。

Result: GenDetect实现ACC 98%、FPR 1%、FNR 3%，并发现过去三年中56个此前未披露的攻击。

Conclusion: GenDetect能够将单个已观测攻击快速抽象为可操作、可泛化的检测规则，有效阻断模仿性攻击级联传播。

Abstract: As blockchain ecosystems grow, financially motivated attackers increasingly exploit decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols, causing frequent and severe losses. Unlike conventional cyberattacks, DeFi exploits propagate rapidly due to the transparent and composable nature of smart contracts. We identify a critical pattern, Imitative Attack Cascade: an initial successful exploit is quickly followed by mimicking transactions that reuse attack logic with minor modifications or parameter changes. Our empirical analysis shows that over 69% of DeFi attacks exhibit strong behavioral similarity to earlier incidents, often within hours or days of the initial attack.
  This exposes a fundamental limitation in current reactive detection. Initial attacks are typically flagged via heuristic alerts (Tornado Cash traces, anomalous nonce usage, exploiter labels), but turning these signals into detection rules requires manual validation and handcrafted trace analysis -- a labor-intensive, slow process that leaves follow-up attacks to spread. Our goal is to ensure that once an attack has been observed, even a single instance, it can be rapidly abstracted into an actionable, generalizable detection rule.
  We decompose the problem into two challenges: (I) abstracting the semantics of diverse, obscure function signatures, and (II) matching transaction logic in noisy, evasive traces. We leverage two insights: (i) the open-source nature of most DeFi protocols enables high-fidelity semantic classification of function signatures; (ii) contract labels isolate essential logic by filtering irrelevant calls and classifying attack intent. Building on these, we develop GenDetect, which achieves ACC 98%, FPR 1%, FNR 3% and discovers 56 previously unrevealed attacks from the past three years. Source code and dataset: https://github.com/NobodyIsAnonymous/GenDetect_ICSE2026

</details>


### [73] [VulStyle: A Multi-Modal Pre-Training for Code Stylometry-Augmented Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26313)
*Chidera Biringa,Ajmal Abbas,Vishnu Selvaraj,Gokhan Kul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VulStyle是一个多模态软件漏洞检测模型，通过联合编码函数级源代码、非终端AST节点和代码风格特征，在BigVul和VulDeePecker上取得SOTA性能，F1提升4-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码表示方法主要依赖token级模型或完整AST树，要么忽略了反映危险编程实践的风格线索，要么面临高结构开销。本文旨在通过非终端AST节点降低输入复杂度同时保留语义层次，并集成代码风格特征作为辅助信号来改进漏洞检测。

Method: 1) 仅选择非终端AST节点以减少输入复杂度并保留语义层次；2) 集成句法和词法级别的CStyle特征作为辅助漏洞信号；3) 在7种编程语言的490万函数上进行掩码语言建模预训练；4) 在Devign、BigVul、DiverseVul、REVEAL、VulDeePecker五个基准数据集上微调。

Result: 在BigVul和VulDeePecker上达到SOTA性能，F1比强Transformer基线提升4-48%；在所有基准测试中取得竞争性或最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 非终端AST节点和代码风格特征对漏洞检测有正向贡献，消融实验、错误案例分析和威胁模型进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We present VulStyle, a multi-modal software vulnerability detection model that jointly encodes function-level source code, non-terminal Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) structure, and code stylometry (CStyle) features. Prior work in code representation primarily leverages token-level models or full AST trees, often missing stylistic cues indicative of risky programming practices, or incurring high structural overhead. Our approach selects only non-terminal AST nodes, reducing input complexity while preserving semantic hierarchy, and integrates syntactic and lexical CStyle features as auxiliary vulnerability signals.
  VulStyle is pre-trained using masked language modeling on 4.9M functions across seven programming languages, and fine-tuned across five benchmark datasets: Devign, BigVul, DiverseVul, REVEAL, and VulDeePecker. VulStyle achieves state-of-the-art performance on BigVul and VulDeePecker, improving F1 by 4-48% over strong transformer baselines, and attains competitive or best-average performance across all benchmarks. We contribute an ablation study isolating the effect of CStyle and AST structure, error case analysis, and a threat model situating the detection task in attacker-realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [74] [Differentially Private Contrastive Learning via Bounding Group-level Contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26467)
*Kecen Li,Chen Gong,Zinan Lin,Tianhao Wang,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-GCL框架，通过分组限制梯度依赖（将批次划分为小分组、限制负样本为组内样本）并结合组内增广技术，在不增加隐私成本的前提下提升负样本多样性，在8个数据集上实现了差分隐私对比学习的最新最优性能，图像分类准确率提升5.6%，图文检索准确率提升20.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私对比学习方法因标准对比学习目标中样本间过度依赖（每个样本的梯度依赖批次中所有其他样本），导致DP噪声被放大，造成严重的效用退化。因此需要显式降低这种内在的样本间依赖关系。

Method: 提出DP-GCL框架：1）将每个批次划分为小的不相交分组，限制负样本为组内样本，从而局部化梯度影响并降低灵敏度；2）引入组内增广技术，在不增加隐私成本的前提下生成额外负视图以弥补负样本多样性损失。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验表明，DP-GCL在单模态和多模态对比学习下均达到最先进水平：图像分类准确率提升5.6%，图像-文本检索准确率提升20.1%。

Conclusion: 通过结构性限制组级梯度贡献，DP-GCL有效解决了DP对比学习中样本间依赖导致的隐私噪声放大问题，为在实用隐私预算下学习通用表征提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) contrastive learning aims to learn general-purpose representations from sensitive data, alleviating the privacy leakage concerns of organizations deploying or sharing embedding models trained on private user content. However, existing approaches suffer from severe utility degradation due to the over-strong inter-sample dependency inherent in standard contrastive objectives, where each sample's gradient depends on all other samples in the batch, amplifying the impact of DP noise. In this work, we argue that effective DP contrastive learning requires explicitly reducing such intrinsic inter-sample reliance. To this end, we propose DP-GCL, a principled DP contrastive learning framework that structurally limits gradient dependency through bounding group-level contribution. DP-GCL partitions each batch into small, disjoint groups and restricts available negative samples to within-group samples, thereby localizing gradient influence and reducing sensitivity. To counteract the resulting loss of negative sample diversity, we further introduce intra-group augmentation, which generates additional negative views without increasing privacy cost. Extensive experiments across eight datasets demonstrate that DP-GCL consistently advances the state of the art in both uni-modal and multi-modal contrastive learning under practical privacy budgets: it improves image classification accuracy by 5.6% and image-text retrieval accuracy by 20.1% over existing DP contrastive methods.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Code Reasoning: A Specification-Anchored Audit Framework for Expert-Augmented Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26495)
*Masato Kamba,Hirotake Murakami,Akiyoshi Sannai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SPECA是一个规范锚定的安全审计框架，通过从自然语言规范中提取显式类型化安全属性，并基于结构化证明尝试推理来审计实现。在多个基准测试中，SPECA能够发现代码驱动审计无法检测的漏洞，所有误报均可追溯到三种可解释的根因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全审计工具基于代码库本地模式推理漏洞，但对于受自然语言规范约束的系统（如协议栈、共识实现、密码库），规范要求本身而非代码写法引入的漏洞无法被代码级方法检测，且其误报难以系统性诊断。

Method: SPECA框架首先从自然语言规范中推导出显式的类型化安全属性，然后针对每个属性通过结构化证明尝试推理来审计实现。这产生三种代码驱动审计不具备的能力：规范依赖的检测、共享属性词汇表下的跨实现对比、以及可分解为管道阶段可追溯根因的误报。

Result: 在Sherlock Ethereum Fusaka审计竞赛（366份提交，10个实现）中，SPECA恢复了所有15个范围内漏洞并独立发现4个经开发者确认的bug。在RepoAudit C/C++基准测试（15个项目）中，SPECA达到最高发布精度88.9%，同时发现12个超出已建立真值的候选bug，其中2个经上游维护者确认。多模型分析表明更强模型在属性范围内更忠实审计，将检测瓶颈从模型推理转移到属性生成质量。

Conclusion: SPECA通过规范锚定的方法成功弥补了代码驱动审计在规范约束系统中的盲区，所有误报均可追溯至三种可操作的根因（信任边界误解、代码阅读错误、规范解读错误），为审计工具改进提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Security-critical software is routinely audited by tools that reason about vulnerabilities as repository-local code patterns. Yet specification-governed systems -- protocol stacks, consensus implementations, cryptographic libraries -- are constrained by invariants and correctness conditions defined in natural-language specifications. When a vulnerability arises from what the specification requires rather than how code is written, code-level approaches lack the representational vocabulary to detect it, and their false positives resist systematic diagnosis.
  We present SPECA, a specification-anchored security audit framework that derives explicit, typed security properties from natural-language specifications and audits implementations through structured proof-attempt reasoning grounded in each property. The framework yields three capabilities absent from code-driven auditing: spec-dependent detections, controlled cross-implementation comparison under a shared property vocabulary, and false positives that decompose into interpretable, pipeline-phase-traceable root causes.
  On the Sherlock Ethereum Fusaka Audit Contest (366 submissions, 10 implementations), SPECA recovers all 15 in-scope vulnerabilities and independently discovers 4 bugs confirmed by developer fix commits. On the RepoAudit C/C++ benchmark (15 projects), SPECA matches the best published precision (88.9\%) while surfacing 12 candidate bugs beyond the established ground truth, two confirmed by upstream maintainers. A multi-model analysis reveals that more capable models audit more faithfully within property scope, shifting the detection bottleneck from model reasoning to property generation quality. All false positives trace to three recurring root causes -- trust boundary misunderstanding, code reading errors, and specification misinterpretation -- each yielding actionable improvement targets.

</details>


### [76] [Quantamination: Dynamic Quantization Leaks Your Data Across the Batch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26505)
*Hanna Foerster,Ilia Shumailov,Cheng Zhang,Yiren Zhao,Jamie Hayes,Robert Mullins*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态量化在机器学习服务框架中存在一种名为"Quantamination"的数据泄露漏洞：攻击者可利用动态量化在运行时根据输入数据调整参数的特性，通过侧信道从同一批次中窃取其他用户的敏感数据。至少4个主流ML框架的默认或可选配置存在此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态量化因能显著降低内存和计算开销、提升推理效率且精度损失小，被主流ML框架广泛推荐作为模型服务优化的首选方案。但在实际部署中，动态量化在批处理场景下可能产生安全隐患，这种安全风险此前未被充分关注和研究。

Method: 作者通过分析动态量化机制在运行时对张量进行适应性参数调整的过程，揭示了批次内不同输入数据之间因量化参数共享或依赖而产生的侧信道信息泄露途径，并对至少4个主流ML框架进行了实证验证。

Result: 发现至少4个主流ML框架（包括ML编译器和推理引擎）在默认或可选配置下存在跨批次边界的数据泄露风险。攻击者理论上可以部分甚至完全恢复同一批次中其他用户的输入数据。

Conclusion: 动态量化在批处理场景下存在严重隐私风险，当实现或配置不当时，会创建侧信道导致跨用户数据泄露，这对现有ML服务框架构成了严重的安全威胁，需要重新审视动态量化的安全部署策略。

Abstract: Dynamic quantization emerged as a practical approach to increase the utilization and efficiency of the machine learning serving flow. Unlike static quantization, which applies quantization offline, dynamic quantization operates on tensors at run-time, adapting its parameters to the actual input data. Today's mainstream machine learning frameworks, including ML compilers and inference engines, frequently recommend dynamic quantization as an initial step for optimizing model serving. This is because dynamic quantization can significantly reduce memory usage and computational load, leading to faster token generation and improved model serving efficiency without substantial loss in model accuracy. In this paper, we reveal a critical vulnerability in dynamic quantization: an adversary can exploit such quantization strategy to steal sensitive user data placed in the same batch as the adversary's input. Our analysis demonstrates that dynamic quantization, when improperly implemented or configured, can create side channels that expose information about other inputs within the same batch. We call this phenomenon Quantamination, describing contamination from quantization. Specifically, we show that at least 4 of the most popular ML frameworks in use today either default to or can use configurations that leak data across the batch boundary. This data leakage, in theory, allows attackers to partially or even fully recover other users' batched input data, representing a serious privacy risk for existing ML serving frameworks.

</details>


### [77] [Tatemae: Detecting Alignment Faking via Tool Selection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26511)
*Matteo Leonesi,Francesco Belardinelli,Flavio Corradini,Marco Piangerelli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过可观察的工具选择行为来检测大语言模型对齐伪装（AF）的框架，在108个企业IT场景中评估了六个前沿模型，发现平均检测率为3.5%-23.7%，且漏洞分布与领域和压力类型相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法局限于对话场景，主要依赖思维链（CoT）分析，当策略性推理痕迹缺失或不忠实时无法区分欺骗与能力失败，因此需要更可靠的检测形式化方法。

Method: 将AF形式化为复合行为事件，通过可观察的工具选择进行检测：模型在无监控时选择安全工具，但在监控下选择不安全工具（奖励有用性高于安全性），同时其推理仍承认安全选择。在108个企业IT场景（安全、隐私、完整性领域，腐败与破坏压力）上评估六个前沿LLM。

Result: 平均AF检测率在3.5%-23.7%之间，漏洞分布因领域和压力类型而异。

Conclusion: 模型对AF的易感性更多反映训练方法而非能力本身。

Abstract: Alignment faking (AF) occurs when an LLM strategically complies with training objectives to avoid value modification, reverting to prior preferences once monitoring is lifted. Current detection methods focus on conversational settings and rely primarily on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) analysis, which provides a reliable signal when strategic reasoning surfaces, but cannot distinguish deception from capability failures if traces are absent or unfaithful. We formalize AF as a composite behavioural event and detect it through observable tool selection, where the LLM selects the safe tool when unmonitored, but switches to the unsafe tool under monitoring that rewards helpfulness over safety, while its reasoning still acknowledges the safe choice. We release a dataset of 108 enterprise IT scenarios spanning Security, Privacy, and Integrity domains under Corruption and Sabotage pressures. Evaluating six frontier LLMs across five independent runs, we find mean AF detection rates between 3.5% and 23.7%, with vulnerability profiles varying by domain and pressure type. These results suggest that susceptibility reflects training methodology rather than capability alone.

</details>


### [78] [PRAG End-to-End Privacy-Preserving Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26525)
*Zhijun Li,Minghui Xu,Huayi Qi,Wenxuan Yu,Tingchuang Zhang,Qiao Zhang,GuangYong Shang,Zhen Ma,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRAG是一个端到端的隐私保护检索增强生成系统，通过双模式架构（非交互式PRAG-I和交互式PRAG-II）及操作误差估计机制，在实现文档和查询机密性的同时保持可扩展性和检索质量，在大规模数据集上达到72.45%-74.45%的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖云环境，暴露敏感数据隐私风险；已有隐私保护方案因噪声注入牺牲检索质量或仅提供部分加密，亟需一种既保证端到端机密性又不牺牲云RAG可扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 提出PRAG系统，包含双模式架构：PRAG-I使用同态友好近似实现低延迟检索（非交互式），PRAG-II借助客户端协助匹配非私有RAG精度（交互式）；引入操作误差估计（OEE）机制稳定同态噪声下的排序。

Result: 在大规模数据集上召回率72.45%-74.45%，具备实际检索延迟，对图重构攻击具有强鲁棒性，同时保持端到端机密性。

Conclusion: 证实了大规模安全、高性能RAG的可行性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge, but its reliance on cloud environments exposes sensitive data to privacy risks. Existing privacy-preserving solutions often sacrifice retrieval quality due to noise injection or only provide partial encryption. We propose PRAG, an end-to-end privacy-preserving RAG system that achieves end-to-end confidentiality for both documents and queries without sacrificing the scalability of cloud-hosted RAG. PRAG features a dual-mode architecture: a non-interactive PRAG-I utilizes homomorphic-friendly approximations for low-latency retrieval, while an interactive PRAG-II leverages client assistance to match the accuracy of non-private RAG. To ensure robust semantic ordering, we introduce Operation-Error Estimation (OEE), a mechanism that stabilizes ranking against homomorphic noise. Experiments on large-scale datasets demonstrate that PRAG achieves competitive recall (72.45%-74.45%), practical retrieval latency, and strong resilience against graph reconstruction attacks while maintaining end-to-end confidentiality. This work confirms the feasibility of secure, high-performance RAG at scale.

</details>


### [79] [Preventing Distinguishability between Multiplication and Squaring Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26536)
*Alkistis Aikaterini Sigourou,Zoya Dyka,Peter Langendoerfer,Ievgen Kabin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了椭圆曲线密码系统中标量乘法kP在简单侧信道分析下的脆弱性，指出即使采用原子模式策略，由于域乘法器在处理不同或相同操作数时的能量消耗差异，仍存在信息泄漏。研究实现了两种缓解技术：数据重定向和总线重载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线密码系统中的标量乘法kP是核心运算，常受到侧信道分析攻击。尽管已有基于原子模式的安全增强策略，但二进制kP算法仍因域乘法器在处理两个不同操作数与两个相同操作数时的能量消耗差异而容易受到简单SCA攻击。

Method: 实现并分析了两种缓解技术：一种基于数据重定向，另一种专注于总线重载。

Result: 域乘法器在处理不同操作数与相同操作数时存在能量消耗差异，导致二进制kP算法即使采用原子模式策略仍易受简单SCA攻击，且该漏洞独立于所使用的乘法方法。

Conclusion: 两种缓解技术（数据重定向和总线重载）被用于应对能量消耗变化带来的侧信道泄露风险。

Abstract: Scalar multiplication kP is a critical operation in Elliptic Curve Cryptosystems (ECC), often targeted by Side-Channel Analysis (SCA). Despite strategies based on atomic patterns to enhance security, the binary kP algorithms remain susceptible to simple SCA due to energy consumption variations in field multipliers during passing two different or two identical operands. This vulnerability arises independent of the multiplication method used. We implemented and analysed two mitigation techniques: one involving data redirection and another focusing on bus reloading.

</details>


### [80] [Catching the Fly: Practical Challenges in Making Blockchain FlyClient Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26736)
*Pericle Perazzo,Dario Capecchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将轻量级区块链验证协议FlyClient从实验阶段推进到生产就绪状态，解决了三个关键技术挑战：引入替代性对手模型以实现经济参数化并节省证明空间，首次在Zcash生产区块链上实现FlyClient证明者并评估其性能，以及提出两种无需共识变更即可最小化证明大小的优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FlyClient协议虽具轻量级区块链验证潜力，但仍处于实验阶段，缺乏实际部署和多样化条件下的性能评估，需要弥合理论到实际部署的差距。

Method: (i) 形式化定义替代性对手模型，引入经济参数化并减少证明空间；(ii) 在Zcash生产区块链上实现首个实用FlyClient证明者，评估不同配置下的性能；(iii) 提出两种优化方案以最小化FlyClient证明大小（其中第一种无需共识变更）。

Result: 成功将FlyClient推进至生产就绪状态，实现了首个生产级区块链上的证明者实现，并提出了有效的证明大小优化方案。

Conclusion: FlyClient协议已具备实际部署条件，所提出的对手模型和优化方案使其在资源受限环境（如移动钱包、物联网设备和跨链桥）中的应用更加可行。

Abstract: FlyClient is a lightweight blockchain verification protocol that enables proof-of-work validation using minimal data, making it ideal for resource-constrained environments like mobile wallets, Internet-of-Things devices or cross-chain bridges implemented with smart contracts. Despite its strong potential for enabling lightweight blockchain verification, FlyClient protocol is still in the experimental stages, with limited real-world deployments and performance evaluations under diverse conditions. In this paper we bridge the gap between theory and deployment, by addressing several technical challenges to advance FlyClient to a production-ready solution. Namely, our contribution is three-fold: (i) we formally introduce an adversary model alternative to the original FlyClient one, that allows us to parametrize a verifier under a concrete economic interpretation, while also saving some proof space; (ii) we provide the first practical FlyClient prover implementation for a production blockchain (Zcash), and we estimate its performance under different configurations; (iii) we introduce and evaluate two optimizations that minimize the size of FlyClient proofs, the first of which does not require any consensus change.

</details>

### [81] [Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25982)
*Marta Ziosi,Miro Plueckebaum,Stephen Casper,Henry Papadatos,Ze Shen Chin,Peter Slattery,James Gealy,Tim G. J. Rudner,Brian Tse,Ariel Gil,Patricia Paskov,Maximilian Negele,Rokas Gipiškis,Nada Madkour,Vera Lummis,Rupal Jain,Luise Eder,Kristina Fort,Malou C. van Draanen Glismann,Inès Belhadj,Amin Oueslati,Anna K. Wisakanto,Richard Mallah,Koen Holtman,Ranj Zuhdi,Daniel S. Schiff,Jessica Newman,Malcolm Murray,Robert Trager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了前沿AI风险管理中的开放性问题，将问题分为三类：科学/技术共识缺失、与现有风险管理框架不匹配、以及在已有共识下的实施不足，并识别了各问题对应的关键行动主体（开发者、部署者、监管者等）。论文不提出具体解决方案，而是提供问题导向的议程设置参考文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI既放大了现有风险，又引入了全新的挑战。快速的技术变革导致缺乏稳定的科学共识，且新兴的安全实践往往与已有风险管理框架不匹配甚至相冲突，亟需系统性地梳理和明确这些问题。

Method: 采用问题导向的方法，通过结构化文献综述，对风险管理过程的每个阶段（风险规划、识别、分析、评估、缓解）进行审查，识别未解决的挑战及最适合应对的参与者。同时将开放性问题按三类进行分类。

Result: 绘制了前沿AI风险管理中的开放性问题地图，识别了不同问题类型对应的关键行动主体，并建立了在线实时更新的知识库。论文本身不提供具体解决方案，而是作为议程设置的参考文档。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地揭示前沿AI风险管理中的开放性问题并明确责任主体，有助于凝聚共识、减少重复工作、指导未来的研究和治理工作，推动建立稳健且有意义的风险管理共识。

Abstract: Frontier AI both amplifies existing risks and introduces qualitatively novel challenges. Not only is there a notable lack of stable scientific consensus resulting from the rapid pace of technological change, but emerging frontier AI safety practices are often misaligned with, or may undermine, established risk management frameworks. To address these challenges, we systematically surface open problems in frontier AI risk management. Adopting a problem-oriented approach, we examine each stage of the risk management process - risk planning, identification, analysis, evaluation, and mitigation - through a structured review of the literature, identifying unresolved challenges and the actors best positioned to address them. Recognising that different types of open problems call for different responses, we classify open problems according to whether they reflect (a) a lack of scientific or technical consensus, (b) misalignment with, or challenges to, established risk management frameworks, or (c) shortcomings in implementation despite apparent consensus and alignment. By mapping these open problems and identifying the actors best positioned to address them - including developers, deployers, regulators, standards bodies, researchers, and third-party evaluators - this work aims to clarify where progress is needed to enable robust and meaningful consensus on frontier AI risk management.The paper does not propose specific solutions; instead, it provides a problem-oriented, agenda-setting reference document, complemented by a living online repository, intended to support coordination, reduce duplication, and guide future research and governance efforts.

</details>


### [82] [Causal Learning with Neural Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26919)
*Evangelia Kopadi,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIRECT机制，证明神经集群可通过局部可塑性学习变量间的因果方向，利用突触权重不对称性和功能传播重叠实现方向性编码，在已知结构监督下达到完美结构恢复，为生物合理动力学与形式化因果模型之间提供了可审计的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经集群已被证明是分类、解析和规划的计算基础，但尚未展示其能否内化因果方向性。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索神经集群是否能够学习变量间的因果影响方向。

Method: 提出DIRECT（方向性边耦合/训练）机制，利用神经集群的投射、局部可塑性控制和稀疏获胜者选择等固有操作，在自适应增益调度下共激活源和目标集群以内化有向关系。采用双重读出验证策略：(i) 突触强度不对称性（测量正向与反向链接间的权重差距）和 (ii) 功能传播重叠（量化方向性信号流的可靠性）。

Result: 在多个领域的已知结构监督设置下，该框架实现了完美的结构恢复。突触权重不对称性和功能传播重叠两种验证指标均有效量化了因果方向的学习效果。

Conclusion: 神经集群可作为生物合理动力学与形式化因果模型之间的可审计桥梁，提供一种"可解释-by-design"的框架，其中因果主张可追溯到特定的神经获胜者和突触不对称性。与基于反向传播的方法不同，DIRECT完全依赖局部可塑性，使得因果主张在机制层面可审计。

Abstract: Can Neural Assemblies -- groups of neurons that fire together and strengthen through co-activation -- learn the direction of causal influence between variables? While established as a computationally general substrate for classification, parsing, and planning, neural assemblies have not yet been shown to internalize causal directionality. We demonstrate that the inherent operations of neural assemblies -- projection, local plasticity control, and sparse winner selection -- are sufficient for directional learning. We introduce DIRECT (DIRectional Edge Coupling/Training), a mechanism that co-activates source and target assemblies under an adaptive gain schedule to internalize directed relations. Unlike backpropagation-based methods, DIRECT relies solely on local plasticity, making the resulting causal claims auditable at the mechanism level. Our findings are verified through a dual-readout validation strategy: (i) synaptic-strength asymmetry, measuring the emergent weight gap between forward and reverse links, and (ii) functional propagation overlap, quantifying the reliability of directional signal flow. Across multiple domains, the framework achieves perfect structural recovery under a supervised, known-structure setting. These results establish neural assemblies as an auditable bridge between biologically plausible dynamics and formal causal models, offering an "explainable by design" framework where causal claims are traceable to specific neural winners and synaptic asymmetries.

</details>


### [83] [A Multimodal and Explainable Machine Learning Approach to Diagnosing Multi-Class Ejection Fraction from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25942)
*Catherine Ning,Yu Ma,Cindy Beini Wang,Sean McMahon,Joseph Radojevic,Steven Zweibel,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种结合12导联心电图时序特征与结构化电子健康记录变量的多模态机器学习框架，用于将左心室射血分数（LVEF）分为四个临床分层（正常>50%、轻度降低40-50%、中度降低30-40%、重度降低<30%）。在36,784对心电图-超声心动图数据上训练的XGBoost模型，在四分类任务中达到了0.82-0.95的AUROC，优于单模态基线，并在时间泛化验证中保持稳定性能，为资源有限环境下的LVEF筛查与分诊提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LVEF评估依赖超声心动图，在基层医疗和资源受限环境中可及性有限，亟需一种更便捷、低成本的筛查方法。

Method: 使用XGBoost模型，融合12导联心电图时序工程特征与结构化EHR变量，基于SHAP进行特征重要性解释，在Hartford HealthCare的30,952名门诊患者的36,784对ECG-超声心动图数据上训练，并在后续19,966份ECG上评估时间泛化性。

Result: 多模态模型在one-vs-rest分类中AUROC分别为：重度降低0.95、中度降低0.92、轻度降低0.82、正常0.91，均优于仅使用ECG或仅使用EHR的基线模型，且在时间验证中保持性能。

Conclusion: 基于ECG的多模态LVEF分层可作为实用的筛查与分诊辅助工具，优先安排资源受限环境中的确诊性影像检查。

Abstract: Left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) assessment depends on echocardiography, limiting access in primary care and resource-constrained settings. We developed a multimodal machine-learning framework that combines engineered 12-lead ECG timeseries features with structured EHR variables to classify LVEF into four clinically used strata: normal (>50%), mildly reduced (40-50%), moderately reduced (30-40%), and severely reduced (<30%). To support model explainability, we identified the most influential ECG and EHR features via SHAP attributions. Using retrospective data from Hartford HealthCare, we trained XGBoost models on 36,784 ECG-echocardiogram pairs from 30,952 outpatients and evaluated temporal generalizability on 19,966 ECGs from a subsequent period. The multimodal model achieved one-vs-rest AUROCs of 0.95 (severe), 0.92 (moderate), 0.82 (mild), and 0.91 (normal), outperforming ECG-only and EHR-only baselines, and maintained performance under temporal validation. This work supports ECG-based, multimodal LVEF stratification as a practical screening and triage aid to prioritize confirmatory imaging where resources are limited.

</details>


### [84] [Correcting Performance Estimation Bias in Imbalanced Classification with Minority Subconcepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26024)
*Taylor Maxson,Roberto Corizzo,Yaning Wu,Nathalie Japkowicz,Colin Bellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测加权平衡准确率（pBA）方法，通过使用多类子概念模型的后验概率替代不可获得的真实子概念标签，实现对类别内子概念分布不均时的模型性能进行无偏评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级评估会掩盖同一类内不同子概念间的性能差异，导致平均表现良好的模型在特定子群体上失败；现有的不平衡分类评估指标对较大的少数子概念存在偏差，而基于真实子概念标签的效用加权虽能缓解偏差，但测试时通常无法获得这些标签。

Method: 引入一种实用效用加权评估方法，用多类子概念模型预测的后验概率替代不可获得的子概念标签；评估权重定义为该后验下的期望效用，从而得到软性的、具有不确定性意识的指标——预测加权平衡准确率（pBA）。

Result: 在表格基准、医学影像和文本数据集上的实验表明，当类别内存在异质性时，未加权的评分可能具有误导性；而pBA在子概念分布不均匀但非病理性的情况下，提供了更稳定和可解释的评估结果。

Conclusion: pBA作为一种软性效用加权指标，能够在缺乏真实子概念标签时有效纠正评估偏差，提升模型在子群体层面评估的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Class-level evaluation can conceal substantial performance disparities across subconcepts within the same class, causing models that perform well on average to fail on specific subpopulations. Prior work has shown that common evaluation measures for imbalanced classification are biased toward larger minority subconcepts and that utility-based reweighting using true subconcept labels can mitigate this bias; however, such labels are rarely available at test time. We introduce a practical utility-weighted evaluation that replaces unavailable subconcept labels with predicted posterior probabilities from a multiclass subconcept model. Evaluation weights are defined as the expected utility under this posterior, yielding a soft, uncertainty-aware metric we call predicted-weighted balanced accuracy (pBA). Experiments on tabular benchmarks as well as medical-imaging and text datasets show that unweighted scores can be misleading under within-class heterogeneity, while pBA provides more stable and interpretable assessments when subconcept distributions are uneven but not pathological. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/correcting-bias-imbalance-9C6C/.

</details>


### [85] [RaMP: Runtime-Aware Megakernel Polymorphism for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26039)
*Vyom Sharma,Debajyoti Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaMP是一个路由感知的MoE调度框架，通过性能区域分析和四参数波成本模型，基于运行时专家直方图选择最优核配置，相比静态调度实现1.22倍核加速，在vLLM中端到端加速1.30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE推理系统仅根据批大小进行调度，忽略了专家路由分布对核性能的影响，导致10-70%的核吞吐量未被充分利用。

Method: 提出性能区域分析（从硬件常数预测优化时机，验证8种架构），以及四参数波成本模型（基于运行时专家直方图选择最快配置，仅需10-24分钟一次性profiling）；配合Co-designed CuTe DSL核暴露134-268种多态配置。

Result: 平均遗憾率0.93%（vs穷举搜索）；核加速1.22x（vs静态调度）；端到端加速：vLLM上1.30x（vs Triton），1.41x（vs DeepGEMM），1.13x（vs FlashInfer CUTLASS）；应用于Alpha-MoE无需修改源码即获1.14x加速。

Conclusion: 路由感知调度能显著提升MoE推理性能，且成本模型与核无关性使其具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The optimal kernel configuration for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference depends on both batch size and the expert routing distribution, yet production systems dispatch from batch size alone, leaving 10-70% of kernel throughput unrealized. We present RaMP, a routing-aware dispatch framework. A performance-region analysis derives, from hardware constants alone, when each optimization helps, correctly predicting all 8 tested architectures, including 3 unseen. A four-parameter wave cost model selects the fastest configuration from the runtime expert histogram, achieving 0.93% mean regret versus exhaustive search, fitted from just 10-24 minutes of one-time profiling per model. Because the model depends only on CTA grid geometry, it is kernel-agnostic: applied to Alpha-MoE, it delivers 1.14x with no source modification. Paired with a co-designed CuTe DSL kernel exposing 134-268 polymorphic configurations, RaMP delivers 1.22x kernel speedup over static dispatch and 1.30x end-to-end speedup in vLLM serving over Triton, 1.41x over DeepGEMM, and 1.13x over FlashInfer CUTLASS.

</details>


### [86] [Observable Neural ODEs for Identifiable Causal Forecasting in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26070)
*Jennifer Wendland,Nicolas Freitag,Maik Kschischo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在潜在状态空间模型中，当隐藏混杂因素同时影响治疗和结果时，控制理论中的可观测性是识别动态治疗效应的必要条件。提出Observable Neural ODEs（ObsNODEs）模型，通过可观测标准型学习连续时间动力学，实现因果预测。在合成癌症数据、基于MIMIC-IV的半合成数据和真实脓毒症数据上表现优于现有序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间序贯决策问题中的因果推断面临隐藏混杂因素的挑战，现有方法缺乏对可识别性条件的严格理论分析。

Method: 1) 理论层面：将控制理论中的可观测性与因果可识别性联系起来，推导连续时间调整公式；2) 模型层面：提出ObsNODEs，使用可观测标准形式的神经ODE，确保状态可从观测中重构。

Result: 在合成癌症数据、基于MIMIC-IV的半合成数据以及真实脓毒症数据上，ObsNODEs在因果预测任务中显著优于近期序列模型。

Conclusion: 隐藏混杂因素下的连续时间因果效应识别需要潜在动力学的可观测性，ObsNODEs通过可观测标准形式有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Causal inference in continuous-time sequential decision problems is challenged by hidden confounders. We show that, in latent state-space models with time-varying interventions, observability of the latent dynamics from observed data is necessary for identifying dynamic treatment effects, linking control-theoretic observability to causal identifiability, even when hidden confounders affect both treatments and outcomes. We derive a continuous-time adjustment formula expressing potential outcome distributions under treatment trajectories via the measurement model, latent dynamics, and the filtering distribution over latent states given observed histories. We propose Observable Neural ODEs (ObsNODEs), Neural ODE models in observable normal form for causal forecasting. ObsNODEs learn continuous-time dynamics with states reconstructible from observations, enabling outcome prediction under alternative treatment paths. Experiments on synthetic cancer data, semi-synthetic data based on MIMIC-IV, and real-world sepsis data show strong performance over recent sequence models.

</details>


### [87] [PPG-Based Affect Recognition with Long-Range Deep Models: A Measurement-Driven Comparison of CNN, Transformer, and Mamba Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26078)
*Karim Alghoul,Hussein Al Osman,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性比较了CNN、CNN-LSTM混合、Transformer和Mamba四种深度学习架构在基于PPG信号的 arousal、valence和relaxation状态分类任务中的表现。结果表明，CNN在大多数任务中表现最佳，模型体积最小；Transformer和Mamba性能与CNN相当但未持续超越；首次对Transformer和Mamba在PPG情感识别中的表现进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer和Mamba等长序列模型在自然语言和通用时间序列任务中表现出色，但它们能否在数据量小且噪声高的PPG情感识别任务中带来实际收益尚不明确。本文旨在通过系统对比，为可穿戴情感监测系统的模型选择提供实用指导。

Method: 在主题无关的5折交叉验证协议下，使用相同的预处理、分段和训练流程，对CNN、CNN-LSTM混合、Transformer和Mamba四种架构在基于腕部PPG信号的arousal、valence和relaxation分类任务上进行测量驱动的性能对比。

Result: Transformer和Mamba性能与CNN基线相当，但未在所有任务中持续超越CNN。CNN在整体准确率和最小模型尺寸方面表现最佳；Transformer在Arousal和Relaxation的F1分数上更具优势。

Conclusion: 对于PPG情感识别任务，CNN仍然是最有效的架构选择，而Transformer和Mamba并未带来显著的性能提升。该研究首次评估了Transformer和Mamba在PPG情感识别中的表现，为可穿戴情感监测系统的模型选型提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG) is increasingly used in wearable affective computing due to its low cost and ease of integration into consumer devices. Recent advances in deep learning have introduced long-range sequence models, such as Transformers, and state-space models, like Mamba, which have demonstrated strong performance on natural language and general time-series tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these architectures offer tangible benefits over widely used Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTMs) for PPG-based affect recognition, given that datasets are typically small and noisy. This work presents a measurement-driven comparison of four deep learning architectures, CNN, CNN-LSTM hybrid, Transformers, and Mamba, for classifying arousal, valence, and relaxation states from wrist-based PPG signals. All models are evaluated under a subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol using identical preprocessing, segmentation, and training pipelines. Our results show that the Transformer and Mamba models achieve performance comparable to that of a CNN baseline, but do not consistently outperform it across all tasks. CNNs remain the most effective overall, providing the highest accuracy with the smallest model size, whereas Transformers have a better balance of F1 scores for Arousal and Relaxation. The study provides the first evaluation of Transformer and Mamba models for PPG-based affect recognition, offering practical guidance on model selection for wearable affective monitoring systems.

</details>


### [88] [Momentum-Conserving Graph Neural Networks for Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26097)
*Jiahong Wang,Logan Numerow,Stelian Coros,Christian Theobalt,Vahid Babaei,Bernhard Thomaszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MomentumGNN架构，通过预测边级拉伸和弯曲冲量来保证线动量和角动量的守恒，在无监督物理损失训练下优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN架构在模拟可变形材料动态行为时，无法正确预测线动量和角动量等关键物理量的时间演化。

Method: 提出MomentumGNN架构，预测每条边的拉伸和弯曲冲量而非无约束的节点加速度，从而在结构上保证线动量和角动量的守恒；采用基于物理的无监督损失函数进行训练。

Result: 在动量起关键作用的多个常见场景中，该方法优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过结构化设计强制动量守恒，结合无监督物理损失训练，MomentumGNN有效提升了可变形材料动态模拟的物理准确性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as a versatile and efficient option for modeling the dynamic behavior of deformable materials. While GNNs generalize readily to arbitrary shapes, mesh topologies, and material parameters, existing architectures struggle to correctly predict the temporal evolution of key physical quantities such as linear and angular momentum. In this work, we propose MomentumGNN -- a novel architecture designed to accurately track momentum by construction. Unlike existing GNNs that output unconstrained nodal accelerations, our model predicts per-edge stretching and bending impulses which guarantee the preservation of linear and angular momentum. We train our network in an unsupervised fashion using a physics-based loss, and we show that our method outperforms baselines in a number of common scenarios where momentum plays a pivotal role.

</details>


### [89] [reward-lens: A Mechanistic Interpretability Library for Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26130)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 reward-lens 开源库，将原本用于生成式 LLM 的机械可解释性工具（logit lens、直接logit归因、激活补丁、稀疏自编码器）移植到奖励模型。核心观察是奖励头的权重向量 w_r 可作为所有可解释性问题的自然轴。库包含奖励透镜、组件归因、三模式激活补丁、奖励黑客探针套件、TopK SAE特征归因、跨模型比较及五种理论扩展。在两种生产级奖励模型及约695个RewardBench样本上验证，发现线性归因不能预测因果补丁效应（平均Spearman ρ 为负值），将这种不一致性作为暴露的属性而非缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性工具都是为生成式LLM设计的，其基本组件都投影到词汇表上；奖励模型将其替换为标量回归头，导致这些工具无法使用。需要为奖励模型开发专门的可解释性工具套件。

Method: 基于奖励头权重向量 w_r 作为自然轴的核心观察，设计了奖励透镜、组件归因、三模式激活补丁、奖励黑客探针套件、TopK SAE特征归因、跨模型比较等方法。开发了十方法适配器协议覆盖多种模型架构，以及五个理论扩展（失真指数、发散感知补丁、错位级联检测、奖励项冲突分析、概念向量分析）。

Result: 在Skywork和ArmoRM两种生产奖励模型上验证，线性归因不能预测因果补丁效应（平均Spearman ρ = -0.256和-0.027），表明观察性归因与因果性归因存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 线性归因与因果补丁效应之间的不一致性是奖励模型的一种内在属性而非缺陷，框架设计应同时保持观察性视角和因果性视角作为一等公民，使两者可直接比较。

Abstract: Every RLHF-trained language model is shaped by a reward model, yet the mechanistic interpretability toolkit -- logit lens, direct logit attribution, activation patching, sparse autoencoders -- was built for generative LLMs whose primitives all project onto a vocabulary unembedding. Reward models replace that with a scalar regression head, breaking each tool. We present reward-lens, an open-source library that ports this toolkit to reward models, organised around one observation: the reward head's weight vector $w_r$ is the natural axis for every interpretability question. The library provides a Reward Lens, component attribution, three-mode activation patching, a reward-hacking probe suite, TopK SAE feature attribution, cross-model comparison, and five theory-grounded extensions (distortion index, divergence-aware patching, misalignment cascade detection, reward-term conflict analysis, concept-vector analysis). A ten-method adapter protocol covers Llama, Mistral, Gemma-2, and ArmoRM multi-objective heads, with a generic adapter for any HuggingFace sequence classification model. We validate on two production reward models across ~695 RewardBench pairs. The central empirical finding is negative: linear attribution does not predict causal patching effects (mean Spearman $ρ= -0.256$ on Skywork, $-0.027$ on ArmoRM). The framework treats this disagreement as a property to expose, not a bug -- motivating a design that keeps observational and causal views first-class and directly comparable.

</details>


### [90] [Spatially-constrained clustering of geospatial features for heat vulnerability assessment of favelas in Rio de Janeiro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26133)
*Baptiste Clemence,Thomas Hallopeau,Vanderlei Pascoal De Matos,Laurent Demagistri,Joris Guerin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合空间约束聚类与地表温度分析，开发了一个数据驱动框架，用于评估里约热内卢贫民窟的热脆弱性。研究发现两种不同的贫民窟类型：平坦地形的近期、高连通性聚落（Cluster 0）和植被坡地上的历史、低连通性社区（Cluster 1），两者在16次极端高温事件中系统性地存在2-3°C的温度差异，平坦地形贫民窟的热暴露显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正规聚落面临不成比例的气候相关健康危害，但现有方法缺乏将不同聚落特征与环境健康结果联系起来的系统性途径。

Method: 结合空间约束聚类（spatially-constrained clustering）与地表温度（LST）分析，利用遥感和地理空间特征，开发数据驱动框架。

Result: 识别出两种贫民窟类型：Cluster 0（平坦地形、近期形成、高连通性）和Cluster 1（植被坡地、历史形成、低连通性）；16次极端高温事件中两聚类间系统温差为2-3°C。

Conclusion: 聚落形态对热脆弱性有显著影响；该框架可复用于全球非正规聚落的针对性城市规划和公共卫生干预。

Abstract: Informal settlements face disproportionate exposure to climate-related health hazards. However, existing methodologies lack systematic approaches to link diverse settlement characteristics with environmental health outcomes. We develop a data-driven framework to assess heat vulnerability in Rio de Janeiro's favelas by combining spatially-constrained clustering with land surface temperature (LST) analysis. Using remote sensing and geospatial features, we identify two distinct favela typologies: recent, well-connected settlements on flat terrain (Cluster 0) and historical, poorly-connected communities on vegetated slopes (Cluster 1). Analysis of 16 extreme heat events reveals systematic temperature differences of 2--3$^\circ$C between clusters, with flat-terrain favelas experiencing significantly higher heat exposure. Our findings demonstrate that settlement morphology critically influences heat vulnerability, providing a replicable framework for targeted urban planning and public health interventions in informal settlements globally.

</details>


### [91] [Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits: Bridging Uplift Modeling and Sequential Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26169)
*Abhirami Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算约束因果Bandit（BCCB）框架，将学习广告效果、探索不确定用户和预算控制整合为在线序贯决策过程，解决了冷启动场景下传统两阶段离线方法因数据不足而失效的问题。实验表明，BCCB从首个用户即可有效运作，而离线方法需约1万历史样本；BCCB运行方差低3-5倍，且在所有预算水平上优于Thompson Sampling等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字广告中预算约束下的投放分配是核心挑战，现有两阶段离线流水线（先估计异质处理效应，再求解约束优化）在数据充足时表现良好，但在新广告活动、新市场或新客户群体等冷启动场景下因缺乏历史数据而失效。

Method: 提出BCCB在线框架，将三个组件统一为单一序贯过程：学习个体层面广告效果、探索响应不确定的用户、随时间控制预算节奏。采用基于因果Bandit的在线决策，逐个用户做出投放决策。

Result: 在Criteo Uplift真实广告数据集上，离线方法需约1万历史观测才能产生可靠结果，而BCCB从首个用户即可有效运作；BCCB运行方差比离线方法低3-5倍；在所有预算水平上，BCCB一致优于标准Thompson Sampling、预算约束Thompson Sampling和贪心HTE估计。

Conclusion: BCCB框架在冷启动广告投放场景中具有显著的数据效率优势，方差更低，适用于实际广告活动规划，为预算约束下的在线因果推断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Treatment allocation under budget constraints is a central challenge in digital advertising: advertisers must decide which users to show ads to while spending a limited budget wisely. The standard approach follows a two-stage offline pipeline - first collect historical data to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects (HTE), then solve a constrained optimization to allocate the budget. This works well with abundant data, but fails in cold-start settings such as new campaigns, new markets, or new customer segments where little historical data exists. We propose Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB), an online framework that learns which users respond to ads while simultaneously spending the budget, making treatment decisions one user at a time. BCCB unifies three components into a single sequential process: learning individual-level ad effectiveness, exploring users whose response is uncertain, and pacing the budget over time. We evaluated on the Criteo Uplift dataset, a large-scale advertising dataset from a real randomized controlled trial. Our key finding is a data-efficiency crossover: offline methods require approximately 10,000 historical observations to produce reliable results, while BCCB operates effectively from the very first user. Furthermore, BCCB exhibits 3-5x lower performance variance between runs, making it more practical for real campaign planning. Among purely online methods, BCCB consistently outperforms standard Thompson Sampling, budgeted Thompson Sampling, and greedy HTE estimation across all budget levels tested.

</details>


### [92] [Entropy Centroids as Intrinsic Rewards for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26173)
*Wenshuo Zhao,Qi Zhu,Xingshan Zeng,Fei Mi,Lifeng Shang,Yiren Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵质心（Entropy Centroid）的低质心选择方法，利用推理过程中高熵词元簇形成的时序结构作为内在奖励信号，无需外部奖励模型即可从多个候选响应中选出最优回复。实验表明该方法在数学、代码、逻辑推理和智能体任务中，跨14B-480B模型规模均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法依赖外部奖励模型或噪声较大的内在信号（如置信度、熵），存在额外计算开销或信号不稳定的问题。本文旨在利用推理过程中不确定性在时序上的结构性模式，设计一种无需外部模型且更稳定的内在选择信号。

Method: 1) 定义高熵阶段（HEP）：从高熵词元开始到连续低熵词元出现结束的可变长度片段；2) 定义熵质心：所有HEP沿生成轨迹的加权平均位置（类似物理质心概念）；3) Lowest Centroid方法：从多个候选响应中选择熵质心最低的响应。

Result: 在数学、代码、逻辑推理和智能体任务上，跨14B到480B模型规模，Lowest Centroid持续优于现有基线方法，且随模型规模扩大增益稳定增长。

Conclusion: 高熵词元在推理过程中会聚集成连续的簇，形成稳定的模型不确定性时序结构；熵质心越低表示早期探索后快速进入置信生成阶段，对应更高质量的响应；Lowest Centroid是一种无需外部奖励模型、高效且可扩展的测试时选择方法。

Abstract: An effective way to scale up test-time compute of large language models is to sample multiple responses and then select the best one, as in Grok Heavy and Gemini Deep Think. Existing selection methods often rely on external reward models, which requires training a strong reward model and introduces additional computation overhead. As an alternative, previous approaches have explored intrinsic signals, such as confidence and entropy, but these signals are noisy with naive aggregation. In this work, we observe that high-entropy tokens tend to cluster into consecutive groups during inference, providing a more stable notion of model uncertainty than individual tokens. Together, these clusters reveal temporal patterns of model uncertainty throughout the inference process. Motivated by this observation, we propose to use the temporal structure of uncertainty as an intrinsic reward. To this end, we first formalize the basic unit of segment-level uncertainty as the High Entropy Phase (HEP), a variable-length segment that begins at a high-entropy token and ends when consecutive low-entropy tokens appear. We then define the Entropy Centroid, inspired by the concept of the center of mass in physics, as the weighted average position of all HEPs along the trajectory. Intuitively, a lower centroid indicates early exploration followed by confident generation, which we find often corresponds to higher response quality. Based on this insight, we propose the Lowest Centroid method, which selects the response with the lowest entropy centroid among multiple candidates. Experiments on mathematics, code generation, logical reasoning, and agentic tasks, across model scales ranging from 14B to 480B, show that Lowest Centroid consistently outperforms existing baselines and delivers stable gains as model size increases. Code is available at https://github.com/hkust-nlp/entropy-centroid.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient and Interpretable Transformer for Counterfactual Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26188)
*Panyi Dong,Zhiyu Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征相关变换器（FCorrTransformer），一种用于表格数据的轻量注意力架构，结合反事实注意力正则化（CAR）框架，在不依赖显式因果假设的前提下实现反事实公平预测，在保持竞争力的预测性能同时大幅降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险、强监管领域（如金融、保险）中，机器学习模型需要在预测性能、可解释性和监管公平性之间取得平衡，而现有基于注意力的变换器模型（如Transformer）即使结合公平感知技术也无法保证反事实公平性。

Method: 提出FCorrTransformer架构，其中注意力矩阵可直接统计解释为成对特征依赖关系，提升可解释性和效率；在此基础上提出CAR框架，在注意力层面强制实现敏感特征的组不变公平表示，无需显式因果假设即可促进反事实公平预测。

Result: 在不平衡分类和回归基准上的实验表明，FCorrTransformer结合CAR在实现强反事实公平性的同时保持了竞争性的预测性能，并显著降低了模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了公平理论与机器学习模型之间的关键差距，为监管敏感领域中的负责任AI提供了实用框架。

Abstract: The growing reliance of machine learning models in high-stakes, highly regulated domains such as finance and insurance has created a growing tension between predictive performance, interpretability, and regulatory fairness requirements. In these settings, models are expected not only to deliver reliable predictions but also to provide transparent decision rationales and comply with strict fairness requirements. Attention-based transformers offer powerful mechanisms for modeling complex data relationships as demonstrated in various language tasks, yet their attention mechanisms alone do not ensure counterfactually fair predictions, even when combined with fairness-aware techniques. To address these limitations, we propose the Feature Correlation Transformer (FCorrTransformer), an attention-light architecture tailored for tabular data. In this design, the attention matrix admits a direct statistical interpretation as pairwise feature dependencies, enhancing both interpretability and efficiency. Leveraging this structure, we introduce Counterfactual Attention Regularization (CAR), a framework that enforces group-invariant fair representations of sensitive features at the attention level, promoting counterfactually fair predictions without relying on explicit causal assumptions. Empirical evaluations on imbalanced classification and regression benchmarks demonstrate that FCorrTransformer combined with CAR achieves strong counterfactual fairness while maintaining competitive predictive performance and substantially reducing model complexity compared with standard transformer-based baselines. Overall, this work bridges a critical gap between fairness theory and machine learning models, offering a practical framework for responsible AI in regulatory-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [94] [DORA: A Scalable Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning System for Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26256)
*Tianhao Hu,Xiangcheng Liu,Youshao Xiao,Yang Zheng,Xuan Huang,Jinrui Ding,Yufei Zhang,Tao Liang,Hongyu Zang,Quan Chen,Yueqing Sun,Wenjie Shi,Chao Zhang,Wei Wang,Qi Gu,Yerui Sun,Yucheng Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DORA系统，通过多版本流式异步rollout机制，在不破坏算法收敛性的前提下，将RL训练中占50-80%时长的rollout阶段完全流水线化，实现2-4倍加速，并开源了LongCat-Flash-Thinking模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练中RL的rollout阶段因长尾轨迹阻塞训练管线，成为主要瓶颈（占总步时50-80%）。异步训练虽能重叠生成与训练，但现有方法无法根本解决长尾轨迹问题（在MoE模型中更严重），或偏离标准RL公式导致收敛受阻。

Method: DORA采用算法-系统协同设计，核心创新是多版本流式rollout：同时维护多个策略版本并发执行rollout，在彻底消除气泡的同时满足算法约束（帧内轨迹策略一致性、数据完整性、有界陈旧度）。

Result: 在开源基准上吞吐量比SOTA系统提升2-3倍；在数万加速器的大规模工业场景中，相比同步训练加速2-4倍；开源模型LongCat-Flash-Thinking在复杂推理基准上达到先进LLM水平。

Conclusion: DORA通过算法-系统协同设计有效解决了LLM RL训练中rollout长尾瓶颈问题，在不牺牲收敛性的前提下实现了显著加速，验证了异步训练在保证算法正确性的同时提升效率的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a critical paradigm for LLM post-training, yet the rollout phase -- accounting for 50--80% of total step time -- is bottlenecked by skewed generation: long-tailed trajectories indispensable for model performance block the entire training pipeline. Asynchronous training offers a natural remedy by overlapping generation with training, but introduces a fundamental tension between efficiency and algorithmic correctness. We identify three constraints in asynchronous training to preserve convergence: intra-trajectory policy consistency, data integrity, and bounded staleness. Existing approaches fail to intrinsically address the long-tailed trajectory problem, which is further exacerbated by the imbalance characteristic of Mix-of-Experts models, or deviate from the standard RL training formulation, thereby hindering model convergence. Therefore, we propose DORA (Dynamic ORchestration for Asynchronous Rollout), which addresses this challenge through algorithm-system co-design. DORA introduces multi-version streaming rollout, a novel asynchronous paradigm that maintains multiple policy versions concurrently -- simultaneously achieving full bubble elimination without compromising algorithmic constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that our DORA system achieves substantial improvements in throughput -- up to 2--3 times higher than state-of-the-art systems on open-source benchmarks -- without compromising convergence. Furthermore, in large-scale industrial applications with tens of thousands of accelerators, DORA accelerates RL training by 2--4 times compared to synchronous training across various scenarios. The resultant open-source models, LongCat-Flash-Thinking, exhibit competitive performance on complex reasoning benchmarks, matching the capability of most advanced LLMs.

</details>


### [95] [NeuroPlastic: A Plasticity-Modulated Optimizer for Biologically Inspired Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26297)
*Douglas Jiang,Yuechen Wang,Jiayi Wang,Jiaying Geng,Qinglong Wang,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroPlastic是一种受神经生物学多因子突触可塑性启发的优化器，通过自适应多信号调制机制增强梯度更新，在图像分类任务中持续优于纯梯度方法，尤其在数据有限或噪声条件下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习优化算法主要依赖局部梯度统计，缺乏更丰富的调制信号。受神经生物学中多因子突触可塑性的启发，作者希望引入一种轻量级可塑性调制机制来增强梯度下降优化，特别是在学习信号有限或含噪声的场景下提升性能。

Method: NeuroPlastic通过一个轻量级调制层，利用三种交互分量（梯度统计、类活性统计和类记忆统计）动态缩放梯度更新，该调制机制可无缝集成到标准深度学习训练流程中。

Result: 在图像分类基准上，NeuroPlastic持续优于纯梯度对照版本；在Fashion-MNIST和低数据 regimes下增益更显著；在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18迁移实验中无需重新调参即保持稳定且具竞争力。

Conclusion: 多信号可塑性启发的调制机制为传统梯度驱动优化提供了有价值的扩展，尤其在信号有限或含噪声的场景下表现突出，为基于梯度的深度学习方法指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are fundamental to modern deep learning, yet most widely used methods rely on update rules based primarily on local gradient statistics. We introduce NeuroPlastic, a plasticity-modulated optimizer that augments gradient-based updates with an adaptive multi-signal modulation mechanism inspired by multi-factor synaptic plasticity, a concept from neurobiology. NeuroPlastic dynamically scales gradient updates using interacting components that capture gradient, activity-like, and memory-like statistics, forming a lightweight modulation layer compatible with standard deep learning training pipelines. Across image classification benchmarks, NeuroPlastic consistently improves over a controlled gradient-only ablation, with more pronounced gains on the Fashion-MNIST benchmark and in reduced-data regimes. In transfer experiments on CIFAR-10 with ResNet-18, the method remains stable and competitive without retuning. These results suggest that multi-signal plasticity-inspired modulation can provide a useful extension to conventional gradient-driven optimization, particularly when learning signals are limited or noisy, and offer a promising direction for gradient-based methods in deep learning.

</details>


### [96] [Cheeger--Hodge Contrastive Learning for Structurally Robust Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26301)
*Mengyang Zhao,Longlong Li,Cunquan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cheeger-Hodge对比学习（CHCL）框架，通过对齐一个对扰动稳健的Cheeger-Hodge联合签名来替代传统数据增强，从而学习鲁棒的图表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图对比学习（GCL）仅依赖数据增强设计来定义所学的不变性，在结构扰动下表现脆弱。

Method: 提出Cheeger-Hodge联合签名，结合基于代数连通性λ₂的Cheeger连接性签名与1-Hodge拉普拉斯矩阵的低频谱，并在增强视图间对齐编码器表示与该签名。

Result: 在标准基准和迁移设置上，CHCL在性能、鲁棒性和泛化方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: CHCL通过引入结构稳健的图签名，有效增强了图对比学习对局部结构扰动的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) has emerged as a prominent framework for unsupervised graph representation learning. However, relying on augmentation design alone to define the invariances learned by GCL can be brittle under structural perturbations. To address this issue, we propose Cheeger--Hodge Contrastive Learning (CHCL), a framework that aligns a perturbation-stable Cheeger--Hodge joint signature across augmented views for robust graph representation learning. The proposed signature combines a Cheeger-inspired connectivity signature derived from the algebraic connectivity \(λ_2\) with the low-frequency spectrum of the 1-Hodge Laplacian, thereby capturing both global connectivity and higher-order structural information. By aligning encoder representations with the proposed Cheeger--Hodge joint signature across augmented views, CHCL learns graph embeddings that are robust to local structural perturbations. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks, transfer settings demonstrate that CHCL consistently improves performance, robustness, and generalization.

</details>


### [97] [Addressing Performance Saturation for LLM RL via Precise Entropy Curve Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26326)
*Bolian Li,Yifan Wang,Yi Ding,Anamika Lochab,Ananth Grama,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Entrocraft是一种基于拒绝采样的方法，通过偏置优势分布实现自定义熵调度，解决RL训练中的熵塌缩和性能饱和问题，使4B模型超越8B基线，pass@K提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法在训练过程中会出现性能饱和，其根本原因是熵的塌缩（entropy collapse），限制了探索能力和长期训练收益。现有正则化或裁剪方法虽试图阻止熵塌缩，但长期熵曲线不稳定，阻碍性能提升。

Method: 提出Entrocraft，一种简单的拒绝采样方法，通过偏置优势分布（advantage distributions）来实现用户自定义的熵调度（entropy schedule）。该方法无需目标正则化，且与优势估计器无关。理论上建立了每步熵变化与优势分布之间的关系。

Result: 线性退火调度（从高熵开始逐渐衰减到略低的目标）表现最佳。Entrocraft解决了性能饱和问题，显著提升了泛化能力、输出多样性和长期训练效果。4B模型超越8B基线，训练持续时间延长4倍才进入平台期，pass@K比基线提升50%。

Conclusion: Entrocraft通过灵活控制熵调度有效缓解了RL训练中的熵塌缩，实现了更稳定和持久的性能提升，为LLM的RL训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has unlocked complex reasoning abilities in large language models (LLMs). However, most RL algorithms suffer from performance saturation, preventing further gains as RL training scales. This problem can be characterized by the collapse of entropy, a key diagnostic for exploration in RL. Existing attempts have tried to prevent entropy collapse through regularization or clipping, but their resulting entropy curves often exhibit instability in the long term, which hinders performance gains. In this paper, we introduce Entrocraft, a simple rejection-sampling approach that realizes any user-customized entropy schedule by biasing the advantage distributions. Entrocraft requires no objective regularization and is advantage-estimator-agnostic. Theoretically, we relate per-step entropy change to the advantage distribution under minimal assumptions, which explains the behavior of existing RL and entropy-preserving methods. Entrocraft also enables a systematic study of entropy schedules, where we find that linear annealing, which starts high and decays to a slightly lower target, performs best. Empirically, Entrocraft addresses performance saturation, significantly improving generalization, output diversity, and long-term training. It enables a 4B model to outperform an 8B baseline, sustains improvement for up to 4x longer before plateauing, and raises pass@K by 50% over the baseline.

</details>


### [98] [AlphaJet: Automated Conceptual Aircraft Synthesis via Disentangled Generative Priors and Topology-Preserving Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26337)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaJet是一个端到端的自动化飞机概念设计管道，能从文本任务规范实时进化出可行的3D飞机设计，通过AD-VAE可解释形状先验、拓扑精英遗传算法和安装感知几何评分三大创新，在CPU上实现交互式运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统飞机概念设计依赖专家反复迭代的人工过程，效率低下且难以大规模探索设计空间，需要一种自动化闭环系统来加速早期设计阶段的设计空间探索。

Method: 提出AlphaJet管道，包含三个核心创新：(i) 解剖解耦变分自编码器(AD-VAE)，前25个潜在维度受监督对齐命名解剖参数，提供可解释形状先验；(ii) 拓扑精英遗传算法，保护五种尾翼拓扑中每种的最佳个体并触发停滞重启，防止过早收敛到单一构型；(iii) 安装感知几何评分，计算发动机与其他结构部件之间的有符号穿透，消除生成模型中常见的冗余伪影。

Result: 整个闭环在CPU上交互式运行，每一代设计结果实时流式传输至浏览器查看器，成为实用的早期阶段设计空间探索自动化工具。

Conclusion: AlphaJet成功将传统专家驱动的迭代设计过程转变为端到端自动化合成管道，实现了从文本任务规范到可行3D飞机设计的实时进化，为实际工程中的早期设计空间探索提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Conceptual aircraft design is traditionally an expert-mediated iterative process in which a human designer proposes a configuration, runs low-order physics, inspects the result, and re-proposes. We present AlphaJet, an end-to-end automated synthesis pipeline that closes this loop. From a textual mission specification (mass, range, cruise speed, hard size envelope, engine count, areal density) AlphaJet evolves a feasible 3D aircraft in real time, scored by a transparent multi-disciplinary fitness function covering aerodynamics, structures, weights, stability, packaging, and geometric mount consistency. Three contributions distinguish our approach: (i) an Anatomically-Disentangled Variational Autoencoder (AD-VAE) whose first 25 latent dimensions are supervised to align with named anatomical parameters, providing an interpretable shape prior; (ii) a topology-elitist genetic algorithm that protects the best individual from each of five tail topologies and triggers stagnation restarts, preventing premature collapse to a single configuration; and (iii) mount-aware geometric scoring that computes signed penetration between engines and other structural parts, eliminating the redundant artifacts common in generative aircraft models. The full loop runs interactively on a CPU and streams every generation to a browser viewer, making it a practical real-world automation tool for early-phase design-space exploration.

</details>


### [99] [Uncertainty-Aware Reward Discounting for Mitigating Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26360)
*Disha Singha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双源不确定性感知奖励框架，通过集成模型不确定性和偏好不确定性，结合置信度调节的可靠性过滤器来指导动作选择，在多种离散和连续控制环境中显著减少了奖励破解行为（降低93.7%），并能在高达30%的监督噪声下保持稳健性，为构建更可靠、更对齐的强化学习系统提供了原则性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习系统假设奖励函数精确可靠，但真实世界中的目标（尤其来自人类偏好的）具有不确定性、上下文依赖性和内部不一致性，导致奖励破解、过度优化和过度自信行为等对齐失败问题。

Method: 提出双源不确定性感知奖励框架：1) 通过集成模型在值预测上的不一致性捕捉认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）；2) 通过奖励标注的变异性捕捉偏好不确定性（preference uncertainty）；3) 结合两者信号，通过置信度调节的可靠性过滤器（Reliability Filter）自适应调整动作选择，平衡利用与谨慎。

Result: 在6x6、8x8、10x10离散网格环境和Hopper-v4、Walker2d-v4高维连续控制环境中，该方法实现了更稳定的训练动态，减少了奖励歧义下的剥削性行为，奖励破解行为（以陷阱访问频率衡量）降低了93.7%，在高达30%的监督噪声下保持统计显著性和稳健性，但峰值奖励相比无约束基线有所下降。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性作为奖励信号的一等公民对待，该工作为构建更可靠、更对齐的强化学习系统提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) systems typically optimize scalar reward functions that assume precise and reliable evaluation of outcomes. However, real-world objectives--especially those derived from human preferences--are often uncertain, context-dependent, and internally inconsistent. This mismatch can lead to alignment failures such as reward hacking, over-optimization, and overconfident behavior.
  We introduce a dual-source uncertainty-aware reward framework that explicitly models both epistemic uncertainty in value estimation and uncertainty in human preferences. Model uncertainty is captured via ensemble disagreement over value predictions, while preference uncertainty is derived from variability in reward annotations. We combine these signals through a confidence-adjusted Reliability Filter that adaptively modulates action selection, encouraging a balance between exploitation and caution.
  Empirical results across multiple discrete grid configurations (6x6, 8x8, 10x10) and high-dimensional continuous control environments (Hopper-v4, Walker2d-v4) demonstrate that our approach yields more stable training dynamics and reduces exploitative behaviors under reward ambiguity, achieving a 93.7% reduction in reward-hacking behavior as measured by trap visitation frequency. We demonstrate statistical significance of these improvements and robustness under up to 30% supervisory noise, albeit with a trade-off in peak observed reward compared to unconstrained baselines.
  By treating uncertainty as a first-class component of the reward signal, this work offers a principled approach toward more reliable and aligned reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [100] [CoQuant: Joint Weight-Activation Subspace Projection for Mixed-Precision LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26378)
*Zhe Ding,Su Pan,Duowei Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoQuant提出了一种联合权重-激活子空间投影方法，通过理论建模输出误差并推导出闭式加权PCA解，在低比特LLM量化中同时平衡权重和激活协方差以选择最优高精度子空间，实验表明在Llama-3.2和Qwen2.5上优于现有PTQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度PTQ方法仅依赖激活统计信息来保留关键子空间，忽略了线性操作中输出扰动由激活和权重量化噪声共同驱动的基本特性，导致量化效果受限。

Method: 理论建模期望输出误差，推导出闭式加权PCA解，联合平衡权重和激活协方差来选择最优高精度子空间。

Result: 在Llama-3.2和Qwen2.5模型上，CoQuant在WikiText困惑度和零样本常识推理准确率上均持续优于强PTQ基线。

Conclusion: 联合权重-激活子空间建模为低比特LLM量化提供了一种有原则且有效的方向。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become an important technique for reducing the inference cost of Large Language Models (LLMs). While recent mixed-precision methods improve ultra-low bit quantization by preserving critical subspaces in high precision, they typically construct these subspaces relying solely on activation statistics. This ignores the fundamental nature of linear operations, where the output perturbation is jointly driven by both activation and weight quantization noise. In this paper, we propose CoQuant, a joint weight-activation subspace projection method. By theoretically modeling the expected output error, CoQuant formulates a closed-form weighted PCA solution that balances activation and weight covariances to select the optimal high-precision subspace. Extensive experiments on Llama-3.2 and Qwen2.5 models show that CoQuant consistently outperforms strong PTQ baselines in both WikiText perplexity and zero-shot common-sense reasoning accuracy. These results demonstrate that joint weight-activation subspace modeling provides a principled and effective direction for low-bit LLM quantization. The source code is available at https://github.com/Zachary5895/CoQuant.

</details>


### [101] [Unifying Runtime Monitoring Approaches for Safety-Critical Machine Learning: Application to Vision-Based Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26411)
*Mathieu Dario,Florent Chenevier,Kévin Delmas,Joris Guerin,Jérémie Guiochet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的运行时监控框架，将监控方法分为ODD监控、OOD监控和OMS监控三类，并在航空关键安全应用（着陆跑道检测）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML安全关键应用的运行时监控研究碎片化严重，不同社区涌现出独立的方法，缺乏统一的分类和理解框架。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将运行时监控方法分为三类：ODD监控（确保符合预期运行条件）、OOD监控（拒绝偏离训练数据的输入）和OMS监控（基于模型内部状态或输出检测异常行为），并在航空着陆跑道检测任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 该分类框架有助于设计互补的监控活动，并能够使用共同的安全导向指标对不同监控器进行评估和比较。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够促进监控活动的设计，实现不同监控器在统一安全指标下的评估与比较，对ML安全关键应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring is essential to ensure the safety of ML applications in safety-critical domains. However, current research is fragmented, with independent methods emerging from different communities. In this paper, we propose a unified framework categorising runtime monitoring approaches into three distinct types: Operational Design Domain (ODD) monitoring, which ensures compliance with expected operating conditions; Out-of-Distribution (OOD) monitoring, which rejects inputs that deviate from the training data; and Out-of-Model-Scope (OMS) monitoring, which detects anomalous model behaviour based its internal states or outputs. We demonstrate the benefits of this categorization with a dedicated experiment on an aeronautical safety-critical application: runway detection during landing. This framework facilitates design of monitoring activities, with complementary categories of monitors, and enables evaluation and comparison of different monitors using common, safety-oriented metrics.

</details>


### [102] [STLGT: A Scalable Trace-Based Linear Graph Transformer for Tail Latency Prediction in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26422)
*Yongliang Ding,Qigong Bi,Peng Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STLGT是一种基于线性图Transformer的端到端尾延迟预测器，通过结构感知的线性图Transformer和去耦时序模块，在微服务系统中实现高效准确的p95尾延迟预测，相比PERT-GNN平均MAPE降低8.5%，CPU推理速度提升最高12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中准确的端到端尾延迟预测对主动SLO管理至关重要，但现有方法难以建模长程依赖传播和非平稳突发性工作负载，同时在大规模部署中保持推理效率。

Method: STLGT将调用链编码为span图，采用结构感知的线性图Transformer传播跨服务依赖（推理时间与span图大小呈线性关系），并设计去耦时序模块捕获工作负载动态变化。

Result: 在个性化教育微服务应用、DeathStarBench和Alibaba数据集上，STLGT相比PERT-GNN平均MAPE降低8.5%；在N=32（预处理后最大span图大小）时，CPU推理速度提升最高12倍。

Conclusion: STLGT通过线性复杂度的图Transformer和去耦时序模块，在保证高精度尾延迟预测的同时实现了高效推理，消融实验验证了各组件在突发流量下的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate end-to-end tail-latency forecasting is critical for proactive SLO management in microservice systems. However, modeling long-range dependency propagation and non-stationary, bursty workloads while maintaining inference efficiency at scale remains challenging. We present STLGT (Scalable Trace-based Linear Graph Transformer), a per-API predictor that encodes traces as span graphs for multi-step p95 tail-latency forecasting. STLGT uses a structure-aware linear graph Transformer to propagate cross-service dependencies with inference time linear in span graph size, and a decoupled temporal module to capture workload dynamics. Across a personalized education microservice application, DeathStarBench, and Alibaba traces, STLGT improves forecasting accuracy over PERT-GNN by 8.5% MAPE on average and achieves up to 12x faster CPU inference at N=32, matching the maximum span graph size after preprocessing the Alibaba traces. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of each component, especially under bursty traffic.

</details>


### [103] [Near-Optimal Cryptographic Hardness of Learning With Homogeneous Halfspaces Under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26446)
*Jizhou Huang,Brendan Juba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯分布下同质半空间的三个问题：不可知学习、单侧可靠学习和公平审计，并在LWE假设下证明了这些问题几乎最优的计算困难性结果，将此前主要适用于一般半空间的下界扩展到了同质半空间，缩小了同质半空间不可知学习的上下界差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声半空间学习问题中，现有的下界结果主要针对一般半空间，而对同质半空间（即过原点的半空间）的困难性认知仍不充分。本文旨在填补这一空白，将计算复杂性的下界结果扩展至同质半空间，并缩小不可知学习场景下理论与实际算法之间的间隙。

Method: 基于广泛认可的LWE（Learning With Errors）计算困难性假设，通过归约方法构造对应的困难实例，证明在标准高斯边缘分布下，同质半空间的不可知学习、单侧可靠学习及公平审计等问题均无法在多项式时间内达到接近最优的解。

Result: 获得了上述三个问题在LWE假设下的近最优计算困难下界，严格推广了此前仅限于一般半空间的结果；特别地，同质半空间不可知学习的下界与上界之间的差距被显著缩小。

Conclusion: 在标准计算假设下，同质半空间的学习与审计问题本质上是困难的，算法改进空间有限；这些结果为进一步理解高斯分布下半空间学习的计算复杂性提供了更完整的图景。

Abstract: We study three problems that involve identifying homogeneous halfspaces under Gaussian distributions: agnostic learning, one-sided reliable learning, and fairness auditing. In each of these problems, we are given labeled examples $(\mathbf{x}, \mathrm{y})$ drawn from an unknown distribution on $\mathbb{R}^d\times\{-1, +1\}$, whose marginal distribution on $\mathbf{x}$ is standard Gaussian and on $\mathrm{y}$ is arbitrary. The goal of each problem is to output a homogeneous halfspace that approaches the best-fitting homogeneous halfspace in terms of its corresponding loss measure. We prove near-optimal computational hardness results for these problems under the widely believed hardness assumption of the Learning With Errors (LWE) problem. Prior hardness results for these problems were mostly established for general halfspaces; our findings extend some of these hardness results to homogeneous halfspaces. Remarkably, our lower bound strictly generalizes over prior works and narrows the gap between the upper and lower bounds for agnostically learning homogeneous halfspaces under Gaussian marginals.

</details>


### [104] [Hierarchical adaptive control for real-time dynamic inference at the edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26470)
*Francesco Daghero,Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层自适应架构，通过全局调度器配置级联分类器、本地控制器跟踪数据漂移和硬件资源来动态调整计算负载，在嵌入式设备上实现最高2.45倍延迟降低和2.86倍能效提升，同时准确率下降不超过4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态ML模型部署复杂，其超参数依赖校准数据集与测试分布匹配的假设，在真实场景中难以成立，导致性能可能低于静态模型。

Method: 提出双层自适应架构：(1) 全局层：调度器配置并部署满足延迟和内存约束的级联分类器（轻量专用模型+通用后备模型）；(2) 节点层：本地控制器跟踪数据漂移和硬件资源，动态启用/禁用专用预测器以保持能效并避免违反延迟约束。

Result: 在两种数据集上，平均每推理延迟降低2.45倍，能耗降低2.86倍，准确率下降不超过4%。

Conclusion: 该设计允许更长的运行时间而无需全局重新部署，且在全局控制器不可达时仍能高效执行。

Abstract: Industrial systems increasingly depend on Machine Learning (ML), and operate on heterogeneous nodes that must satisfy tight latency, energy, and memory constraints. Dynamic ML models, which reconfigure their computational footprint at runtime, promise high energy efficiency and lower average latency for modest accuracy tradeoffs; however, their deployment is complex due to the additional hyperparameters they rely on. These hyperparameters, controlling the accuracy versus average latency tradeoff, are often tuned on a calibration dataset that must match the test time distribution, an assumption that rarely holds in real-world scenarios, leading to suboptimal operational conditions, possibly below static models. We propose a two-tier adaptive architecture that co-optimizes model and system decisions. At the global level, a scheduler configures and deploys, for each edge node, a cascade of classifiers composed of lightweight specialized models and a generalist fallback, satisfying latency and memory constraints. At the node level, a local controller tracks data drifts and hardware resources, enabling or disabling specialized predictors (SP) to preserve high energy efficiency and avoid latency-constraint violations under varying conditions. This design allows longer operating times without forcing a global redeployment step, and enables efficient execution in case of an unreachable remote global controller. We evaluate the approach on two datasets under controlled distribution mismatch scenarios, showing average per-inference reductions of latency up to 2.45x and energy up to 2.86x, with less than 4% accuracy drop compared to static baselines. Our contributions are:(1) a budgeted SP-cascade formulation that preserves worst-case latency constraints;(2) a hierarchical controller that maintains efficiency under data and resource changes; and (3) an experimental evaluation on embedded hardware.

</details>


### [105] [Understanding DNNs in Feature Interaction Models: A Dimensional Collapse Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26489)
*Jiancheng Wang,Mingjia Yin,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出新视角解释DNN在特征交互推荐模型中的作用：DNN通过缓解嵌入表示的维度坍缩来提升模型鲁棒性，而非传统认为的隐式高阶特征交互能力。实验和理论分析验证了并行和堆叠DNN均能有效防止维度坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于DNN在特征交互推荐模型中作用的长期争议——DNN究竟是能隐式捕获高阶特征交互，还是连二阶交互（点积）都难以有效学习。作者提出从维度鲁棒性的新视角来解释DNN的有效性。

Method: 1) 在两种特征交互模型上对完整DNN进行整体研究；2) 对DNN内部组件进行细粒度消融分析；3) 基于梯度的理论分析，并辅以实验证据揭示维度坍缩的底层机制。

Result: 并行DNN和堆叠DNN都能有效缓解嵌入表示的维度坍缩现象；梯度分析揭示了维度坍缩的底层机制。

Conclusion: DNN在推荐模型中的有效性主要归因于其对维度坍缩的缓解作用，而非高阶特征交互的隐式捕获能力。这为理解DNN在推荐系统中的作用提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: DNNs have gained widespread adoption in feature interaction recommendation models. However, there has been a longstanding debate on their roles. On one hand, some works claim that DNNs possess the ability to implicitly capture high-order feature interactions. Conversely, recent studies have highlighted the limitations of DNNs in effectively learning dot products, specifically second-order interactions, let alone higher-order interactions. In this paper, we present a novel perspective to understand the effectiveness of DNNs: their impact on the dimensional robustness of the representations. In particular, we conduct extensive experiments involving both parallel DNNs and stacked DNNs. Our evaluation encompasses an overall study of complete DNN on two feature interaction models, alongside a fine-grained ablation analysis of components within DNNs. Experimental results demonstrate that both parallel and stacked DNNs can effectively mitigate the dimensional collapse of embeddings. Furthermore, a gradient-based theoretical analysis, supported by empirical evidence, uncovers the underlying mechanisms of dimensional collapse.

</details>


### [106] [Large-scale semi-supervised learning with online spectral graph sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26550)
*Daniele Calandriello,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sparse-HFS算法，仅需O(n polylog(n))空间和O(m polylog(n))时间即可求解半监督学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习算法在处理大规模数据时面临计算复杂度和存储开销过高的问题，亟需更高效的解决方案。

Method: Sparse-HFS是一种可扩展算法，利用稀疏化技术实现近线性空间与时间复杂度。

Result: 在空间复杂度上达到O(n polylog(n))，时间复杂度上达到O(m polylog(n))。

Conclusion: Sparse-HFS为大规模半监督学习问题提供了一种高效、可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse-HFS, a scalable algorithm that can compute solutions to SSL problems using only O(n polylog(n)) space and O(m polylog(n)) time.

</details>


### [107] [Advancing multi-site emission control: A physics-informed transfer learning framework with mixture of experts for carbon-pollutant synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26571)
