*Yuxuan Ying,Hanqing Yang,Kaige Wang,Yu Hu,Zhiming Zheng,Yunliang Jiang,Xiaoqing Lin,Xiaodong Li,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息引导的迁移学习框架（碳-污染物混合专家模型），通过在13个垃圾焚烧厂中联合考虑物理约束、运行工况异质性和碳-污染物耦合关系，实现了跨设施的高精度排放预测与迁移，并构建了可解释的数字孪生体以支持工况感知的协同控制操作导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动模型在单一工厂内预测准确，但难以跨设施迁移，限制了其在规模化排放控制策略中的应用价值。因此，需要一种能够跨设施迁移的、考虑物理约束和运行工况异质性的可扩展排放建模方法。

Method: 提出物理信息引导的迁移学习框架，核心是碳-污染物混合专家模型（Carbon-Pollutant Mixture-of-Experts），包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于运行工况的专家路由机制（regime-dependent expert routing）；(2) 基于守恒定律的物理约束正则化（conservation-based regularization）；(3) 碳-污染物协同指数（CPSI）用于综合风险评估。该框架可从参考设施迁移至目标设施，并通过工况加权适应而非完全重新学习。

Result: 在13个垃圾焚烧厂中，源域平均污染物R²达0.668-0.904，CPSI R²达0.666-0.970；迁移至12个目标厂后，平均污染物R²保持在0.661-0.842，CPSI R²为0.610-0.841。专家利用模式表明适应通过工况的结构化重加权实现。

Conclusion: 该框架将学习到的表示扩展为可解释的数字孪生体，提供了从排放预测到工况感知操作导航的路径，支持异质性的垃圾焚烧系统中可扩展的碳-污染物协同控制。

Abstract: Municipal solid waste incineration is increasingly central to urban waste management, yet its sustainability benefit depends on controlling carbon emissions and multiple air pollutants under highly heterogeneous operating conditions. Current data-driven models are often accurate within individual plants but are difficult to transfer across facilities, limiting their value for scalable emission-control strategies. Here we show that multi-site emission behaviour can be represented through transferable system-level structures when physical constraints, operating-regime heterogeneity and carbon--pollutant coupling are jointly considered. We develop a physics-informed transfer learning framework built on a carbon--pollutant mixture-of-experts model, which combines regime-dependent expert routing with conservation-based regularization and a carbon--pollutant synergistic index for integrated risk evaluation. Across 13 municipal solid waste incineration plants, the model captured both pollutant-specific emissions and system-level risk, achieving source-domain average pollutant $R^2$ values of 0.668--0.904 and CPSI $R^2$ values of 0.666--0.970. After transfer from a reference facility to 12 target plants, average pollutant $R^2$ remained between 0.661 and 0.842, while CPSI retained comparable transferability ($R^2$ = 0.610--0.841). Expert-utilization patterns further indicate that adaptation occurs through structured re-weighting of operating regimes rather than complete model re-learning. By extending the learned representation into an interpretable digital twin, this framework provides a route from emission prediction to regime-aware operational navigation, supporting scalable carbon--pollutant synergistic control across heterogeneous waste-to-energy systems.

</details>


### [108] [PAINT: Partial-Solution Adaptive Interpolated Training for Self-Distilled Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26573)
*Zhiquan Tan,Yinrong Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAINT（部分解自适应插值训练）方法，通过根据 rollout-参考解重叠程度部分遮蔽已验证解，并对熵不匹配的token位置进行能量空间插值，在竞赛级数学推理基准上优于现有在线自蒸馏和GRPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理需要既对齐模型测试时状态、又在token级别具有信息量的监督信号。现有方法中，RL可验证奖励提供在线探索但监督稀疏，SFT/蒸馏提供密集目标但依赖固定轨迹或更强教师模型。需要一种中间地带的监督方法。

Method: PAINT方法：1）根据模型自身rollout与已验证解的重叠程度对解进行部分遮蔽；2）在稀疏的熵不匹配token位置上进行小范围能量空间插值，实现上下文自适应重评分。

Result: 在竞赛级数学基准上，PAINT在三个Qwen3规模上一致优于强在线自蒸馏基线。在Qwen3-8B上，macro Avg@12比基线提升2.1个点，比GRPO提升2.9个点。

Conclusion: 推理监督的关键不仅在于是否提供特权上下文，更在于揭示多少上下文以及其分布应如何塑造学生模型。部分解自适应插值是一种有效的中间监督策略。

Abstract: Improving large language model (LLM) reasoning requires supervision that is both aligned with the model's own test-time states and informative at the token level. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards provides on-policy exploration but offers sparse, high-variance credit; supervised fine-tuning and distillation provide dense targets but often train on fixed trajectories or rely on stronger teachers. Recent privileged on-policy self-distillation explores a middle ground by scoring student rollouts with the same model under verified solution context. We revisit this setting through a contextual re-scoring lens: for reasoning, the important choices are not only whether privileged context is available, but how much of it should be revealed and where its distribution should shape the student. We propose PAINT (Partial-solution Adaptive INterpolated Training), which masks the verified solution according to rollout-reference overlap and applies a small energy-space interpolation on a sparse set of entropy-mismatch token positions. Across competition-level math benchmarks, PAINT consistently improves over a strong prior on-policy self-distillation baseline at all three Qwen3 scales. On Qwen3-8B, it raises macro Avg@12 by 2.1 points over this prior baseline and 2.9 points over GRPO.

</details>


### [109] [Who Trains Matters: Federated Learning under Enrollment and Participation Selection Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26604)
*Gota Morishita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习中因入选规则（入选偏差）和客户端参与（参与偏差）导致的两阶段选择偏差问题，提出了基于逆概率加权（FedIPW）的聚合方案来恢复目标总体均值，并针对非入选客户端不可用信息的情况，引入有限信息聚合校准方法。实验表明，修正入选偏差能有效减少目标总体误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究多关注轮次层面的参与偏差，但很少关注总体层面的入选偏差，后者会导致训练目标与目标总体目标之间持续失配。本文旨在形式化并纠正此偏差。

Method: 形式化了两阶段选择模型；提出FedIPW（逆概率加权聚合方案），在标准可忽略性和正定性假设下恢复目标总体均值；针对非入选客户端不可用协变量的问题，引入有限信息聚合校准方法，利用已知目标总体汇总统计量重新加权入选样本。

Result: 在合成联邦逻辑回归实验中验证了目标失配的预测，并表明入选偏差修正能减少两阶段选择下目标总体误差。

Conclusion: 不完整的选择纠偏会导致不可忽略的偏差下限，而入选偏差修正对于减少目标总体误差至关重要。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) trains a shared model from updates contributed by distributed clients, often implicitly assuming that contributing clients are representative of the target population. In practice, this representativeness assumption can fail at two distinct stages, inducing selection bias. First, eligibility rules such as device constraints, software requirements, or user consent determine which clients are ever enrolled and reachable for training, inducing \emph{enrollment bias}. Second, among enrolled clients, user and system factors such as battery state, network status, and local time determine which clients participate in each communication round, inducing \emph{participation bias}. Although existing work has largely addressed round-level participation bias, it has paid far less attention to population-level enrollment bias, which can induce a persistent mismatch between the training objective and the target-population objective. We formalize FL under a two-stage selection model and derive \textsc{FedIPW}, an inverse-probability-weighted aggregation scheme that recovers the target-population mean update under standard ignorability and positivity assumptions. Because client-level covariates are often unavailable for non-enrolled clients, we also introduce a limited-information aggregate-calibration extension that uses known target-population summaries to reweight the enrolled sample, partially correcting enrollment bias. We further provide an algorithm-agnostic optimization analysis under residual weighting error and show that incomplete selection correction can induce a non-vanishing bias floor. Finally, experiments on synthetic federated logistic regression validate the predicted objective mismatch and show that enrollment correction reduces target-population error under two-stage selection.

</details>


### [110] [Electricity price forecasting across Norway's five bidding zones in the post-crisis era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26634)
*My Thi Diem Phan,Trung Tuyen Truong,Hoai Phuong Ha,Dat Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了挪威五个电力竞价区的电价预测，发现LightGBM在所有区域表现最优（MAE 1.64-5.74 EUR/MWh），仅靠滞后价格和日历变量即可达到高精度，但外部特征（如水库水位、天然气价格）在压力市场状态下对误差分层至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2021-2022年能源危机和与欧洲大陆的深度融合从根本上改变了挪威水电主导电力市场的价格形成机制，历史数据训练的预测模型可靠性下降，且缺乏统一的特征贡献评估基准。

Method: 构建了2019-2025年多模态小时级数据集，评估了LightGBM、ARX和多种深度学习模型，采用严格因果测试集、滚动原点回测、留一组特征消融和条件状态分析。

Result: LightGBM在所有区域表现最佳（MAE 1.64-5.74 EUR/MWh），Ridge ARX在北部区域具有竞争力。仅基于滞后价格和日历变量的模型即可达到高精度，但外部特征在压力市场状态下对误差分层至关重要。

Conclusion: 模型可解释性和状态感知对于应对市场动态结构性变化的决策者具有实际价值。

Abstract: Norway's electricity market is heavily dominated by hydropower, but the 2021--2022 energy crisis and stronger integration with Continental Europe have fundamentally altered price formation, reducing the reliability of forecasting models calibrated on historical data. Despite the critical need for updated models, a unified benchmark evaluating feature contributions across all structurally diverse Norwegian bidding zones remains lacking. Here we present a comprehensive evaluation of electricity price forecasting across all five Norwegian Nord Pool bidding zones. We constructed a multimodal hourly dataset spanning 2019--2025 and evaluated eight forecasting model families including LightGBM, ARX, and advanced deep learning architectures using a strictly causal test set. We implemented robust rolling-origin backtesting, leave-one-group-out feature ablation, and conditional regime analysis to dissect model performance and feature utility. Our results show that LightGBM achieves the best performance in every zone with MAE ranging from 1.64 to 5.74~EUR/MWh, while the ridge ARX model remains a highly competitive linear benchmark in northern zones. Feature ablation reveals that models relying solely on lagged prices and calendar variables achieve high accuracy and often match or exceed full multimodal integration. However, conditional regime analysis demonstrates that external features like reservoir levels and gas prices remain crucial to stratify forecast errors, which consistently increase under stressed market regimes. This highlights the practical value of model interpretability and regime awareness for decision makers facing structural changes in market dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Exploring the Potential of Probabilistic Transformer for Time Series Modeling: A Report on the ST-PT Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26762)
*Zhangzhi Xiong,Haoyi Wu,You Wu,Shuqi Gu,Kan Ren,Kewei Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-时间概率Transformer（ST-PT），将Transformer重新解释为可编程因子图（基于平均场变分推断与条件随机场的数学等价性），并系统探讨了其在时间序列建模中的三大独特优势：结构可编程性、条件因子调制以及贝叶斯后验更新驱动的潜变量自回归预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Transformer（PT）已在自然语言领域验证了Transformer与平均场变分推断在条件随机场上的数学等价性，但尚未在时间序列领域得到充分探索。本文旨在验证PT在时间序列中的潜力，并修复其在通道轴和逐帧语义上的缺陷。

Method: 首先将PT扩展为空间-时间概率Transformer（ST-PT），增加通道轴并强化逐时间步语义；然后基于因子图模型的三大属性提出三个研究问题：RQ1通过结构图修改注入符号先验；RQ2通过外部条件逐样本编程因子矩阵实现结构化条件生成；RQ3将MFVI迭代转化为潜变量后验更新，并用CRF教师蒸馏潜变量到AR学生以抑制累积误差。

Result: 针对三个研究问题各给出一个实证研究，验证了ST-PT作为可编程时间序列建模框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ST-PT作为可编程因子图框架，能够将符号先验注入、条件生成和贝叶斯后验更新等机制整合到时间序列建模中，超越了传统黑箱Transformer。

Abstract: The Probabilistic Transformer (PT) establishes that the Transformer's self-attention plus its feed-forward block is mathematically equivalent to Mean-Field Variational Inference (MFVI) on a Conditional Random Field (CRF). Under this equivalence the Transformer ceases to be a black-box neural network and becomes a programmable factor graph: graph topology, factor potentials, and the message-passing schedule are all explicit and inspectable primitives that can be engineered. PT was originally developed for natural language and in this report we investigate its potential for time series. We first lift PT into the Spatial-Temporal Probabilistic Transformer (ST-PT) to repair PT's missing channel axis and weak per-step semantics, and adopt ST-PT as a shared cornerstone backbone. We then identify three distinct properties that PT/ST-PT offers as a factor-graph model and derive three Research Questions, one per property, that probe how each property can be exploited in time series: RQ1. The graph topology and potentials are direct programmable primitives. Can this be used to inject symbolic time-series priors into ST-PT through structural graph modifications, especially under data scarcity and noise? RQ2. The CRF's factor matrices are the operator's potentials. Can an external condition program these factor matrices on a per-sample basis, so that conditional generation becomes structural rather than feature-level modulation of a fixed one? RQ3. Each MFVI iteration is a Bayesian posterior update on the factor graph. Can this turn the latent transition of latent-space AutoRegressive (AR) forecasting from an opaque MLP into a principled posterior update, and can a CRF teacher distill its latents into the AR student to counter cumulative error? We give one empirical study per question. Together, these three studies position ST-PT as a programmable framework for time-series modeling.

</details>


### [112] [Accelerating RL Post-Training Rollouts via System-Integrated Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26779)
*Hayate Iso,Tiyasa Mitra,Sudipta Mondal,Rasoul Shafipour,Venmugil Elango,Terry Kong,Yuki Huang,Seonjin Na,Izzy Putterman,Benjamin Chislett,Maor Ashkenazi,Joseph Guman,Gerald Shen,Tugrul Konuk,Ashwath Aithal,Ritika Borkar,Ran Zilberstein,Bita Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将投机性解码（Speculative Decoding）作为强化学习（RL）后训练中无损加速自回归 rollout 生成的方法。在 NeMo-RL 中通过 vLLM 后端实现，支持同步和异步流水线，在 8B 规模推理后训练中取得 1.8x rollout 吞吐提升，通过模拟器预测在 235B 规模下结合异步 RL 可实现 2.5x 端到端训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型的 RL 后训练中，自回归 rollout 生成日益成为性能瓶颈，现有的效率方法（如 off-policy、replay、低精度生成）通过改变 rollout 或优化范式来提升吞吐量，但可能改变训练分布。因此需要一种无损加速方法。

Method: 将投机性解码作为无损加速原语集成到 RL rollouts 中，在 NeMo-RL 框架中利用 vLLM 后端实现，支持同步和异步流水线，并兼容多种投机机制（预训练 MTP 头、小型外部草稿模型、Eagle3 等）。

Result: 在 8B 规模推理后训练同步 RL 场景下，rollout 吞吐量提升 1.8x；通过高保真性能模拟器预测，在 235B 规模下结合异步 RL 可实现高达 2.5x 的端到端训练加速。

Conclusion: 投机性解码可作为 RL 训练中部署先进解码加速技术的有效路径，在保持输出分布不变的前提下显著提升 rollout 效率，且规模越大收益越明显。

Abstract: RL post-training of frontier language models is increasingly bottlenecked by autoregressive rollout generation, making rollout acceleration a central systems challenge. Many existing efficiency methods improve throughput by changing the rollout or optimization regime, for example, through off-policy execution, replay, or lower-precision generation. We study speculative decoding as a lossless acceleration primitive for RL rollouts that preserves the target model's output distribution. We implement speculative decoding in NeMo-RL with a vLLM backend, supporting both synchronous and asynchronous pipelines and enabling speculation during RL rollouts. This benefit is realizable across speculation mechanisms, such as pretrained MTP heads, small external draft models or even techniques such as Eagle3, which are traditionally applied after RL phase. This yields a deployment path for state-of-the-art speculative decoding inside RL training. In a reasoning post-training workload at 8B scale under synchronous RL, speculative decoding improves rollout throughput by 1.8x. Using a high-fidelity performance simulator, we project that combining speculative decoding with asynchronous RL yields up to 2.5x end-to-end training speedup at 235B scale.

</details>


### [113] [Hankel and Toeplitz Rank-1 Decomposition of Arbitrary Matrices with Applications to Signal Direction-of-Arrival Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26787)
*Georgios I. Orfanidis,Dimitris A. Pados,George Sklivanitis,Elizabeth Serena Bentley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵的最优秩-1 Hankel和Toeplitz结构逼近问题（L₂和L₁范数），并将其应用于少样本波达方向（DoA）估计。提出了高效的结构化矩阵分解算法，推导了对应的高斯和拉普拉斯噪声下的最大似然最优估计器，并通过仿真和实测数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程系统中，如现代自动驾驶等应用中的少样本波达方向（DoA）估计问题，需要高效且准确的结构化矩阵逼近方法来处理小样本支持下的信号估计。

Method: 针对L₂和L₁范数误差，分别开发了计算高效且精确的秩-1 Hankel和Toeplitz结构化矩阵分解算法；在此基础上推导了适用于小样本支持场景的解析DoA估计器。

Result: L₂和L₁范数下的估计器被证明分别在白高斯噪声和拉普拉斯噪声下达到最大似然最优；通过大量仿真和真实数据实验验证了少样本DoA推断的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化逼近方法在计算效率和估计精度上具有优势，尤其适合实际传感系统中少样本场景下的DoA估计任务。

Abstract: We consider the problems of computing the optimal rank-$1$ Hankel and Toeplitz-structured approximation of arbitrary matrices under $L_2$ and $L_1$-norm error. Such problems arise naturally in engineered systems, including the basic few-shot signal Direction-of-Arrival (DoA) estimation problem that is of importance to modern autonomous systems applications. We develop accurate and computationally efficient structured matrix decomposition algorithms for both formulations and then derive analytically grounded small-sample-support DoA estimators for practical sensing system deployments. The resulting estimators under the $L_2$ and $L_1$ norms are formally shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal under white Gaussian and Laplace noise, respectively. The estimators are further validated through extensive simulation studies and real-world data experiments in few-shot DoA inference.

</details>


### [114] [Super-resolution Multi-signal Direction-of-Arrival Estimation by Hankel-structured Sensing and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26793)
*Georgios I. Orfanidis,Dimitris A. Pados,George Sklivanitis,Elizabeth Serena Bentley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现代自主系统中硬件受限的大阵列空间采样问题，提出了一种基于Hankel结构感知和任意秩数据矩阵分解的快速超分辨率多信号波达方向(DoA)估计框架，包含L2和L1范数两种形式，分别在高斯白噪声和i.i.d.各向同性拉普拉斯噪声下达到最大似然最优，仿真表明该方法在更低信噪比下具有更强超分辨能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自主系统中的传感模态涉及硬件受限的大阵列空间采样，且相干时间有限，需要快速超分辨率的DoA估计方法。

Method: 提出基于Hankel结构感知和任意秩数据矩阵分解的DoA估计框架，分别采用L2和L1范数进行优化。

Result: L2范数估计器在高斯白噪声下达到最大似然最优；L1范数估计器在i.i.d.各向同性拉普拉斯噪声下达到最大似然最优；仿真表明所需SNR更低、分辨率概率显著高于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法具有强大的超分辨能力，且对脉冲干扰和测量损坏具有鲁棒性，适用于实际自主系统。

Abstract: Motivated by sensing modalities in modern autonomous systems that involve hardware-constrained spatial sampling over large arrays with limited coherence time, we develop a novel framework for rapid super-resolution multi-signal direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation based on Hankel-structured sensing and data matrix decomposition of arbitrary rank, under both the $L_2$ and $L_1$-norm formulation. The resulting $L_2$-norm estimator is shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal in white Gaussian noise. The $L_1$-norm estimator is shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal in independent, identically distributed (i.i.d.) isotropic Laplace noise, offering broad robustness to impulsive interference and corrupted measurements commonly encountered in practice. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed methods exhibit powerful super-resolution capabilities, requiring significantly lower SNR and achieving substantially higher resolution probability than recent competing approaches.

</details>


### [115] [Asynchronous Federated Unlearning with Invariance Calibration for Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26809)
*Zhaoyuan Cai,Xinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异步联邦遗忘框架AFU-IC，通过解耦擦除过程与全局训练流程，并引入服务器端不变性校准机制，在医疗影像场景下实现了高效、合规且可靠的联邦遗忘，显著降低了时间延迟的同时保持了与重新训练相当的遗忘效果和模型保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法大多依赖同步协调，导致系统因设备异构性而产生显著延迟，且被擦除数据的影响仅被暂时抑制，会在后续训练中重新浮现，无法实现真正移除。

Method: 提出AFU-IC框架，通过解耦擦除过程与全局训练流程实现异步遗忘，目标客户端可在不中断全局训练的情况下执行遗忘；同时引入服务器端不变性校准机制，防止模型重新学习已被擦除的数据。

Result: 在三个医学基准数据集上的实验表明，AFU-IC的遗忘效果和模型保真度与黄金标准的重新训练相当，同时相比同步基线方法显著降低了挂钟时间延迟。

Conclusion: AFU-IC为跨孤岛医疗环境中的联邦学习提供了高效、合规且可靠的遗忘方案，解决了同步遗忘的延迟问题和遗忘不彻底问题。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is an emerging paradigm in Federated Learning (FL) that enables participating clients to fully remove their contributions from a trained global model, driven by data protection regulations that mandate the right to be forgotten. However, existing FU methods mostly rely on synchronous coordination. This requirement forces the entire federation to halt and wait for stragglers to complete erasure, creating significant delays due to device heterogeneity. Furthermore, these methods often face the problem that the influence of erased data is merely suppressed temporarily and resurfaces during subsequent training, rather than being genuinely removed. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes Asynchronous Federated Unlearning with Invariance Calibration (AFU-IC), a novel framework for medical imaging that decouples the erasure process from the global training workflow. This enables the target client to perform unlearning asynchronously without interrupting global training. Meanwhile, a server-side invariance calibration mechanism prevents the model from relearning the erased data. Extensive experiments on three medical benchmarks demonstrate that AFU-IC achieves unlearning efficacy and model fidelity comparable to gold-standard retraining while significantly reducing wall-clock latency compared to synchronous baselines. AFU-IC ensures efficient, compliant and reliable FL in cross-silo medical environments.

</details>


### [116] [Semi-supervised learning with max-margin graph cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26818)
*Branislav Kveton,Michal Valko,Ali Rahimi,Ling Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的半监督学习算法，通过学习最大化与调和函数解所诱导标签之间间隔的图割，并在理论和实验上证明了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督最大间隔学习方法（如流形正则化支持向量机）存在局限性，本文旨在提出一种更优的算法来最大化标签间隔，从而提升半监督学习性能。

Method: 提出一种学习图割的算法，该图割能最大化与调和函数解所诱导标签之间的间隔；推导了泛化误差上界。

Result: 在合成数据集和三个UCI数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多数情况下优于流形正则化支持向量机这一最先进的半监督最大间隔学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在半监督学习中通过最大化标签间隔实现了更好的性能，并具有理论保证。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel algorithm for semisupervised learning. This algorithm learns graph cuts that maximize the margin with respect to the labels induced by the harmonic function solution. We motivate the approach, compare it to existing work, and prove a bound on its generalization error. The quality of our solutions is evaluated on a synthetic problem and three UCI ML repository datasets. In most cases, we outperform manifold regularization of support vector machines, which is a state-of-the-art approach to semi-supervised max-margin learning.

</details>


### [117] [Unifying Sparse Attention with Hierarchical Memory for Scalable Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26837)
*Zihan Zhao,Baotong Lu,Shengjie Lin,Yizou Chen,Jing Liu,Yanqi Zhang,Ziming Miao,Ming-Chang Yang,Haiying Shen,Qi Chen,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN是一个面向稀疏注意力的推理框架，通过统一分区抽象、 locality感知的KV缓存管理和两级分层元数据布局，在分层KV存储上实现端到端加速，相比vLLM吞吐量提升1.66-5.66倍，TTFT降低7-9倍，TPOT最多降低58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM服务受限于KV缓存的全量注意力计算开销。动态稀疏注意力虽能通过仅访问小部分KV状态来缓解，但算法层面的稀疏收益难以转化为端到端系统收益：稀疏方法在不同粒度下操作，依赖临时的逐算法实现；同时分层KV存储引入GPU-CPU间检索细粒度不规则KV子集的系统瓶颈，容易抵消稀疏带来的好处。

Method: (1) 统一分区抽象：将不同稀疏粒度映射到共享的基于页面的KV基板上；(2) locality感知的KV缓存管理器：动态调整每请求的HBM预算，采用GPU友好的分桶LRU策略减少PCIe往返；(3) 两级分层元数据布局：按活跃工作集而非最坏情况地址空间进行大小规划。在vLLM上集成三种代表性稀疏注意力算法实现。

Result: 相比vLLM，端到端吞吐量提升1.66-5.66倍，TTFT降低7-9倍；相比原始稀疏注意力实现，TPOT最多降低58%。

Conclusion: SPIN通过系统-算法协同设计，有效将稀疏注意力的算法收益转化为实际的端到端系统性能提升，解决了分层KV存储中检索不规则KV子集的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Long-context LLM serving is bottlenecked by the cost of attending over ever-growing KV caches. Dynamic sparse attention promises relief by accessing only a small, query-dependent subset of the KV state per decoding step and extending the KV storage to CPU memory. In practice, however, these algorithmic savings rarely translate into end-to-end system-level gains because sparse methods typically operate at different granularities and thus rely on ad hoc, per-algorithm implementations. At the same time, hierarchical KV storage introduces a new systems bottleneck: retrieving fine-grained, irregular KV subsets across the GPU-CPU boundary can easily erase the benefits of sparsity.
  We present SPIN, a sparse-attention-aware inference framework that co-designs the execution pipeline with hierarchical KV storage through three techniques: (1) a unified partition abstraction that maps different sparsity granularities onto a shared page-based KV substrate; (2) a locality-aware KV cache manager that dynamically sizes per-request HBM budgets and uses a GPU-friendly bucketed LRU policy to cut PCIe round-trips; and (3) a two-level hierarchical metadata layout sized to the active working set rather than the worst-case address space. Built on vLLM with three representative sparse attention algorithms, SPIN delivers 1.66-5.66x higher end-to-end throughput and 7-9x lower TTFT than vLLM, and reduces TPOT by up to 58% over the original sparse-attention implementations.

</details>


### [118] [Language Diffusion Models are Associative Memories Capable of Retrieving Unseen Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26841)
*Bao Pham,Mohammed J. Zaki,Luca Ambrogioni,Dmitry Krotov,Matteo Negri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于均匀离散扩散模型（UDDMs）本质上表现为具有涌现创造能力的联想记忆（AMs），并通过条件熵作为实用探针，识别出训练数据集大小驱动的记忆化到泛化的尖锐转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言扩散模型何时记忆训练数据，以及如何定量评估其真实的生成机制。

Method: 将UDDMs重新解释为联想记忆模型，通过评估训练和测试样本的token恢复率来识别记忆化到泛化的转变，并利用条件熵作为检测该转变的实用探针。

Result: 发现UDDMs中存在由训练数据集大小驱动的尖锐记忆化到泛化转变：训练集增大时，训练样本周围的吸引域收缩，未见测试样本周围的吸引域扩张，最终两者趋于一致；记忆化阶段条件熵趋近于零，泛化阶段条件熵保持有限。

Conclusion: 条件熵可作为部署模型中记忆化到泛化转变的实用检测工具。

Abstract: When do language diffusion models memorize their training data, and how to quantitatively assess their true generative regime? We address these questions by showing that Uniform-based Discrete Diffusion Models (UDDMs) fundamentally behave as Associative Memories (AMs) $\textit{with emergent creative capabilities}$. The core idea of an AM is to reliably recover stored data points as $\textit{memories}$ by establishing distinct basins of attraction around them. Historically, models like Hopfield networks use an explicit energy function to guarantee these stable attractors. We broaden this perspective by leveraging the observation that energy is not strictly necessary, as basins of attraction can also be formed via conditional likelihood maximization. By evaluating token recovery of $\textit{training}$ and $\textit{test}$ examples, we identify in UDDMs a sharp memorization-to-generalization transition governed by the size of the training dataset: as it increases, basins around training examples shrink and basins around unseen test examples expand, until both later converge to the same level. Crucially, we can detect this transition using only the conditional entropy of predicted token sequences: memorization is characterized by vanishing conditional entropy, while in the generalization regime the conditional entropy of most tokens remains finite. Thus, conditional entropy offers a practical probe for the memorization-to-generalization transition in deployed models.

</details>


### [119] [KAYRA: A Microservice Architecture for AI-Assisted Karyotyping with Cloud and On-Premise Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26869)
*Attila Pintér,Javier Rico,Attila Répai,Jalal Al-Afandi,Adrienn Éva Borsy,András Kozma,Hajnalka Andrikovics,György Cserey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAYRA是一个端到端的染色体核型分析系统，采用容器化微服务架构，集成了EfficientNet-B5+U-Net语义分割器、Mask R-CNN实例检测器和ResNet-18分类器，通过级联ROI缩小策略进行编排。该系统可作为云服务或本地部署，在临床试验中展现出色性能，达到TRL 6成熟度，并整合了人类专家审核工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床细胞遗传学实验室需要在操作约束下进行染色体核型分析，现有商业系统性能参差不齐，且存在数据隐私（患者数据不可出域）与灵活部署之间的矛盾。作者旨在构建一个既能在云端又能本地部署、且满足临床诊断流程要求的多模型细胞遗传学AI系统。

Method: 采用容器化微服务架构，ML栈组合三个模型：EfficientNet-B5+U-Net用于语义分割，Mask R-CNN（ResNet-50+FPN）用于实例检测，ResNet-18用于分类。通过级联ROI缩小策略依次聚焦下游模型于染色体区域。相同容器镜像可部署为云服务或本地安装。集成人类专家审核工作流。

Result: 在459条染色体（来自10个中期分裂相）的临床试验中，分割准确率98.91%（对比78.21%/40.52%），分类准确率89.1%（对比86.9%/54.5%），旋转准确率89.76%（对比94.55%/78.43%）。在分割和分类上显著优于旧密度阈值参考系统（p<0.0001），分割也优于现代AI参考系统（p<0.0001）；分类与现代AI参考差异不显著（p=0.34）。系统达到TRL 6成熟度。

Conclusion: 多模型细胞遗传学AI服务可以打包为微服务架构，支持灵活的云或本地部署，同时在临床试验中展现出强大的实证性能。KAYRA证明了该架构在临床细胞遗传学实验室中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: We present KAYRA, an end-to-end karyotyping system that operates inside the operational constraints of a clinical cytogenetic laboratory. KAYRA is architected as a containerized microservice pipeline whose ML stack combines an EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net semantic segmenter, a Mask R-CNN (ResNet-50 + FPN) instance detector, and a ResNet-18 classifier, orchestrated through a cascaded ROI-narrowing strategy that focuses each downstream model on the chromosome-bearing region. The same container images are deployed both as a cloud service and as an on-premise installation, supporting clinical environments where patient-data egress is not permitted as well as those where it is. A pilot clinical evaluation against two commercial reference karyotyping systems on 459 chromosomes from 10 metaphase spreads shows segmentation accuracy of 98.91 % (vs. 78.21 % / 40.52 %), classification accuracy of 89.1 % (vs. 86.9 % / 54.5 %), and rotation accuracy of 89.76 % (vs. 94.55 % / 78.43 %). KAYRA improves over the older density-thresholding reference on all three axes (p < 0.0001 for segmentation and classification by Fisher's exact test on chromosome-level counts), and on segmentation also against the modern AI- supported reference (p < 0.0001); on classification the difference vs. the modern AI reference is not statistically significant at the present test-set size (p = 0.34). The system reaches TRL 6 maturity and integrates the human-in-the-loop expert-review workflow that diagnostic cytogenetic practice requires. The thesis of this paper is that a multi-model cytogenetic AI service can be packaged as a microservice architecture supporting flexible deployment - cloud-hosted or on-premise - while delivering strong empirical performance on a pilot clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [120] [On the Learning Curves of Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26922)
*Steve Hanneke,Alkis Kalavasis,Shay Moran,Grigoris Velegkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了收益最大化问题的学习曲线，在单物品单买家场景下给出了学习速率衰减的近乎完整刻画：无约束分布下存在贝叶斯一致算法但收敛可任意慢；若最优收益由有限价格实现则最优速率为约1/√n；离散分布下可实现近乎指数级衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于收益最大化学习算法的工作采用分布无关视角（类似PAC学习框架），评估最坏情况下的误差上界，无法捕捉学习曲线的具体形状。本文旨在刻画收益最大化问题中学习曲线的衰减速率，填补这一空白。

Method: 理论分析，在单物品单买家基本设定下，分别考虑三种情形：无约束任意估值分布、最优收益由有限价格实现、以及离散支撑集分布，推导并证明学习曲线的最优衰减速率下界与上界。

Result: (1) 任意无约束分布下存在贝叶斯一致算法（收敛到0），但收敛速度可任意慢；(2) 若最优收益由有限价格实现，最优衰减速率为Θ(1/√n)；(3) 离散分布下可实现近乎指数级衰减，该速率在PAC框架下无法达到。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统刻画了收益最大化学习曲线的衰减速率，揭示了不同分布假设下的根本性差异，并指出在分布可知场景下可比PAC框架获得更优的学习速率。

Abstract: Learning curves are a fundamental primitive in supervised learning, describing how an algorithm's performance improves with more data and providing a quantitative measure of its generalization ability. Formally, a learning curve plots the decay of an algorithm's error for a fixed underlying distribution as a function of the number of training samples. Prior work on revenue-maximizing learning algorithms, starting with the seminal work of Cole and Roughgarden [STOC, 2014], adopts a distribution-free perspective, which parallels the PAC learning framework in learning theory. This approach evaluates performance against the hardest possible sequence of valuation distributions, one for each sample size, effectively defining the upper envelope of learning curves over all possible distributions, thus leading to error bounds that do not capture the shape of the learning curves.
  In this work we initiate the study of learning curves for revenue maximization and provide a near-complete characterization of their rate of decay in the basic setting of a single item and a single buyer. In the absence of any restriction on the valuation distribution, we show that there exists a Bayes-consistent algorithm, meaning that its learning curve converges to zero for any arbitrary valuation distribution as the number of samples $n \to \infty$. However, this convergence must be arbitrarily slow, even if the optimal revenue is finite. In contrast, if the optimal revenue is achieved by a finite price, then the optimal rate of decay is roughly $1/\sqrt{n}$. Finally, for distributions supported on discrete sets of values, we show that learning curves decay almost exponentially fast, a rate unattainable under the PAC framework.

</details>


### [121] [A Note on How to Remove the $\ln\ln T$ Term from the Squint Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26926)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将Orabona和Pál [2016]中提出的移位KT势等价于改变Krichevsky-Trofimov算法中的先验，并利用相同思想消除了Squint算法数据无关界中的ln ln T因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移除参数自由学习与专家界中的ln ln T因子，简化理论界限。

Method: 将移位KT势重新解释为改变KT算法中的先验分布，并将此思想推广到Squint算法中。

Result: 成功移除了Squint算法数据无关界中的ln ln T因子。

Conclusion: 移位KT势的本质是改变先验，该技术可推广至其他算法以消除多余的ln ln T因子。

Abstract: In Orabona and Pál [2016], we introduced the shifted KT potentials, to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the parameter-free learning with expert bound. In this short technical note, I show that this is equivalent to changing the prior in the Krichevsky--Trofimov algorithm. Then, I show how to use the same idea to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the data-independent bound for the Squint algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Finite Functional Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26161)
*Michael Arntzenius,Max Willsey*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文通过将谓词视为带有支持集（输出非零的输入集合）的函数，统一了函数式编程和逻辑编程。Datalog 可被视为有限支撑布尔函数的语言。将布尔函数推广到其他带点集，可以自然地处理聚合和加权逻辑编程。这种将有限支撑函数（表示为数据）与高阶函数（表示为代码）结合的方式被称为有限函数式编程。论文还给出了一个简单的类型系统来检查有限支撑性质，使用分级效应检查变量绑定，使用相关类型建模带点集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一函数式编程和逻辑编程，处理聚合和加权逻辑编程中的有限支撑问题。

Method: 将谓词视为带有支持集的函数，将Datalog视为有限支撑布尔函数的语言，通过有限函数式编程结合高阶函数，并设计类型系统（分级效应+相关类型）来检查有限支撑。

Result: 实现了函数式与逻辑编程的统一，能够处理聚合和加权逻辑编程，并给出了类型系统来保证有限支撑性质。

Conclusion: 有限函数式编程有效统一了函数式与逻辑编程范式，类型系统能可靠地检查有限支撑性质。

Abstract: We unify functional and logic programming by treating predicatesas functions equipped with their support: the set of inputs whose output is nonzero. Datalog, for instance, is a language of finitely supported boolean functions. Finite support allows representing functions as input-output tables. Generalizing from boolean functions to other pointed sets neatly handles aggregation and weighted logic programming. We refer to the combination of finitely supported functions, represented as data, with higher order functions, represented as code, as finite functional programming. We give a simple type system to check finite support, using graded effects to check variable grounding and relevance types to model pointed sets.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [On the Role of Time Series Clustering in Traffic Matrix Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26081)
*Martha Cash,Charlotte Fowler,Alexander M. Wyglinski*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于时间序列聚类的流量矩阵预测框架，通过将异构流量流分组后分别训练预测器，相比全局预测模型能有效提升预测性能，且聚类表示方式的选择对最终预测精度影响有限，主要增益来源于任务分解本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流量矩阵中的流量流常呈现异质性行为，全局预测模型将所有流量流联合预测的效果不佳，需要一种能处理异质性的预测方法。

Method: 提出聚类预测框架：先对流量流进行聚类分组（探索了直方图、自相关函数、功率谱密度和朴素分区四种表示方式），再为每组分别训练独立的预测器。

Result: 在Abilene和GÉANT数据集上，聚类方法一致优于全局预测基线，且计算成本远低于局部预测；中等聚类数K即可获得大部分性能提升；不同聚类表示虽产生不同分区，但RMSE相近。

Conclusion: 聚类的主要优势在于将TM预测任务分解为更小的子问题，而具体的聚类结构对整体预测精度的影响相对有限。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the role of time-series clustering in traffic matrix (TM) prediction. Traffic flows within a TM often exhibit heterogeneous behavior, which can reduce the effectiveness of global forecasting models that predict all flows jointly. To address this, we propose a clustering-based prediction framework that groups flows into smaller subsets and trains separate predictors for each group. Four traffic-flow representations for clustering are explored, namely, histogram, autocorrelation function (ACF), power spectral density (PSD), and naïve partitioning, and how the representation choice and the number of clusters affect prediction performance. Experiments using the publicly available Abilene and GÉANT datasets show that clustering consistently improves over global forecasting baselines, while remaining substantially less costly than local prediction. The results further show that most of the performance gain is achieved at moderate values of K, with diminishing returns as the number of clusters increases. Although different clustering representations produce different partitions of the traffic flows, they often achieve similar root mean squared error (RMSE). This suggests that the main benefit of clustering lies in decomposing the TM prediction task into smaller subproblems, while the exact cluster structure plays a more limited role in determining overall prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [StreamGuard: Exploring a 5G Architecture for Efficient, Quality of Experience-Aware Video Conferencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26223)
*Xuyang Cao,Oliver Michel,Kyle Jamieson*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: StreamGuard是一种面向5G视频会议的子流级QoE感知优先级架构，通过RAN监控、控制器决策和标记模块形成闭环控制，在真实5G测试平台上相比传统方法在保持相似QoE时提升2倍背景吞吐量，或在可比吞吐量下提升高达70%的QoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G视频会议在有限无线资源下QoE严重下降，原因在于5G需同时服务大量用户而交互式流量需要精细调度，现有方案缺乏对子流级别的QoE感知优先级处理。

Method: 提出StreamGuard架构，包含三个闭环组件：(1) RAN监控器通过深度包检测推断QoE和RAN状态；(2) 控制器选择优先级动作以平衡QoE和公平性；(3) 标记模块通过标记数据包将子流引导至适当优先级队列。此外采用选择性子流丢弃和探测式速率控制等机制对齐应用行为与无线约束。

Result: 在真实5G测试平台上，StreamGuard实现了优于传统5G和现有最优方法的QoE-公平性权衡：在可比背景吞吐量下QoE提升高达70%，或在相似QoE下保持高达2倍背景吞吐量。

Conclusion: StreamGuard通过子流级别的细粒度QoE感知优先级调度，有效解决了5G视频会议中资源受限下的QoE退化问题，实现了更好的QoE与公平性平衡。

Abstract: Video conferencing over 5G is increasingly prevalent, yet its Quality of Experience (QoE) often degrades under limited radio resources. This has two causes: 5G networks must serve many users, while interactive traffic requires careful handling. Motivated by the insight that different subflows within an interactive session have a disproportionate effect on QoE, we present the design and implementation of StreamGuard, a practical 5G architecture for subflow-level, QoE-aware prioritization. StreamGuard forms a closed control loop with three components: (1) a monitor in the Radio Access Network (RAN) that uses deep packet inspection to infer QoE and RAN state, (2) a controller that selects prioritization actions to balance QoE and fairness, and (3) a marking module that applies these decisions by marking packets to steer subflows into appropriate priority queues. StreamGuard further shapes application behaviors via mechanisms including selective subflow dropping and probe-based rate control, to align application behavior with radio constraints. Implemented in a real 5G testbed, StreamGuard achieves a superior QoE-fairness tradeoff compared to vanilla 5G and prior state-of-the-art approaches, improving QoE by up to 70% at comparable background throughput or preserving up to 2x higher background throughput at similar QoE.

</details>


### [125] [A 3GPP Perspective on Spectrum Sharing for the 5G-to-6G Migration: From DSS to MRSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26853)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文回顾了LTE-NR动态频谱共享（DSS）的经验教训，探讨如何将这些教训应用到5G-6G共存的多无线接入技术频谱共享（MRSS）设计中，指出核心挑战已从基本共存可行性转向共存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G开始成形，7 GHz以下频谱稀缺使5G-6G频谱共享成为迁移工具，需要从LTE-NR DSS的经验中学习以指导MRSS设计。

Method: 回顾LTE-NR DSS实际部署中暴露的问题（开销、控制信道瓶颈、邻区干扰等），分析其对MRSS设计的启示。

Result: 发现主要挑战不再是基本共存可行性，而是共存效率，这决定了MRSS能否成为5G到6G频谱迁移的广泛可用框架。

Conclusion: MRSS设计应吸取LTE-NR DSS的经验教训，聚焦于提升共存效率，以实现5G-6G的有效频谱共享。

Abstract: Dynamic spectrum sharing (DSS) played an important role in the 4G-to-5G transition by allowing 5G new radio (NR) to enter valuable legacy spectrum without immediate static refarming. Yet practical deployments also exposed the cost of coexistence of NR with long-term evolution (LTE), including overheads, control-channel bottlenecks, neighbor-cell interference, etc. As 6G begins to take shape, spectrum scarcity below 7 GHz is again making 5G-6G spectrum sharing a migration tool of interest. Multi radio access technology spectrum sharing (MRSS) is being considered by the 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) as a key mechanism for 5G-6G coexistence. This article reviews the lessons learned from LTE-NR DSS and examines how those lessons should shape MRSS design. The main challenge is no longer basic coexistence feasibility, but coexistence efficiency which determines whether MRSS will become a broadly usable framework for 5G-to-6G spectrum migration.

</details>
